    `<input>` stayed parked over an invisible field: a ~610×22 transparent element above the canvas,
    eating every click inside it — including on the room picker's thumbnails. And since it is the
    canvas that releases focus, the state could not repair itself while the user kept clicking in the
    band. Belt and braces: `mousedown`/`mouseup`/`mousemove` are now bound to the overlay too and
    routed through the display list unless the point is over the field it actually serves, so the
    overlay can never swallow a click even if mispositioned. `#vortex-login-input` is addressable
    from the console for the next time something like this is suspected.
  - **`Password field is not contained in a form`** — the shared `<input>` now lives in a
    `display: contents` `<form>` whose `submit` is cancelled (a single-field form submits implicitly
    on Enter, which would reload the page; Enter already has a job — `SsoTokenView` reads it).
    `autocomplete` is per-field (`current-password`/`username`) instead of a blanket `off`.
  - **`Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'length')` in `_onInputKeyDown`.** `event.key`
    is not guaranteed: nothing in this repo dispatches a synthetic `keydown` (checked), so it comes
    from a password manager or similar extension, which became interested in the field the moment it
    became a real password field in a form. `TextFieldController.resolveCharCode()` already guards
    this in the engine; `Stage` now does too. The throw also swallowed Enter for that keystroke.
  - **`EnvironmentView`'s restored login button wore the wrong skin.** The 701 dump declares
    `_loginButton` and never builds it (see the divergence note in the file); the port built it as a
    bare `Button`, which is the STYLE_ILLUMINA skin no login screen uses. Now a green `ColouredButton`
    like every other confirm button here — matching the ticket button beside it on purpose, since the
    only other hitch skins are Cancel-pink and HC-yellow.
  - **`RoomPicker`: one dead render blocked the whole step.** AS3 listens for `"complete"` and
    nothing else, and `initView()` is only ever called from `_loadedCount == _numOfRooms`, so a
    single 404/CORS failure left the step permanently empty with no way out of the flow. Failures now
    count (deliberate divergence, logged); that room renders blank and is not clickable. Also
    `_selectedIcon.mouseEnabled = false` — `_infoPanel` is added after the holders, so the marker sat
    on the corner of the selected thumbnail and ate the press meant for it.
  - **`OnBoardingHcFlow.showErrorMessage()` stacked balloons.** AS3 calls `removeChild()` on the flow
    while adding to `_mainSprite` two lines later — in Flash that is `ArgumentError #2025` on the
    second error; here `removeChild` no-ops, so they piled up. Removed from the parent it was added to.
  - Still not runtime-verified: `AvatarView`, `WebCaptchaView`, `HitchNameChangeDialog`,
    `RandomAvatarCloudsAnimation`. The room-picking round trip is wired correctly end to end —
    composer 3267 / event 3624 agree across the WIN63 registry, `Headers.cs` and our maps, and
    `vortex-emulator` has the full handler/composer/serializer chain.

- ✅ **Login + onboarding rebuilt from the 701 dump**, 2026-07-31. The login flow had drifted into a
  redesign: two invented screens (`SCREEN_REGISTER`, `SCREEN_AVATAR_CREATE`) whose AS3 traces pointed
  at files in no dump, an invented `isSsoTokenEnabled()` opening on Login where AS3 opens on the
  ticket screen, a split-screen SCSS layout, English strings hard-coded into the views, and
  `loadImages()` fetching one of the two landing illustrations. Replaced with the source.
  - **`onBoardingHcUi` ported** (15 widgets) onto a small canvas-2D display list under
    `onBoardingHcUi/display/` — the port's UI is canvas 2D (`WindowRenderer`), and these widgets are
    bitmap blitting, so PixiJS was the wrong target. `Scale9Shape` reproduces Flash's live
    `scale9Grid` (assigning `width` re-slices instead of scaling), which every button skin depends on.
  - **`login/` rewritten** — `LoginFlow` (4 screens, `init()` opens on `SCREEN_SSO_TOKEN`),
    `EnvironmentView`, `LoginView`, `AvatarView`, `SsoTokenView`, `Background`, `ImageLoader`,
    plus `WebCaptchaView`, which was previously a stub returning null.
  - **`ILoginViewer` restored to its 17 members** and `WebApiLoginProvider` rewritten against the
    web-API session instead of speaking `fetch()` itself; `register`/`createAvatar`/`checkName`
    (invented) are gone. `ICaptchaView`/`ICaptchaHandler` added.
  - **`loadDefaultEmbedLocalizations()` implemented** — it was a stub returning false, so every
    login caption rendered as its raw `${key}`. The twelve `default_localizations*` embeds now ship
    in `assets/configurations/`.
  - **Onboarding ported** (`onBoardingHc/`, `onBoardingHcSteps/`): `OnBoardingHcFlow`,
    `AvatarEditor`, `HitchNameChangeDialog` + `NameChangeDialog`, `RoomPicker`,
    `RandomAvatarCloudsAnimation`, `Background`. Triggered off `suggestedLoginActions`, as
    `HabboLandingView.onAvatarRendererReady()` does.
  - **Three messages added**: `UpdateFigureDataMessageComposer` (3339, gender-then-figure on the
    wire), `SelectInitialRoomMessageComposer` (3267) and `SelectInitialRoomMessageEvent` (3624).
    IDs from WIN63's registry, names corroborated by the emulator.
  - Verified headlessly: the ticket screen and the hotel picker render with localised text and the
    AS3 layout. The onboarding path needs a live server (it runs off `suggestedLoginActions`) and is
    **not** runtime-verified yet.

- ✅ **Dormant-TODO sweep: five clusters closed, five stale notes retracted**, 2026-07-31
  (branch `feat/dormant-todo-waves`). Chosen by scanning all 522 `TODO`s in `src/` for work that
  was blocked on something already present, rather than by module.
  - **Nine notification message events woken up.** `NotificationMessageHandler` gated moderator
    messages/cautions, bans, respects, HC gift claims, safety-lock (both directions) and
    restore-client behind "TODO: uncomment when X is implemented". Eight of the nine event classes
    already existed — under `incoming/notifications/`, not the `moderation/`/`users/`/`catalog/`
    paths the dead imports named, which is why they read as missing; the ninth is
    `UserObjectMessageEvent`. Not a straight uncomment: the commented bodies had drifted from
    `_SafeCls_1951.as` (missing `useNotifications()`/`useNotificationFeed()` gates, an empty
    `onRespectNotification`).
  - **Eight composers ported** (`SetObjectData` 246, `SetRandomState` 1942, `UseWallItem` 3590,
    `PlacePostIt` 1122, `PlaceBot` 2102, `RemoveBotFromFlat` 2743, `GetDailyTasks` 4100,
    `SetChatPreferences` 1149), all resolved from `_SafeCls_2046.as` by tracing send sites in
    `_SafeCls_1821.as`. Three of the TODOs naming them were wrong — `PlaceBot` was documented as
    1295, which belongs to the user-move composer; `SetChatPreferences` was documented as a
    boolean in the `isDisabledInPreferences` setter, which sends nothing in AS3. `UseWallItem` is
    the one header the emulator disagrees with (it listens on 1540 and calls its own entry
    "UNRESOLVED"); registry wins, note is on the composer. Wired up random-state furni, wall-item
    use, ad-furni stuff-data save, bot place/pickup, and the daily-tasks request at login.
  - **`BadgeImageManager` ported** — `SessionDataManager`'s five badge accessors all returned
    `null`, so no badge rendered anywhere in the client. Also fixed the four `*AssetName`
    accessors, which were not stubs but inventions (`<badge>_b`, `group_badge_<badge>`) naming
    assets nothing ever registered. Caches through a `Map` rather than the AssetLibrary, because
    `BitmapDataAsset.content` is a PixiJS `Texture` while the whole badge API is typed
    `HTMLImageElement`; documented on the class.
  - **`restrict` now filters typing.** `TextController` parsed, stored and cloned the mask without
    ever applying it, and `ITextFieldWindow` did not expose it. Flash's semantics ported and
    validated against ten cases; masks live on the wired number/text/textarea inputs and the paged
    table's page box.
  - **The ten flagged `HabboMessages` headers audited.** Two describe live gaps; five were stale or
    misread (see the commit). The one that matters: 996 `Users` is missing AS3's `badgesRank` read,
    and it stays missing on purpose — `vortex-emulator`'s `RoomAvatarSerializer` stops at
    `IsModerator` too, so reading it client-side alone would desync every later user in the packet.
    Needs both repos changed together.
  - Two smaller fixes found while reading around the above: `changeRoomObjectState()` was missing
    the `!activeRoomHasFreeFurniMovementsMode` half of its play-test guard, and
    `UsersMessageParser`'s `userType==3` branch set `webID = -roomIndex` instead of the id it read.
  - Retracted rather than implemented: four `ChatWidgetHandler` localisation TODOs. The behaviour
    is already ported in `ChatBubbleFactory.applySpecialChatContent()`, and that handler is dead
    once `freeFlowChat` resolves — the primary tree has no `ui/handler/ChatWidgetHandler.as` at all.

- ✅ **`HabboActivityPointNotificationMessageEvent` (2046) ported and wired to its three AS3 subscribers**, 2026-07-30.
  - The single-currency counterpart of `ActivityPointsMessageEvent` (509, whole wallet) was missing
    from the client entirely, while `vortex-emulator` already sends it from `PlayerWalletModule`
    on every `CurrencyType.ActivityPoints` movement — so ducket/diamond balances only refreshed on
    a full-wallet push. Event + parser (`amount`, `change`, `type`; three ints) added and registered.
  - Header **2046** from `_SafeCls_2046.as` (`_SafeStr_4546[2046] = _SafeCls_2011`), corroborated by
    the emulator's `Revision20260701/Headers.cs`. The **1016** carried by `win63_version` and Nitro
    is a 2016-build id and is not used.
  - Subscribed in all three places AS3 does: `HabboCatalog.onActivityPointNotification()` (writes the
    one currency into the purse *without* clearing the others, `updatePurse()`, `PurseEvent` +
    `PurseUpdateEvent`), `NotificationMessageHandler.onActivityPointNotification()` (loyalty-only
    bubble, `change > 0`, `if_icon_diamond` — the port drops AS3's `_png` suffix) and
    `QuestMessageHandler.onActivityPointsNotification()` → `QuestController.onActivityPoints()`.
  - Two gaps left visible as `TODO(AS3)`: the catalog's `HBST_pixels` sound (this port's
    `HabboCatalog` has no sound-manager dependency at all) and the quest module's own
    `ActivityPointsMessageEvent` registration, which AS3 has and only `HabboCatalog` carries here.

- ✅ **Pets: the `avatarinfo` UI slice closed — context menus, use-product and the whole breeding
  chain**, 2026-08-03. Completes the scope the 2026-07-30 entry below left open.
  - **Eleven new classes in `ui/widget/avatarinfo/`** (`avatarinfo/` goes 4/28 → 15/28):
    `PetInfoData`, `PetMenuView`, `OwnPetMenuView`, `BreedPetView`, `UseProductView`,
    `UseProductConfirmationView`, `BreedMonsterPlantsConfirmationView`, `ConfirmPetBreedingView`,
    `BreedPetsResultView`/`Data` and `NestBreedingSuccessView`, plus `widget/events/UseProductItem`.
    Every layout they need was already shipping (`pet_menu`, `own_pet_menu`, `breed_pet_menu`,
    `use_product_menu`, the seven `use_product_controller_*`, the four frames,
    `confirm_pet_breeding_xml`, `breed_pets_confirmation_xml`, `breed_pets_result_xml`,
    `nestBreedingSuccess_xml`, `pet_breeding_pet_preview_xml`) — nothing new had to be built.
  - **`AvatarInfoWidgetHandler` went from 142 to ~490 lines**: it had *zero* pet code against AS3's
    636. Added the three session subscriptions (`RSPFUE_PET_STATUS_UPDATE`, `RSPLUE_PET_LEVEL_UPDATE`,
    `RSPFUE_NEST_BREEDING_SUCCESS`), `findPetWithWebId()`, `getFurniData()`,
    `activateUseProductMenuForPets()`/`activateBreedMenuForPets()` (the two room scans that decide
    which pets a product or a breeding offer applies to), `requestBreedMenu()` and the five action
    branches (`RWUAM_REQUEST_PET_UPDATE`, `RWUPM_PET_PRODUCT`, `RWUAM_HARVEST_PET`,
    `RWUAM_COMPOST_PLANT` with its confirm dialog, `RWUAM_REQUEST_BREED_PET`).
  - **The three breeding composers now have a sender.** `BreedPets` (1922), `ConfirmPetBreeding`
    (2872) and `CancelPetBreeding` (3367) were constructed nowhere; `AvatarInfoWidget` now owns
    `breedPets()`/`acceptBreedPets()`/`cancelBreedPets()`/`confirmPetBreeding()`/`cancelPetBreeding()`
    and the four `RWPPBE_*`/`RWPBRE_*` desktop events that drive them have listeners.
  - **One real bug found while wiring, in code that was already there.** `_handlePetInfo` is cleared
    by `RWUAM_REQUEST_PET_UPDATE` so the infostand's own refresh does not re-open the bubble — but
    nothing set it back, because AS3 does that in the `RWROUE_OBJECT_SELECTED` branch this port had
    never subscribed to. Without it the pet bubble would stop opening for the rest of the session
    after the first refresh.
  - **Notifications closed**: `PetImageUtility` ported and hung off `HabboNotifications`
    (`get petImageUtility()`, `get windowManager()`), and `onPetLevelNotification()`/`onPetReceived()`/
    `onPetRespectFailed()` — three parse-then-return stubs — now render their feed item / alert.
  - **Two smaller gaps**: `InfoStandWidget.onPetFigureUpdate()` was an empty body and now
    re-rasterises through the handler's `getPetImage()` (made public: this port's event carries the
    figure string where AS3's carries a rendered BitmapData);
    `AnimatedPetVisualizationData.defineVisualizations()` is overridden, so `disableheadturn` is read
    instead of `isAllowedToTurnHead` being hardcoded true.
  - **Supporting members added where the port lacked them**: `IRoomEngine.getActiveRoomIsPlayingGame()`,
    a nullable `listener` on `getPetImage()` (the implementation always allowed it; only the type did
    not), and `IHabboInventory.getFloorItemById()`/`requestSelectedFurniToMover()` — both AS3
    `IHabboInventory` members, needed by the breeding-result dialog's place/pick pair.
  - **Server mismatch resolved**, so the `PetExperienceEventParser` warning is gone: Revision20260701
    now writes `PetId, PetRoomIndex, GainedExperience` and `MovePetMessageEvent` is 432.
  - **The use-product trigger closed too, in the same pass.** Both AS3 entry points now exist:
    `RoomEngine.showUseProductSelection()` (the inventory's `use_btn`, whose `FurniModel` stub
    already named the missing engine method) and the `ROWRE_PET_PRODUCT_MENU` case of
    `handleObjectWidgetRequestEvent()` (double-clicking a product already in the room —
    `FurniturePetProductLogic` was ported and raising the request, but the engine's switch fell
    through to its `log.warn` default). `RoomUI` forwards both `ROSM_USE_PRODUCT_*` to
    `roomObjectEventHandler` as AS3 does at RoomUI.as:299-304, which is the only hop between the
    engine and the bubbles.
  - **Scope left open, marked TODO(AS3), not omitted.** `IHabboUserDefinedRoomEvents` is still null
    everywhere, so the wired click gate reads as "no wired" and `WIRED_USER_CLICK_HANDLED` is not
    subscribed. The `avatarinfo` avatar-name bubbles, decorate mode, the bot menus and the highlight
    timer remain unported.

- ✅ **Pets: protocol re-derived from WIN63's own registry, session + infostand chain closed**, 2026-07-30.
  - **The derivation is gone.** `PetVocalMessageEvent` (3073) and `IssuePetCommandMessageComposer`
    (3072) were Vortex-custom inventions with no counterpart in any AS3 tree, and 3073 is
    `WhisperMessageEvent` — registering the pet event there silently overwrote whisper in the
    `_events` Map. Both classes and their parser are deleted. There is no "issue pet command"
    packet in the source: AS3 issues a command as ordinary chat, `"<pet name> <localised command>"`,
    through `InfoStandWidgetHandler`'s `RWPCM_PET_COMMAND` branch.
  - **Every pet header re-read from `_SafeCls_2046.as`.** The pet message package is
    package-obfuscated in the primary dump, so each class was identified by the handler that
    subscribes to it (`RoomUsersHandler.as:85-95`, `habbo/inventory/_SafeCls_1951.as:159-195`,
    `RoomChatHandler.as:41`, `habbo/room/_SafeCls_1984.as:284`) and then looked up in the registry.
    All eight previously-registered room-pet ids were already right and are now annotated with their
    obfuscated class; the block used to claim they came from the emulator.
  - **One real header bug found.** `MovePetMessageComposer` was registered at **2761**, which is
    `win63_version`'s id — a different build. This revision's registry has no 2761 at all; the real
    header is **432** (`_SafeCls_2560`, sent by `_SafeCls_1821::sendMoveUserObjectMessage()` on the
    `monsterplant` branch). Every monster-plant move was going nowhere. `vortex-emulator`'s
    `Revision20260701/Headers.cs` carries the same wrong 2761 and still needs fixing there.
  - **One real parser bug found.** `PetInventoryMessageParser` never read `rarityLevel`, the last
    field of each pet record, so in a multi-pet fragment the next pet's id was read out of the
    previous pet's rarity field. `Pet.rarityLevel` was a hard-coded `-1` behind a TODO; it is now
    parsed and stored.
  - **Eight incoming messages added** with parsers: `PetBreeding` 939, `ConfirmBreedingRequest`
    1477, `ConfirmBreedingResult` 2068, `NestBreedingSuccess` 40, `GoToBreedingNestFailure` 2441,
    `PetRemovedFromInventory` 3013, `PetAddedToInventory` 3653, `PetSupplementedNotification` 3858 —
    plus the two breeding DTOs (`BreedingPetInfo`, `PetBreedingRarityCategory`).
    **Four composers added**: `BreedPets` 1922, `ConfirmPetBreeding` 2872, `CancelPetBreeding` 3367,
    `PassCarryItemToPet` 1429.
  - **Handlers closed.** `RoomUsersHandler` had only `onPetInfo`; the other ten AS3 pet handlers now
    exist, so commands, placing errors, figure/status/level updates and the whole breeding chain
    have a subscriber. `RoomChatHandler.onPetSupplementedNotification()` and the inventory's
    `onPetAdded`/`onPetRemoved`/`onGoToBreedingNestFailure` are ported too.
  - **Infostand chain closed.** The nine missing `RoomWidget*` pet events and their three data
    classes, `RoomWidgetPetCommandMessage`, seven `InfoStandWidgetHandler` session subscriptions,
    the pet-care composer branches (treat/water/light/carry-item, previously a `log.warn` TODO), and
    **`PetCommandTool`** itself — which removes the four stubs `InfoStandPetView` was carrying and
    makes the `train` button actually open the training window.
  - **Scope left open, marked TODO(AS3), not omitted.** The `avatarinfo` pet views are still absent:
    `OwnPetMenuView`, `PetMenuView`, `PetInfoData`, `BreedPetView`, `ConfirmPetBreedingView`,
    `BreedPetsResultView`/`Data`, `NestBreedingSuccessView`, `BreedMonsterPlantsConfirmationView`,
    `UseProductView`/`UseProductConfirmationView`. The breeding messages therefore reach
    `desktopEvents` with nothing listening yet — the wire and session layers are complete beneath
    them. `InfoStandWidget.onPetFigureUpdate()` also still needs the figure re-rasterised.

- ✅ **Mystery box + mystery trophy wired end to end**, 2026-07-29.
  - **What landed.** The three missing incoming messages (`ShowMysteryBoxWait` 691,
    `CancelMysteryBoxWait` 3840, `GotMysteryBoxPrize` 353) with their parsers, the two composers
    (`MysteryBoxWaitingCanceled` 1063, `OpenMysteryTrophy` 2242), and the whole UI cluster that had
    no port at all: `FurnitureContextInfoView`, `FurnitureContextMenuWidget` +
    `FurnitureContextMenuWidgetHandler`, `MysteryBoxContextMenuView`, `MysteryBoxOpenDialogView`,
    `MysteryBoxToolbarExtension` and `MysteryTrophyOpenDialogView`. `RWE_FURNITURE_CONTEXT_MENU` was
    already being requested by `RoomUI` — the factory returned null for it, so nothing existed
    behind the request.
  - **Two bugs found in code that was already there, both silent.** (1) `FurnitureLogic` gated its
    context-menu event types and its mouse-click dispatch on the private `_contextMenu` **field**
    where AS3 reads the `contextMenu` **getter**. Every logic that supplies its menu by overriding
    the getter — the mystery box, the mystery trophy, the guild furni, the monsterplant seed — never
    reached `contextMenuType`, so the field stayed null and the menu could not open for any of them.
    (2) `RoomUI` forwarded the `RETWE_REQUEST_*` widget requests to the desktop but not
    `RETWE_OPEN/CLOSE_FURNI_CONTEXT_MENU`, which is how a handler's `processEvent()` is reached at
    all. Either one alone would have left the new widget dead.
  - **Also ported while in `FurnitureLogic`:** the `RoomObjectSelectedMessage` branch of
    `processUpdateMessage()`, which opens/closes the menu on select/deselect. It is inert today —
    nothing in this port constructs that message yet — and says so at the site.
  - **Scope left open, marked TODO(AS3), not omitted.** The widget's other AS3 sub-views (guild,
    random teleport, monsterplant seed + confirmation, friend furni, generic usable, purchasable
    clothing, effect box) are stubs, as are the handler's guild-furni-info and figure-set-ids
    message events. The room engine does not raise their requests either, so nothing fires into a
    gap.
  - **Server side already existed.** `vortex-emulator`'s `RoomGrain.MysteryBox` +
    `PlayerMysteryBoxGrain` route `UseFurniture` (3353) into the wait/prize flow, and the live
    gamedata carries `mysterybox.tracker.active`, `mysterybox.faq.url` and all 49
    `mysterybox.*` texts — so the tracker and both dialogs have their data.

- ✅ **`habbo/friendlist` ported in full** (3 → 41 TS files against 40 AS3), 2026-07-29.
  - **What landed.** The domain (`Friend`, `FriendCategory`, `FriendCategories`, `FriendRequest(s)`,
    `FriendListTab(s)`, `AvatarSearchResults`, `FriendOnlineImageListener` and the four `*Deps`
    pairs), all eleven views (`FriendListView`, `FriendListTabsView`, `FriendsView`,
    `FriendRequestsView`, `SearchView`, `AlertView` + `FriendRemoveView`/`RoomInviteView`,
    `RelationshipStatusSelector`, `OpenedToWebPopup`, `Util`), `FriendListLookAndFeel`,
    `RelationshipStatusEnum`, `FriendListTabEnum`, both event classes, and `HabboFriendList`
    rebuilt on top of them. Also `habbo/utils/UserInfoRegionUtil` (AS3 `_SafeCls_3877`, used by
    three of the views) and `IHabboNotifications.addItemWithBitmap()`, which the friend-online
    toast needs and which the notifications component was missing.
  - **The old manager was a documented re-architecture, and it is gone.** `HabboFriendList.ts` kept
    friends in one flat `Map<number, FriendData>` with no categories, no requests domain and no
    search state — its own `canBeAskedForAFriend()` carried a `TODO(AS3)` saying the two missing
    conditions could not be checked "against a flat-Map re-architecture". Both conditions are now
    checked, because the state they read exists. The port-specific `friendListEvents` bus is kept
    and still fed: several ported systems listen on it.
  - **Two wiring gaps, neither introduced here, both since closed.** (1) `HabboFriendList` was
    **never constructed** — `VortexMain` registered `HabboGroupsManager`, `HabboNotifications` and
    the rest, but nothing instantiated the friend list, so no version of this code had ever run.
    `initFriendList()` does it now. (2) Its bitmaps (`open_minimail`, `room_invite`, `hdr_friends`,
    `arrow_*`, …) were not in `App.ts`'s asset-library allowlist, the same trap recorded for the
    group wizard — a missing entry renders blank rather than throwing. The names were taken off
    `HabboFriendListCom.as`'s `*_png` fields rather than off a grep of call sites, because AS3
    resolves some of them by suffix and a grep misses those.
  - **Category names go in as `${...}` tokens, not resolved text.** AS3 resolves eagerly at
    construction because its localization tables are loaded before the socket opens. Here the texts
    arrive over HTTP and `MessengerInit` can win the race — and `getLocalization()` returns `''`
    for a key it has not loaded yet, so the name was frozen to the empty string for the session.
    Resolving at draw time is what `CoreLocalizationManager` already does for every caption.
  - **Names.** Recovered where a tree has them (`IFriend`, `RelationshipStatusEnum`,
    `IHabboFriendsList` → `IHabboFriendList`, all from PRODUCTION); derived and **stated as derived
    at the declaration** where no tree does (`IFriendsView`, `IFriendRequestsView`,
    `FriendRequestsView`, `FriendListTabEnum`, `FriendListLookAndFeel`, `UserInfoRegionUtil`, the
    four `I*Deps`, and `HEART`/`..._MINIMIZED`, both read off their own value tables).
  - **One dead field found on the way.** `Friend` reads `lastAccess` off the wire DTO, but the DTO
    (`src/unknowns/_SafePkg_1764/_SafeCls_1774.as`) declares that field and **never assigns it** —
    its constructor reads 14 values and that is not one of them. It is always null in the real
    client; `FriendData.ts` is right not to have it.

- ✅ **The friend bar, the messenger, and the silent DI lock that hid all of it**, 2026-07-29.
  - **What landed.** `friendbar/data/` 8/8 (`HabboFriendBarData` + `FriendEntity`,
    `FriendNotification`, `FriendRequest` and their interfaces), `friendbar/view/` 22/22
    (`HabboFriendBarView`, the 9 tabs, the 6 tokens, the 4 utils), `friendbar/events/` 7, and
    `habbo/messenger/` `HabboMessenger` + `DummyFriend`. `HabboFriendBar`'s two
    `attachComponent()` calls — commented out because neither component existed — are live and in
    AS3's order (data, view, landing view), and its `set visible()` stub delegates through
    `queueInterface()` the way AS3 does.
  - **The bar never appeared, and nothing in the bar was why.** `HabboFriendBarData` declares
    `IID_HabboMessenger` a **required** dependency, and no component in this port was ever attached
    against that IID. A required dependency on an unprovided IID leaves the component locked for the
    whole session: `initComponent()` never runs, its message handlers are never registered, its
    events never fire. **Nothing throws and nothing logs.** The view waits on the data component in
    turn, so `buildUserInterface()` was never reached. Writing `HabboMessenger` fixed it; softening
    the dependency would have "fixed" it too, at the cost of null-guards through the whole domain
    layer. Before declaring a dependency required, confirm something calls
    `attachComponent(..., [IID_X])` — and read the file, because a one-line grep gives false
    negatives on multi-line attach calls (it gave two here).
  - **Still open: the chat window.** `_mainView` is null until `MainView` (1237 l.) and
    `habbicons/` (1399 l.) are ported, so clicking a friend opens nothing. Every delegation through
    it is guarded, which is AS3's own shape rather than a concession — the source guards all of
    them, because the view is built lazily on first open. `IID_HabboSoundManager` and
    `IID_HabboHelp` are declared optional for the reason above, with the reason on the line.
  - **Two AS3 quirks kept verbatim**, each with its reason on the line: the animated path in
    `FriendsView.select()` is guarded by an always-false expression and can never run, and
    `releaseFriendTabWindow()` removes three icon listeners with one handler.
  - **Names.** `AddFriendsTab` (`_SafeCls_3052`) and `OpenMessengerTab` (`_SafeCls_3739`) are
    obfuscated in every tree; both names are **derived**, from their layouts and their actions, and
    said so at the declaration, as are the constants whose names had to come off their values.

- ✅ **Two asset lookups that could never hit, both silent** (`AvatarImageWidget`,
  `SimpleAlertDialog`), 2026-07-29. `getAssetByName()` returns null without complaint, so the
  bitmap renders blank and the layout does not build.
  - **`_png` is not part of the name.** Images register under their bare file basename
    (`App.ts::registerImageAssets`), while AS3 looks them up as the `*Com.as` field name
    (`hdr_friends_png`). Code ported literally therefore asks for a name the library never had.
    Found in three places: `HabboFriendList.getButtonImage()`,
    `AvatarImageWidget.requestPlaceholder()`, and the bundle itself, where a sibling variant had
    been "fixed" by renaming the *file* to `placeholder_avatar_small_head_cropped_png.png` — the
    wrong end of it. Note the extraction tool sometimes does the opposite and names a file after the
    embed rather than the field (`icons_toolbar_me_menu_placeholder` ships as
    `me_menu_placeholder.png`); that one needs the generator fixed, not the call site.
  - **Layout names are per-component and two components declare `simple_alert_xml`.**
    `HabboWindowManagerCom` and `HabboFriendListCom` both do, so both ship qualified and the bare
    key does not exist — `simpleAlert()` was throwing for **every** caller in the client, not just
    the friend list's. Join declarations to files on the embed's whole linkage name; collapsing to
    the short name merges distinct assets.

- ✅ **A dead emulator left the client sitting there fully rendered**, 2026-07-29.
  Two independent causes. `setConnectionActions()` had **no caller**, so `_connectionActions` stayed
  null and every state change — the server going away included — was logged and dropped;
  `App.ts::installConnectionActions()` provides them now. And `connectionClosed()` took no
  arguments, so every close looked alike and there was no way to react to one without reacting to
  all: it carries the `CloseEvent`'s `code`/`wasClean` now, and `HabboCommunicationManager` acts
  only on **1000/1001**, the two a peer sends deliberately. That distinction is the whole point — a
  backgrounded tab drops the socket with 1006, and Chrome halts the tab's JS entirely after ~5
  minutes hidden, so treating a transport drop as a disconnect logs the player out for switching
  tabs. AS3 hands this to `loginFlow.showDisconnected()`, but this port disposes its `LoginFlow`
  once boot is done and there is no live instance to show; a reload lands on the same login screen
  with no half-torn-down engine behind it.

- ✅ **Input fields: the Flash `border` was never drawn, and the caret bridge was aligned to the wrong box** (`core/window/graphics/renderer/TextSkinRenderer.ts`, `core/window/components/TextController.ts`, `TextFieldController.ts`).
  - **Where the border went missing.** AS3 never draws one either — `TextSkinRenderer.draw()` is `param2.draw(_loc8_, matrix, ...)`, a blit of the *live* `flash.text.TextField`, and Flash paints that field's background **and** border into the bitmap before it is copied. `TextController` only mirrors the two layout properties onto `_field` (`setBorder`/`setBorderColor`). This port has no `_field`; it strokes glyphs itself, so the border simply did not exist and every `<var key="border">` field rendered as a bare fill — room settings' name/description/tags, the group wizard's, the messenger's search box, ~20 layouts in total. `drawBorder()` now runs **before** the empty-text early return, because AS3 blits the field whatever it holds: an empty bordered field still has its border. `borderColor` also had no public accessor (AS3 declares one, l.657), so `border_color` — `0x959595` in room settings — was parsed into a field nothing could read.
  - **The caret sat where the text was not**, for three independent reasons, all in the hidden-`<input>` bridge that stands in for Flash's own caret. (1) The element was laid over the *whole field* with the field's height, and a single-line `<input>` centres its editor vertically no matter what `line-height` says — a 9px line in a 26px field landed ~7px low, and in the 80px description box, halfway down it. (2) `font-family` was assigned the raw `fontFace`, which is invalid CSS for any quoted family (`Volter (Goldfish)` — parentheses), so the browser dropped the declaration and advanced the caret in **Arial** while the canvas drew Volter. (3) The element's kind was decided in the constructor, before `multiline`/`word_wrap` arrive from the layout's `<variables>` — so a wrapped field got a single-line `<input>`, its caret running off horizontally while the canvas wrapped the text over four lines. The element now covers the text box only (one line tall unless the field wraps), takes its font from the same builder the measuring path uses, and is rebuilt as a `<textarea>` when the field turns out to be multi-line. `maxChars` was applied at construction for the same reason and had the same fate; it is applied with the geometry now.
  - **`word_wrap` on an editable field also had no effect on the canvas side**: the renderer's wrap test excluded `TEXTFIELD`, though Flash lays text out from `multiline`/`wordWrap` alone, with no regard for whether the field is editable.
  - **A pre-existing 2px offset, made visible by the border.** Flash reserves a gutter on all four sides of a TextField; the port applied only the top one, so glyphs ran straight into the newly-drawn border line. Fixed for input/password fields (`FLASH_TEXT_FIELD_LEFT_GUTTER`) and left as a `TODO(AS3)` for the rest: an auto-sized label takes its width from the measured text, so shifting glyphs right without widening the box — `measureLayout()` compensates only for the vertical gutter, and only for background-filled fields — would clip the last 2px off captions across the client.
  - **Verified in a browser, not by reading**: the layouts were built standalone through `vortex-glaze`'s window subsystem and the DOM bridge's normally-transparent text was coloured red to expose where the caret actually is. Before: red text several pixels below/right of the black canvas text, description unwrapped, no border pixels anywhere on the field's edges. After: red on black exactly, and the field edge reads `#000000` (group wizard) / `#959595` (room settings, its `border_color`).

- ✅ **Room-preview canvases were never taken off the stage when their widget died** (`habbo/room/preview/RoomPreviewer.ts`, `habbo/window/widgets/RoomPreviewerWidget.ts`, `habbo/catalog/viewer/widgets/ProductViewCatalogWidget.ts`). Both widgets' `dispose()` unregistered the canvas from `PreviewCanvasStack` and dropped the reference — but never detached the PixiJS container, which `RoomEngine.createRoomCanvas()` parents onto the **shared root stage**, not into the window tree. In Flash the canvas is a child of the widget's own window, so disposing the window removes it from the display list and AS3's `RoomPreviewer.dispose()` can ignore it entirely; here nothing owned it. With the per-frame sync callback also unregistered, nothing hid it either: the last previewed piece of furniture stayed rendered on the stage forever, frozen at its last screen position, showing through the transparent hole the composite punches for the room view. The same file already did the right thing in `showPreview()` (`parent?.removeChild()`) when swapping the live canvas for a static bitmap — only the teardown path missed it. `RoomPreviewer.releaseRoomCanvas()` (TS-only, needs `IRoomEngine.disposeRenderingCanvas()`, also TS-only) hands the canvas back for the widget that owns its previewer; the catalog's widget only detaches its own container, because the previewer belongs to `HabboCatalog` and is shared — a widget disposed *after* its replacement was built (page switches rebuild the product view) would otherwise dispose the fresh canvas out from under it, both keying on the same preview room id.

- ✅ **The trophy furni widget — and the room-object widget-request bridge no furni widget could work without** (`habbo/ui/widget/furniture/trophy/`, `handler/FurnitureTrophyWidgetHandler.ts`, `RoomEngine.handleObjectWidgetRequestEvent()`).
  - **The blocking gap was one missing translation step, not the widgets.** The chain is `double-click → FurnitureTrophyLogic.useObject() → ROWRE_TROPHY → RoomEngine → RETWE_REQUEST_TROPHY → RoomDesktop → RoomWidgetFurniToWidgetMessage → FurnitureTrophyWidgetHandler → RoomWidgetTrophyDataUpdateEvent → TrophyFurniWidget → TrophyView`. Both event vocabularies already existed in full — `RoomObjectWidgetRequestEvent` with 35 `ROWRE_*` constants, `RoomEngineToWidgetEvent` with 30 `RETWE_*` — and the furniture logics already emitted the first. **Nothing anywhere translated one into the other**, so every `ROWRE_*` a furni raised died in `RoomEngine.onRoomObjectEvent()`. Mechanism 4 from the 2026-07-17 audit ("data plumbed end to end, never consumed at the last step"), and it gated *all 24* furniture widget clusters, not just this one. `handleObjectWidgetRequestEvent()` is a faithful port of `_SafeCls_1821.as` (AS3's `RoomObjectEventHandler`, folded into `RoomEngine` here because that is already the class implementing `IRoomRenderingCanvasMouseListener`): 14 cases mapped, the rest carrying an explicit `TODO(AS3)` that lists them — left unmapped rather than emitted, so no event fires that nothing can service.
  - **A fidelity bug in `FurnitureTrophyLogic` that would have defeated the port anyway**: it emitted `ROWRE_OPEN_WIDGET`, the *generic* "this furni has a widget" request, where AS3 emits `ROWRE_TROPHY` — which is what the trophy handler claims, so the engraving could never have arrived. It was also missing `getEventTypes()` and the `mouseEvent()` override that makes a trophy open on double-click, and carried an invented `widgetType = 'trophy'` field with no AS3 counterpart (now gone).
  - **The cluster keeps AS3's interface/view split rather than collapsing it** (rule 2): `ITrophyFurniWidget`/`ITrophyView`, `TrophyTheme`, `TrophyFurniWidget`, and the two views — including AS3's own asymmetry, where `NikoTrophyView` takes the concrete `TrophyFurniWidget` instead of the interface because it also reads `localizations` and `configuration`, neither of which `ITrophyFurniWidget` declares.
  - **Two AS3 oddities preserved rather than "fixed"**: `TrophyFurniWidget.get color()` returns the constant white tint and ignores the event's `color`, which carries the *theme* instead (1-based, hence `normalize(color - 1)`); and `updateInterface()` switches on `viewType - 10`, so only 10 (Niko silver) and 20 (Niko gold) reach `NikoTrophyView`.
  - **Which widget went first was decided by which layouts actually ship**, not by size: `trophy.xml`, `niko_trophy.xml` and `trophy_bg_{gold,silver,bronze}.png` are all present in the built assets. `present/` (999 lines) and `roomlink/` ship no layout at all and would have rendered nothing — the silent-failure pattern this project keeps rediscovering.
  - Furni widgets are created **eagerly at room entry** (`RoomUI.as:941`): creating one is what registers its handler's message types, so the widget has to exist before the furni is ever clicked.
  - **Not verified live.** `pnpm build` and `pnpm lint` are clean, but nothing has yet opened a trophy in a running room — that needs the emulator and an engraved trophy placed.

- ✅ **`sendRaw()` stopped reporting success for sends that went nowhere** (`core/communication/connection/SocketConnection.ts`, commit `1e0ca5f`). Switch tabs, come back, and the client looked alive while doing nothing — every click silently dropped, only a reconnect helped. `sendRaw()` queued into `_pendingMessages` and returned `true` whenever `_connected` was false; that branch cannot distinguish "not connected yet" from "connection lost", and `onclose` leaves `_socket` non-null while clearing `_connected`, so a dropped link sent every subsequent action into a queue only `onopen` drains — and there is no reconnect. AS3 has no queue on that path at all (`SocketConnection.as::send()` l.222-229: `if(this._socket.connected){ writeBytes; flush; } else { return false; }`); it queues in exactly one other case (`authenticated && !configurationReady`), which this port already models separately as `_pendingComposers`. **The byte-level queue on `!_connected` was a port invention.** Queuing is now limited to `readyState === CONNECTING` — the async-connect window Flash did not have — and anything else returns `false` with a one-shot warning. `onclose` also records close code/reason/`wasClean`, evidence that previously lived only for the handler's lifetime: 1006 says the transport dropped rather than the peer closing, which is what a backgrounded tab or a sleeping machine looks like.

- ✅ **Six message events had no reachable header, and header collisions were silent** (`HabboMessages.ts`, commit `5622901`). `RoomCompetitionController`, `AchievementsResolutionController` and the navigator each registered handlers that could never fire — `MessageRegistry` dropped them with a bare "Unknown message event class" at startup and the replies simply never arrived. Five had no header at all. The sixth, `GuestRoomSearchResultMessageEvent`, is the instructive one: it sat on **1265**, which is the Wired furni-trigger push, and since `_events` is a `Map`, the later `set(1265, WiredFurniTriggerEvent)` further down the file silently replaced it. `MessageRegistry`'s own duplicate check never fires because the `Map` has already deduped by the time it sees the config — **ID collisions are now loud**. IDs were recovered from WIN63's own registry (`_SafeCls_2046.as`) by identifying each obfuscated event class through the parser it constructs, since member names are not obfuscated: 3143, 1844, 1166, 2617, 3222, 160. All six independently match what `vortex-emulator` already sends — a second source agreeing on the same numbers, in the order CLAUDE.md prescribes (registry first, emulator as corroboration).

- ✅ **Boot order now follows AS3: nothing loads before login** (commit `49dae39`). `HabboAir.as::createLoginFlowOrLoadingScreen()` returns *before* creating the loading screen when there is no SSO ticket, which makes the very next call — `startCoreInitializationIfPossible()` — bail on `if(_loadingScreen == null)`. So AS3 starts no core, no `SessionDataManager`, and no furnidata download until the user is authenticated. This port booted the whole engine first and showed the login flow afterwards, because `LoginFlow` was handed the engine's configuration manager. `LoginFlow` now builds its own from the embedded configuration assets alone, which is what AS3's `createConfiguration()` does (`login/LoginFlow.as:157`, out of `HabboConfigurationCom`) — every property the login path reads ships in `common_configuration_txt`, so no `external_variables` download is needed either. The avatar creator, the one screen with no AS3 counterpart and the only one needing a renderer, boots the engine on demand through an `ensureEngine` callback. Two more mismatches fixed in the same area: **the loading screen had two owners** (`VortexMain.onExitFrame()` implemented AS3's real condition, `HabboAir.as:466`, while `App` also disposed it unconditionally after `connect()`, so its lifetime depended on which fired first — the engine owns it alone now), and **window skins/layouts are handed to `HabboWindowManager` at construction** via `IVortexConfig.windowAssets`, the way AS3 passes its `AssetLibrary` as the third constructor argument (`HabboWindowManagerComponent.as:142`), instead of being pushed in after `Vortex.bootstrap()` returned — which left the manager alive but empty while the socket was already open.

- ✅ **Logging rebuilt: namespaced levels, bound console sinks, and a level audit over all ~905 call sites** (`core/utils/Logger.ts`, 186 files retagged). Infrastructure, not a port — AS3's `debug()`/`warning()` (`_SafeCls_56.as` l.71-97) store the last message and dispatch a component event; they were never console output, so none of this codebase's console logging is AS3-traceable and the levels were the port's own choice.
  - **Names are now dotted module paths** (`habbo.room.RoomEngine`, `core.communication.connection.SocketConnection`), resolved by walking the path outwards. The old flat namespace could not express "this subsystem at DEBUG", and it collided: `Moderation` was three different files, `Navigator` two, `ChatBubbleFactory` two across packages. Enforced by `no-restricted-syntax` in `eslint.config.mjs`; 38 hand-written `[ClassName]` message prefixes that existed *because* the real prefix was useless are gone.
  - **`log.debug` is a property holding a pre-bound `console` method, not a wrapper.** A disabled level is a shared no-op, and — the bigger win — the console attributes each line to the **caller** instead of to `Logger.ts`. Verified in Edge: boot lines report `HabboLocalizationManager.ts:75`, `LoginFlow.ts:653`. This is why timestamps and `Logger.onRecord()` are opt-in: both need a wrapper frame and give the attribution back.
  - **New `TRACE` floor**, off at every default, for per-frame/per-packet/per-item detail. 42 "…received" packet-arrival traces moved down to it. `__packets.on()` raises its own namespace to TRACE explicitly, or it would silently do nothing.
  - **Level audit**: 51 `info` → `debug` (component lifecycle — `'Navigator initialized'` is not a milestone), 27 `debug`/`info` → `warn` (`'Unknown pricing model'`, `'not ported yet'`, `'Could not find element'` — the silent-failure class this project keeps rediscovering; at `debug` they are invisible in production, which is exactly where they matter). Dev default moved DEBUG → INFO. Pre-login boot console: **5 lines**, of which 2 are Vite's. Post-login was not measured — it needs a running emulator.
  - **Runtime control, no rebuild**: `__log.set('habbo.room', 'trace')`, `__log.only(ns)`, `__log.list()`, `__log.spec()`; same spec as `?log=warn,habbo.room:trace,-core.window` and the `vortex:log` localStorage key. `log.once(key)` dedupes a warning raised from a loop. Full reference: `docs/STYLEGUIDE.md` → Logging.
  - **One latent bug fixed while testing**: `applySpec(spec, persist=false)` mutated the config without bumping the invalidation generation, so a non-persisted spec only took effect if some later call happened to invalidate first. `persist` now decides durability only, never whether a change applies.

- ✅ **Text rendering moved from `ctx.fillText()` to a baked glyph atlas — and Flash's four text-quality fields finally do something** (`core/window/utils/GlyphAtlas.ts`, `TextSkinRenderer`, `LabelRenderer`, `TextController`, `TextLabelController`).
  - **The gap was never structural.** Text already drew into each window's own cached `OffscreenCanvas` (`WindowRendererItem.render()`), so there was no canvas-per-text problem to solve. What was wrong is that `antiAliasType`, `sharpness`, `thickness` and `gridFitType` were parsed, stored and then **ignored** — `ctx.fillText()` has no equivalent knobs — while `text_styles_css` sets `anti-alias-type: normal` on every Volter 9px style, i.e. on essentially the whole UI. Flash's `"normal"` is the pre-Flash-8 rasteriser: near-binary coverage on a pixel font. Skia gives greyscale AA instead. That difference *is* the "it doesn't look like Flash" complaint.
  - **Why a threshold alone does not work, and what does.** Thresholding a greyscale-AA'd 9px render destroys sub-pixel-wide stems — the middle of an `M` sits around 40% coverage and vanishes. Averaging supersamples has the same failure for the same reason. The atlas instead renders at 5× and reads back **only the sub-pixel at each destination pixel's centre**: the literal "does the outline cover the pixel centre" test an aliased rasteriser performs. The supersampling adds no blur because samples are never averaged; it only shrinks the browser's AA to 1/5 px before the read. Verified side-by-side against the previous rendering before wiring anything in.
  - **AS3 mapping**: `"normal"` → centre-sample + threshold; `gridFitType = "pixel"` → integer glyph origins and advances. Defaults are AS3's (`advanced` + `pixel`, sharpness/thickness 0 — ctor l.82-90, style resolution l.554-566).
  - **`"advanced"` deliberately stays on `ctx.fillText()`** (`GlyphAtlas.handles()`). `fillText()` composites glyphs with a text-specific gamma correction — dark-on-light coverage is lightened so stems do not read as heavy — that `drawImage()` of a pre-rasterised glyph does not get. Identical coverage therefore comes out visibly **bolder** through the atlas; that is what made the navigator's Ubuntu tabs (`button_shiny_regular`: advanced, sharpness 80, thickness -15) look bold against the AS3 client. The trade only pays where the atlas buys fidelity: `"normal"` is binary, so it has no mid-tones for gamma to act on and cannot suffer this; `"advanced"` greyscale AA is already close to the browser's, so the atlas would have bought speed at the price of a permanently heavier face. Every Volter style is `normal`, so the atlas still covers nearly the whole UI. The `sharpness`/`thickness` curve is kept in `buildAlphaLut()` — reversing the policy is one edit to `handles()` — and its two scale divisors are marked DERIVED at their declaration, since Flash's rasteriser is closed and no AS3 source states how either value scales.
  - **Two real port gaps closed on the way.** `TextController` had no public `antiAliasType`/`gridFitType`/`sharpness`/`thickness` accessors although AS3 declares all four (l.637/687/737/782). `TextLabelController` resolved its text style but **discarded** those same four values, where AS3's `TextFieldCache.getTextField()` (l.51-54) stamps them onto the pooled `TextField` and the controller's getters read them straight back off it (l.50/95/129/174).
  - **Measurement had to move too.** With `gridFitType = "pixel"` the atlas rounds advances, so `TextController.measureLineWidth()` and `TextLabelController.refresh()` now measure *through the atlas*. Leaving them on `ctx.measureText()` would auto-size fields to a width the renderer never draws.
  - **Costs**: one atlas page per (font string, quality) — a few hundred KB for the whole client — plus a colourised copy per fill colour, capped at 24 and rebuilt only when a new glyph is baked. Baking is lazy per glyph. All pages are dropped on `document.fonts` `loadingdone`, since a web font arriving after a bake would otherwise leave the fallback face cached forever — a staleness `fillText()` cannot have.
  - **Kill switch**: `globalThis.__vortexTextAtlas = false` restores the exact previous `fillText()` path, which is kept intact rather than deleted.
  - **Follow-up — the two halves of the text system disagreed on line height.** `TextController.getLineHeight()` used `measureFontLineHeight()` (font metrics: Volter 9px is 11.02px, so a 12px line) while `TextSkinRenderer.compositeTextMultiline()` used `fontSize + 2 + leading` = 11px, an approximation with nothing behind it. The controller therefore sized a multiline box at 12px per line and the renderer stacked lines every 11px, drifting text one pixel per line upward inside its own box — visible on descriptions, long tooltips and any multiline field with a background or border. Now both call `measureFontLineHeight()`; it was the only such hardcoded guess left in either package.
  - **Two dead ends, both measured rather than assumed** — recorded so they are not re-investigated. (1) HiDPI: `Vortex.ts` initialises Pixi at `resolution: 1`, so a display-scaled screen would have the compositor resample the whole canvas; the machine this was tested on reports `AppliedDPI = 96` (100%), so it does not apply here — it would return on a scaled display. (2) Kerning: none of `Volter.ttf`, `Volter Bold.ttf` or `Ubuntu.ttf` carries a `kern`/`GPOS`/`GSUB` table, so the atlas summing per-glyph advances is exactly equal to `measureText()` over the whole string, and no text width can have shifted. A third, `ctx.textRendering = 'geometricPrecision'` at bake time, was tried on the live client and made no visible difference — expected at `SUPERSAMPLE = 5`, where hinting can move a stem by at most 1/5 of a final pixel; see the note at that constant.
  - Comparison harness used to pick the approach: `packages/vortex-client/text-lab.html` (dev-server only, not part of the build).

- ✅ **Navigator modern re-skin — a Vortex-only restyle, zero code changes** (`vortex-client/src/vortex-layouts/navigator_frame_2_xml.xml`). **Not a port**: the dump's navigator layout is restyled, not reimplemented, so the file carries no `AS3:` traces and says so at the top.
  - **Override mechanism, not a fork of the asset.** The file deliberately reuses the dump asset's name. `App.registerVortexLayouts()` (step 3b) runs *after* the dump layouts are registered (step 3) and writes into the same `HabboWindowManager._widgetLayouts` map, so registering `navigator_frame_2_xml` replaces the dump entry that `NavigatorView.LAYOUT_NAME` resolves. It could not live in `src/assets/window-layouts/`: that directory is gitignored and rebuilt by `build-window-assets.mjs`, which would wipe it on the next asset build.
  - **The contract that had to survive** is enumerated in the file header and was checked mechanically (names/types/tags diffed against the dump layout): every `findChildByName` target, the three `block_results` items *in order* (`createMainWindow` clones item 0 three times), the `TEMPLATE`/`SUBTEMPLATE`/`category_header` tags, `navigator_entry_tile` staying a list item of its container, and the geometry anchors code reads back — frame width 578 (`MAX_WINDOW_WIDTH`), `left_pane.x`/`right_pane.x` (their delta is `setLeftPaneVisibility`'s collapse offset), and the row template's height (`rowEntryTemplateHeight` sizes each category card). The tab strip's x cannot be styled from XML at all: `STARTING_TAB_POSITION = 115` overwrites it at runtime.
  - **Six deliberate type changes**, each verified against the *body* of every call that touches the element, not its name: `left_pane`, `create_room_border`, `random_room_border`, `promote_room_border` and `navigator_entry_tile` `<border>`→`<container>` (only `.visible`/`.x` are read, and a skin border paints square corners behind a rounded card); `room_info_usercount_border` `<border>`→`<shape>` so that the `getUserCountColor()` value the factory writes into `.color` renders as a rounded pill. `navigator_entry_row_container` deliberately stays a `<border>` — the alternating row tint from `getModulatedBackgroundColor()` needs a skin-rendered fill.
  - **No new assets.** Modern chrome comes from what already ships: `<shape shape="round_rectangle">` + `radius`/`stroke_*` (`ShapeSkinRenderer`), `<gradient blend BLEND_ADD>`, and the Illumina-light styles already used across the client — dropmenu 100, input border 105, scrollable itemlist 100. This matters: `src/assets/images` is gitignored dump output, so a design depending on new PNGs would not be committable.
  - **Out of scope, and why**: the mockup's "Trending Room" hero, per-room tag labels, "View all" links, tab icons and a bottom action bar have no controller to populate them — they are code, not layout. Create/Random stay in the right pane rather than moving into the sidebar as the mockup shows: the navigator opens with `setLeftPaneVisibility(false)`, so a sidebar Create Room would be hidden by default.
  - Full production build passes and bundles the layout.

- ✅ **Furni editor — a Vortex-only staff tool for rewriting a placed furni's stored server state live** (`habbo/vortex/furnieditor/`, `vortex-client/src/vortex-layouts/`, + emulator). **Not a port**: there is no AS3 or Habbo equivalent, so nothing here carries an `AS3:` trace and every file says so at the top. It edits the real persisted row — transform, altitude, wall offset, extra data, owner and definition — gated server-side on a new `room.furni.edit` capability.
  - **Live refresh came for free.** `RoomMessageHandler` already handles `ObjectUpdate` (pos/rot/state), `ObjectDataUpdate` (stuff data) and `ItemDataUpdate` (wall), so the server re-broadcasts the **standard** composers after an edit and every client in the room updates with no new incoming machinery. Only an outgoing edit request was actually needed; the read/ack packet exists so the window shows the server's truth rather than what was typed.
  - **Wire**: 4 Vortex-specific headers in the **8000–8999 band**, chosen because it is empty in *both* registries (0 occurrences in `HabboMessages.ts` and in the emulator's `Headers.cs`) and clear of the `9xxx` placeholders, which are unresolved *real* messages awaiting a retrace — do not allocate Vortex headers there. 8001 get-data / 8002 data+ack / 8003 apply / 8004 rights. Both sides carry a note; never renumber one alone.
  - **Permission**: `Capabilities.Room.FurniEdit`, deliberately separate from `BuildAny` — that one only lifts the ownership check on the ordinary place/move path, and everything it allows an ordinary player could also do in their own room. This one grants writes with no player-facing equivalent (reassigning ownership, swapping a definition, arbitrary altitude). Both handlers re-resolve it per request; the handshake flag (8004) only decides whether the button is drawn. Every edit publishes `ItemStaffEditedEvent` → `ItemEventType.StaffAction` forensics with before/after JSON per touched field.
  - **Three constraints found by reading the emulator, each worth knowing before extending this**:
    - **A definition swap cannot cross `ProductType`.** `RoomItemsProvider.CreateFromEntity` picks `RoomFloorItem` vs `RoomWallItem` from the definition, so a floor item that became a wall definition would come back as a different kind of object on the next room load. Refused with `definition_product_type_mismatch`.
    - **The dirty-item flush does not persist `definition_id`.** `RoomPersistenceGrain` writes owner/x/y/z/rotation/extra-data/wall-offset and nothing else, and `RoomItem.Definition` is `init`-only — so the swap is a rebuild (detach + re-attach a new object with the same id) plus a **direct** `ExecuteUpdateAsync` on the column. Without that write the swap would show in the room and silently revert on reload.
    - **The swap must not use `ObjectModule.RemoveObjectAsync`.** That enqueues the item with `remove: true`, and nothing clears the flag before the next flush: the row's `room_id` would be nulled moments after the replacement was attached, and the furni would vanish on the next load. It also runs `Logic.OnPickupAsync`, a semantic a swap must not trigger. `SwapFurniDefinitionAsync` is that method minus those two steps, and says why in-code.
  - **Client**: `HabboFurniEditor` (DI Component + `IID_HabboFurniEditor`, per the manager convention) + `FurniEditorView`, built from **authored** layouts in `vortex-client/src/vortex-layouts/` registered by `App.registerVortexLayouts()` via `import.meta.glob` — deliberately *not* `src/assets/window-layouts/`, which is gitignored and regenerated by `build-window-assets.mjs`, so a file there would be wiped on the next asset build. The entry button is added as a list item to the infostand's `infostand_element_list` for the same reason (`furni_view` ships verbatim from the dump). Reached via `Vortex.instance` rather than `IRoomWidgetHandlerContainer`, because adding a non-AS3 member to that ported interface would make the port's traceability lie.
  - **Each input commits on its own mask bit** (no Apply button — that is what makes it real-time), but the payload always carries **every** field: X and Y share the `POSITION` bit, so a commit that filled only the edited one would have teleported the item to row 0 the moment its X was touched. The mask still decides what the server reads; the rest is inert filler. A `_applyingServerState` guard stops the server's echo from bouncing back as fresh edits, since `WE_CHANGE` fires for programmatic writes too.
  - **Second window: the furniture *definition* editor** (`furniture_definitions`, i.e. a furniture *type*), opened from the placed-item editor on that item's current base. Headers 8005/8006/8007, gated on its own `furniture.definition.edit` capability — deliberately not folded into `room.furni.edit`, because one write here changes every placed copy and every future purchase, where the other one touches a single item. The write is `IFurnitureAdminService.UpdateAsync`, the same service the dashboard uses, so the duplicate sprite/type/category guard and the `IFurnitureDefinitionProvider` reload after every write are inherited rather than reimplemented — that reload is what makes it live, since every handler reads the provider's snapshot.
    - **What "live" covers, and what it does not.** The 19 columns are read by the *server*: interaction (`Logic`), footprint, stack height, walk/sit/lay, state count, usage and trade policy — those take effect immediately, hotel-wide, with no restart. The client's *rendering* does not change: `SessionDataManager` loads furnidata from `furnidata.load.url` (an assets host), not from this table, and the emulator does not generate it. Editing `Width` changes where the server allows the item, not the sprite the client draws. Generating furnidata from the DB was considered and deliberately left out of scope.
    - Explicit Save rather than per-field commit, unlike the placed-item window: the write path rewrites every column at once, so a per-keystroke commit would splatter half-typed values across the whole row.
  - **Localization**: no hard-coded strings. Layout captions use `${vortex.furni_editor.*}` / `${vortex.furni_definition.*}` through `WindowParser.localizationResolver`, and the strings built in code go through `getLocalizationWithParamMap` with the key itself as the default, so a missing key is visibly missing rather than blank. The keys ship as plain `key=value` lists in `packages/vortex-client/src/vortex-locale/` for the hotel to paste into its own localization file — no loader, deliberately: the external file stays the single source of truth.
  - **UI redesign pass** (both windows). Fields the operator can't be expected to know by heart became dropdowns (`<dropmenu>` → `IDropMenuWindow.populateWithStrings`/`selection`/`enumerateSelection`): the definition editor's **interaction (`logic`)** — all 151 `[RoomObjectLogic("...")]` keys, extracted from `Vortex.Rooms/Object/Logic/**`, in `FurniDefinitionOptions.ts` — plus **product / category / usage / stuff-data**, mapped index↔enum value. The logic dropdown injects the current server value if the embedded list has drifted, so an existing row is never silently reassigned (drift only hides a value from the picker). Genuinely free fields stay inputs. Both authored layouts were rebuilt in the glaze vocabulary — tinted section-header bars (`container background` + bold `il_button` label), a shared label column (x=8) and field column so inputs align, 21-high `style=105` boxes and `style=100` dropmenus — replacing the flat, unaligned 28px stack. Styles verified present in the client's own dump skins (dropmenu 100, checkbox 101). New section-header localization keys added to the two `.txt` lists.
  - Both packages typecheck + lint clean (0 errors), full production build passes and bundles the layouts; the emulator solution builds with 0 errors.

- ✅ **Room furniture-manipulation click shortcuts (CTRL=pick up, SHIFT=rotate) — the "Habbo keyboard shortcuts" that are actually mouse-modifier gestures.** An exhaustive AS3 sweep established there are essentially **no room keyboard shortcuts** (no arrow-walking/camera pan/screenshot): the only in-room keyboard use is chat (SHIFT+Enter=shout, already ported at `RoomChatInputView.ts:391`). Everything users call a "shortcut" is a modifier+click in `RoomObjectEventHandler` (`_SafeCls_1821.as`): plain click sends the click-through (gated off at line 581 when any modifier is held), then `handleRoomObjectMouseClick` branches — **SHIFT-only click rotates floor furni** (cat 10 only, line 753 → `REOE_REQUEST_ROTATE`), **CTRL-only click picks up floor/wall furni** (line 763 → `REOE_REQUEST_PICKUP`), ALT-drag moves (mouse-down, line 1065 → `REOE_REQUEST_MOVE`). These `REOE_REQUEST_*` events were defined in the port but **never emitted or handled**.
  - **Wired the full faithful chain**: `RoomEngine.handleObjectMouseEvent` now emits `REOE_REQUEST_ROTATE`/`REOE_REQUEST_PICKUP` for the two exact gestures (only these are intercepted; plain clicks and other combos fall through to normal selection; disabled in game mode per AS3 line 751) → `RoomUI` subscribes the three request events → `RoomDesktop.roomObjectEventHandler` handles them exactly as AS3 `RoomDesktop.as:1222-1236`: `checkFurniManipulationRights()` (ported: controllerLevel≥1 / isAnyRoomController / isOwnerOfFurniture / `activeRoomHasFreeFurniMovementsMode`) gates MOVE/ROTATE, PICKUP is ungated (server validates), then `modifyRoomObject(OBJECT_MOVE/OBJECT_ROTATE_POSITIVE/OBJECT_PICKUP)` — all three cases already existed. Added `RoomEngine.activeRoomHasFreeFurniMovementsMode` (reads the `free_furni_movements_mode` room var). Also removed a leftover `[CLICK]` debug `log.info` that was flooding the console on every furni click.
  - **Follow-up pass — the remaining modifier/wheel gestures wired** (same faithful chain):
    - **ALT+drag = move floor furni (cat 10)** — added `RoomEngine.handleObjectMouseDown()` (ports `handleRoomObjectMouseDown`, `_SafeCls_1821.as:1040`): ALT-only mouse-down (or a plain drag in decorate mode, per `decorateModeMove()`) on floor furni dispatches `REOE_REQUEST_MOVE` → the existing `RoomDesktop`/`checkFurniManipulationRights`/`modifyRoomObject(OBJECT_MOVE)` chain → the ghost follows the cursor (`handleObjectMove`) and a click confirms (`confirmObjectMove`). Routed via a new `ROE_MOUSE_DOWN` branch in `handleObjectMouseEvent`. AS3 also dispatches this for wall (cat 20) and bot/plant (cat 100), but the port's move machinery + wall/user-move composers are cat-10-only (pre-existing documented gaps), so only cat 10 is enabled.
    - **Wheel-rotate-preview** — ported `RoomEngine.rotateActiveObjectPreview()` (`_SafeCls_1821.as:829`, pure-local: validates the next direction, `setDirection`, `updateSelectedObjectData`, `recalibrateMovements`). Wired in `App.ts._onWheel`: a plain wheel (no modifier) over the room while a cat-10 place/move preview is active rotates the ghost (Flash delta>0 = scroll-up = forward → DOM `deltaY < 0`); CTRL+wheel still zooms.
    - **CTRL+click monsterplant = pick up** — added `modifyRoomObject` `OBJECT_PICKUP_PET` (`roomSession.pickUpPet(webID)` resolved via `userDataManager.getUserDataByIndex`). Fully works (pets are server-supported).
  - **Deferred, `TODO(AS3)` (genuinely blocked, documented in-code)**: rentable-**bot** pickup/rotate + monsterplant **rotate** need the unported user-move/bot composers (`_SafeCls_3108` id 2743, `_SafeCls_2801` id 1295, `_SafeCls_2560` id 432) **and the Turbo server has no bots** (docs/CLIENT-SERVER-ARCHITECTURE.md §"Bots — Do Not Exist"), so building them would be untestable dead wire code — the gestures are recognised, the send is stubbed with a `log.warn`; **cat-20 wall move** + **cat-100 move commit** (wall-move composer + `getLegacyGeometry`, user-move composer — same pre-existing gaps); **SHIFT+drag rectangular area selection** (`RoomAreaSelectionManager`'s interactive tile-highlight is a standing TODO — a decorate multi-select subsystem, left as one deliberate remaining piece). Both packages typecheck + lint clean (0 errors).

- 🔴 **Clicking empty space never deselected, so no context UI ever closed — `RoomEngine` was discarding `canvas.handleMouseEvent()`'s return value.** Surfaced live: the own-avatar bubble wouldn't dismiss on clicking away, and the user noted *the furni/user InfoStand panels had the same problem* — which is what moved the search upstream of the new widget to the shared cause.
  - **Root cause.** AS3 (`_SafeCls_90.as:2391-2398`) dismisses from the **canvas** mouse handler: `if(!canvas.handleMouseEvent(...)) { if(click) dispatch REOE_DESELECTED(roomId, -1, MINIMUM) }` — an unconditional deselect whenever no room object consumed the click. The port called `canvas.handleMouseEvent(...)` and **threw the boolean away** (`RoomEngine.handleRoomCanvasMouseEvent`), so the "nothing consumed this click" branch did not exist. `deselectRoomObject()` is additionally guarded on a prior selection, so it could not cover the gap either. `InfoStandWidget` already listened for `RWROUE_OBJECT_DESELECTED`; it simply never arrived. Fixed by capturing the return and emitting the AS3 event — which closed the InfoStand panels with no change to them.
  - **A wrong first attempt worth recording**: the same AS3 snippet was first ported into `handleObjectMouseEvent`, which only runs once a room object *was* hit — so an empty-space click never reached it and nothing changed. The fix was matched to AS3 by code content instead of by tracing which port function corresponds to the AS3 one. The second pass added a temporary `[CLICK-AWAY]` log to confirm the real path from the console rather than deduce it again.
- ✅ **Own-avatar bubble finished to AS3 parity** (follow-up to the slice below): opens on **clicking your own avatar** (the id needed for positioning was only resolved in `selectOwnAvatar()`, so the direct-click entry produced an invisible bubble until the toolbar me-menu had been used once); real **minimize** collapsing to the `minimized_menu` bubble and re-expanding on click; **name change** wired to `habboHelp.startNameChange()` with `IHabboHelp` added to `IRoomWidgetHandlerContainer` (injected RoomUI → RoomDesktop, mirroring the inventory wiring); and a null-guard pass on `HabboInventory`'s effect API (`_effectsModel` is created in the lazy `init()`, so the bubble could read effects before the inventory existed — AS3 guards `effectsModel == null` in each method). dance/expression/posture, effects, and drop-carry/wired-inspect/open-profile all reach their handlers through the faithful message routing. Only `change_looks` (avatar editor) and `decorate` (DecorateModeView) remain `TODO(AS3)`, blocked on unported subsystems.

- ✅ **Own-avatar bubble menu (`RWE_AVATAR_INFO` slice) — the faithful trigger for the effects widget** (`habbo/ui/widget/avatarinfo/`, `habbo/ui/widget/contextmenu/`, `habbo/ui/handler/AvatarInfoWidgetHandler.ts`). The effects flyout shipped without a trigger; the authentic entry point is the in-room bubble next to your own avatar (`OwnAvatarMenuView`). Investigation established `MeMenuMainView`/`RWE_ME_MENU` is legacy dead code in WIN63 — the modern bubble is `OwnAvatarMenuView`, part of the `RWE_AVATAR_INFO` widget. Ported the **own-avatar slice**, deferring pets/bots/other-avatars/decorate/breeding/use-product.
  - **Built**: the message primitives (`RoomWidgetDanceMessage`/`RoomWidgetAvatarExpressionMessage`/`RoomWidgetChangePostureMessage`, `AvatarExpressionEnum`), `AvatarInfoData` (`_SafeCls_2798`, populated from the existing `RWUIUE_OWN_USER` event), the context-menu view base hierarchy (`ContextInfoView` build/show/hide/per-frame-position + `ButtonMenuView` showButton/showButtonGrid/hover + `AvatarContextInfoButtonView`), `OwnAvatarMenuView` (mode-driven buttons: normal/club-dances/expressions/signs; each dispatches a `RoomWidget*` message), the shell `AvatarInfoWidget` (opens on `RWUIUE_OWN_USER`, repositions each frame via the update receiver + live state getters isDancing/hasClub/hasEffectOn/hasVip/posture/swimming/riding), and `AvatarInfoWidgetHandler` (toolbar MEMENU → `selectOwnAvatar`; routes dance/expression/posture → `roomSession.send*`). Wired `RWE_AVATAR_INFO` into `RoomDesktop.createWidget` + `RoomWidgetFactory` (RoomUI already creates it).
  - **Reused**: the 4 avatar composers (Dance/Expression/ChangePosture/Sign via `RoomSession.send*`), `getRoomObjectBoundingRectangle` for positioning, the toolbar `HabboToolbarEvent` MEMENU click, the ported `InfoStandWidgetHandler`'s `RWUIUE_OWN_USER` emission, the existing `FixedSizeStack`, and `EffectsWidgetHandler` (the bubble's `effects` button dispatches `RWRWM_EFFECTS`, closing the loop from the effects branch).
  - **AS3 adaptations** (documented in-code): positioning calls `roomEngine.getRoomObjectBoundingRectangle` directly instead of the `RWGOI` message round-trip (+ derives the screen point from the rect, since `getRoomObjectScreenLocation` is private); `selectOwnAvatar` sends `GET_OBJECT_INFO` (no `roomEngine.selectAvatar` in the port); action routing is consolidated into one handler (AS3 splits it across MeMenu/InfoStand handlers); `isDancing` is derived optimistically from the sent dance style (no `RSDE_DANCE` round-trip); the hover hit-test refinement and the FixedSizeStack are simplified/reused.
  - **Deferred, `TODO(AS3)`**: `openAvatarEditor` (avatar editor not ported), decorate mode (`isUserDecorating` stub), name-change (`habboHelp` not ported), the sign-grid per-icon `setImageAsset`, the minimized/new-user-help views, and the friend relationship badge. Typechecks + lints clean (0 errors).

- ✅ **Avatar effects UI — the me-menu EffectsWidget + its owned-effects data pipeline** (`habbo/inventory/effects/`, `habbo/ui/widget/effects/`). Investigation first corrected two stale premises: (1) the effect **render/apply** pipeline (network `AvatarEffect` 3629 → `AvatarLogic`/`AvatarVisualization.appendAction(EFFECT)` → `EffectAssetDownloadManager` → fx animation) was **already fully ported and wired** — nothing to build there; (2) the **emulator** (`vortex-emulator`, Turbo Cloud) effects subsystem was **not** deferred as its docs claimed — it is fully implemented (parsers/handlers/`PlayerEffectGrain`/`player_effects` table/serializers/login-push/room re-sync/catalog grant/expiry/wired) and **byte-compatible** with the client. All effect header IDs and all 5 serializer field orders were cross-checked against the WIN63 registry (`_SafeCls_2046.as`) and the client parsers and **match** (2405/1577/3814/2236/3629 out; 2362/3022 in). The emulator's extra Selected-ack composer at 1333 is harmless — the client registers no handler at 1333 (no collision).
  - **What was actually missing was the client-side effects *UI* + data population.** The `inventory/effects/` `EffectsModel`/`Effect` existed but were **orphaned** (never populated; `EffectsModel` was a data-pure divergence that sent no composers). Reworked `EffectsModel` to the AS3 shape (takes an `IConnection`, sends `AvatarEffectActivatedComposer` 3022 / `AvatarEffectSelectedComposer` 2362 itself). Ported the 3 missing incoming pushes — `AvatarEffectAdded` (1577), `AvatarEffectActivated` (3814), `AvatarEffectExpired` (2236), parser read-orders verified against `unknowns/_SafePkg_2975/*`. Added the inventory-owned effects message handling (faithful to `inventory/_SafeCls_1951.as`) in `HabboInventory` — populates `EffectsModel` from 2405/1577/3814/2236 and fires `HabboInventoryEffectsEvent('HIEE_EFFECTS_CHANGED')` — plus the `getAvatarEffects()`/`setEffectSelected()`/`setEffectDeselected()`/`getAvatarEffect()` API the widget consumes. (`SessionDataManager` still caches 2405 in a dead field; the `MessageRegistry` allows both listeners.)
