The real modern surface is the in-room me-menu flyout, so `EffectsWidget` + `EffectView` (`memenu_effect_{selected,unselected,inactive}` rows, countdown bar, icon) + `EffectsWidgetHandler` (RWE_EFFECTS) were ported from `ui/widget/effects/`. Wired: `IRoomWidgetHandlerContainer.inventory` (new; injected RoomUI→RoomDesktop via `IID_HabboInventory`), registered in `RoomWidgetFactory` + `RoomDesktop.createWidget` + created on room enter in `RoomUI`. `App.ts` now eager-decodes `fx_icon_*`/`memenu_fx_*` into the asset library (they were URL-registered only — same silent-blank-bitmap trap the `ctlg_` fix addressed).
  - **Deferred, `TODO(AS3)`: the trigger.** The faithful entry point (the avatar-bubble me-menu `ui/widget/memenu/MeMenuMainView`, or the own-avatar `OwnAvatarMenuView` 'effects' case) is not ported, so nothing yet dispatches `RWRWM_EFFECTS`. The widget is complete and opens as soon as that message is sent; entry-point choice left to a follow-up. Typechecks + lints clean (0 errors).

- 🔴 **Landing view: every fixed-placeholder widget (avatarimage, dailyquest, catalogpromo, communitygoal, expiringcatalogpage, etc. — all 15 `FIXED_WIDGET_TYPES`) was silently never created, despite the module being marked "fully ported."** Surfaced live: the hotel view's `widget_placeholder_avatarimage` icon never got swapped for the real avatar, and the browser console showed nothing at all about it — not even a failed-lookup warning, which was the tell that `initialize()` was never being reached.
  - **Root cause — `WidgetContainerLayout.ts::registerPlaceholderAnchoredWidget()` had an invented `if(!this._landingView || !this._settings) return;` guard with no AS3 equivalent.** The constructor calls `registerFixedWidgets()` *before* assigning `this._settings = new CommonWidgetSettings(...)`, in both AS3 and this port, so `_settings` is genuinely `null` at that call site — matching AS3 exactly. AS3's own `registerPlaceholderAnchoredWidget()` doesn't check for that; it pushes the `WidgetContainer` unconditionally, passing the still-null settings through (harmless: `WidgetContainer` accepts a null `settings`, and none of the 15 fixed widget types implement `ISettingsAwareWidget`, so nothing ever reads it). The added guard turned that AS3-faithful `null` into a silent, permanent no-op — `_widgetContainers` never received any of the 15 fixed widgets, so `WidgetContainerLayout.activate()`'s refresh loop had nothing to iterate and every `widget_placeholder_*` XML placeholder stayed on screen forever. Fixed by dropping the `!this._settings` half of the guard.
  - **A real but secondary bug found en route**: `friendbar/landingview/widget/AvatarImageWidget.ts::initialize()` called `landingView.getXmlWindow('avatar_image')`, which resolves to the bare `avatar_image_xml` key — but that asset was split into `HabboFriendBar_avatar_image_xml`/`HabboWindowManager_avatar_image_xml` by the collision-qualification fix described below (same-named embed in two components), and the bare key was never re-pointed at either. `habbo/window/widgets/AvatarImageWidget.ts:96` already had the equivalent fix (with a comment documenting the exact collision) for its own, unrelated internal lookup — the landing-view call site had just been missed. Fixed to call `windowManager.buildWidgetLayout('HabboFriendBar_avatar_image_xml')` directly. This alone had no visible effect until the guard above was also fixed, since the code path calling it was unreachable either way.
  - **Diagnosis method, worth repeating**: reading `WidgetContainer.refresh()` in isolation looked correct (it unconditionally removes+disposes the placeholder once `initialize()` runs, even if the widget's container came back null) — the actual break was one level up, in whether `refresh()` ever got a `WidgetContainer` to call in the first place. Confirmed via live console probes against the running client (`Vortex.instance.windowManager`, walking the real desktop window tree with `findChildByName`/`getChildByName`, and calling `buildWidgetLayout()` directly to rule out an asset-side failure) rather than more code-reading — the asset lookup worked standalone the whole time, which is what pointed at construction/registration instead of asset resolution.

- 🔴 **Window layouts and skins now ship as XML named exactly as AS3 names them; the JSON compile step is gone, and with it two silent asset-corruption bugs.** Five tools (`compile-window-layouts`, `compile-window-skins`, `build-asset-name-manifest`, `organize-binary-layout-xml`, `import-crypted-layouts`, `import-crypted-skins`) collapse into one `build-window-assets.mjs`; `WindowLayoutXmlSerializer.ts` is deleted.
  - **The JSON was a round trip.** `registerWidgetLayout(name, xml)` has always taken an XML *string*: the pipeline compiled XML→JSON at build time, then `WindowLayoutXmlSerializer` re-serialized JSON→XML at boot to feed the same parser. `WindowParser` already folds `<params>` children into the param bitfield and URL-decodes `name`/`tags`/`caption` at runtime, exactly as AS3 does, so everything the compile step pre-baked was recomputed anyway. Assets are now emitted verbatim.
  - **Naming is now single-sourced from `*Com.as`**, the field name AS3 passes to `assets.getAssetByName()`. The old chain guessed three ways: a uid manifest built from the sibling `../vortex-client` checkout (which resolved only 157 of 783 layouts — its regex matched `public static const`, and 2045 of that tree's 2535 declarations are `var`), a filename derivation off `binaryDataXml_organized/` (whose names come from the XML's internal `<layout name="...">`, a Flash-authoring label AS3 never reads), and `resolveSkinAssetId()`'s layout→asset back-inference. All three are gone, along with the `../vortex-client` dependency. 2958 declarations resolve with **0 unresolved XML refs**.
  - **Two real bugs, both from collapsing an embed's linkage name to its short form** (`stripTypeSuffix`, still correct for the image/chatstyle importers that own it). `avatar_image_xml` is declared by *both* HabboFriendBar (`_SafeCls_224`) and HabboWindowManager (`_SafeCls_223`) — different embeds, same field name, because AS3 asset libraries are per-component. Both reduce to `avatar_image`, so only one file was ever written and FriendBar's variant was silently lost. Worse, `_SafeCls_894` (`header_png$…`, a `Bitmap` declared as `badge_leaderboard_header`) and `_SafeCls_895` (`header_xml$…`, the real `habbo_window_layout_header_xml`) both reduce to `header` — so the header *layout* was shipped under an *image's* name. The `*_png` entries that used to sit in `window-layouts/` (`icon_messenger_png`, `illumina_light_switch_png`, `icons_facebook`) were all this. Declarations are now joined to files on the whole linkage name, hash included; genuine same-name collisions are qualified by component (3 of them).
  - **`normalizeAssetName()` no longer strips `_xml` from the element description's `asset`/`window_layout`.** Both are asset-library keys AS3 uses verbatim; the strip forced the old pipeline to *also* emit 61 `habbo_window_layout_*` aliases so `getLayoutByTypeAndStyle()` and `loadSkinAssets()` could find them. It still strips `_png` for atlas refs, which do map to filenames.
  - **`InfoStandFurniView`'s white background was a port bug, not an asset bug** — and the previous "fix" (hand-editing the generated `furni_view.json`'s `info_border` from style 2 to style 1, commit `7806456`) masked it and was erased by regeneration. Style 2 is `border_colorless`: a deliberately untinted skin. AS3's `update()` tints it per provenance — `isBuilderClubId` → `0x331C00`, `isTempId` → `0x142B44`, else `0x3D3D3D` — and sets `spacerColor` alongside. The port had an invented `isBuilderClubId(id) ? 0xD77900 : 0xFFFFFF`, no temp branch, and no `spacerColor` setter at all; `0xFFFFFF` is the white. Ported for real, along with `isTempId`/`isNormalId` and the true ranges (`sources/win63_version/habbo/utils/class_2262.as` is an older revision with an unbounded `isBuilderClubId` and no temp range — the port had followed it).
  - **Assets are no longer tracked.** They are derived dump content like `sources/`, `configurations/` and `*.png`; the 990 committed JSON files were the anomaly. Both directories are now gitignored and regenerated per checkout.
  - **Verified**, not reasoned: all 916 documents parse in the real Blink `DOMParser` (Edge headless, mirroring `parseXmlDocument`'s own repair fallback) with 1 needing the existing `repairMalformedAttributeSpacing()` and 0 failures; and the client boots to `Element registry loaded: 332 descriptors` + `Skin assets loaded: 309 renderers registered from 132 skins` — the latter being the decisive check, since a wrong `asset` key would have matched no descriptors and registered 0.
  - Known gap, pre-existing: `RoomUserCountWidget` requests `room_user_count_xml`, which is declared nowhere in the AS3 and never existed as an asset. It resolved to nothing before this change too.

- 🟢 **Pet pickup from a room now actually fires — the last missing link in the pet infostand chain.** The pet buttons (`btn_pick`/`btn_kick` for an owned pet) dispatched `RoomWidgetUserActionMessage('RWUAM_PICKUP_PET', petId)` correctly, but `InfoStandWidgetHandler.processWidgetMessage()` had **no `RoomWidgetUserActionMessage` branch at all** — every `RWUAM_*` message fell straight through to the `UNIMPLEMENTED_WIDGET_MESSAGES` debug log and was dropped. Ported AS3's `processWidgetMessage()` `RWUAM_*` pet cases as `processUserActionMessage()`: it pre-resolves the pet via `userDataManager.getPetUserData(userId)` and bails on `null` (AS3's stale-click guard), then routes `PICK_UP_PET`→`pickUpPet`, `MOUNT_PET`/`DISMOUNT_PET`, `TOGGLE_PET_RIDING/BREEDING_PERMISSION`, `SADDLE_OFF`, and `RESPECT_PET` (the spaced `' RWUAM_RESPECT_PET'` literal) to the already-ported `roomSession`/`sessionDataManager` methods. `pickUpPet` → `PickUpPetComposer` (header **1640**, answered by the emulator's `RemovePetFromFlatMessageHandler` → `PickUpPetInRoomAsync`). Pet care composers (treat/water/light: AS3 `_SafeCls_1909`/`_SafeCls_2695`) are unported and left as a visible `TODO(AS3)`; the user-scoped `RWUAM_*` cases (whisper/kick/ban/…) belong to the not-yet-wired InfoStandUserView and stay in the unimplemented list.
  - **Guest place-pet now wired too.** `PetsModel.placePetToRoom()` had the room-owner path (drag-drop via `initializeRoomObjectInsert`) but the guest branch (non-owner, pets allowed) was a `log.warn` stub. Ported AS3's `new _SafeCls_2777(pet.id, 0, 0)` as `PlacePetComposer` (`[petId, x, y]`, header **1018** `PlacePetMessageEvent`, matching the emulator's `PlacePetMessageParser` → `PlacePetMessageHandler`). AS3 always sends x=y=0; the server resolves the drop tile. Not to be confused with `MovePetMessageComposer` (4-arg vortex-custom, header 2761), which repositions an already-placed pet.

- 🔴 **Pets are usable for the first time: they render as themselves in a room, and clicking one opens the infostand. Three separate one-line-shaped bugs, each found by a trace, none by reading code.** Verified live against the running client + emulator.
  - **Pets rendered as black boxes — and had since forever.** `RoomEngine.addObjectUser()` skipped AS3's `if(_loc11_ == "pet") _loc11_ = getPetType(param7);`, so the room object was created with the literal type `'pet'`. No asset library is called that, and `RoomContentLoader.hasInternalContent()` recognises only `'user'`/`'game_snowball'`/`'game_snowsplash'`, so `RoomManager` resolved logic+visualization off a non-existent bundle and hit its `default:` — **`FurnitureLogic` + `FurnitureVisualization`**. `FurnitureLogic` ignores `RoomObjectAvatarFigureUpdateMessage`, so the figure was dropped, the model never got `pet_type`/`pet_palette_index`/`pet_color`, and **`PetLogic` was attached to nothing — dead code since it was ported**. Ported AS3's `getPetType(figure)` (first figure token → `contentLoader.getPetType(typeId)`) and made `addRoomObjectUser()` pass `type` to `createRoomObject()` instead of a mapped `logicType` — that mapping was a pure identity (`RoomObjectLogicEnum` and `RoomObjectUserTypes` declare the same four strings), so it changed nothing for users/bots but undid the pet-type resolution. This also explains why "pet rendering made to work" (below) did not cover it: that pass validated `getPetImage()`, the catalog path, which never goes through here.
  - **`UserDataManager.requestPetInfo()` was an empty `TODO(AS3)`** — the client never asked the server for pet info, so the whole Phase 8 chain sat idle waiting for a message that was never sent. Ported `GetPetInfoMessageComposer` (header **3899**, AS3-verified in the emulator's own `Headers.cs`, answered for real by `GetPetInfoMessageHandler` with `PetInfo` 3192) and wired it. Also ported `PetSelectedMessageComposer` (**2757**, likewise real server-side — it is what answers with `PetCommands` 332).
  - **Two `UserDataManager` instances, only one wired.** `SessionDataManager` builds one *and gives it a connection*; `RoomSession` builds its own and never did — and `container.roomSession.userDataManager` is the second one. Every composer-sending method on it was silently inert. AS3's `RoomSession.set connection()` forwards the connection to its UserDataManager (`if(var_1480 != null) var_1480.connection = param1;`); this port only did `this._connection = value`. Same shape as the duplicate-IStuffData-trees trap: two instance trees, the one in use is not the one wired.
  - **`InfoStandPetView` ported in full** (59-line stub → the 892-line AS3): name, centred pet image, level/breed/age, respect, the happiness/experience/energy bars or the monsterplant well-being/growth pair, and the right-to-left button strip. `PetCommandTool` is not ported, so the training tool is a documented `TODO(AS3)` at four call sites. Also ported `RoomWidgetUserActionMessage` (**absent entirely** — the pet buttons had no message class to dispatch; note `RESPECT_PET`'s value has a leading space verbatim from AS3, and the handler matches the same spaced literal) and `SecondsFormatter` (AS3's class name is obfuscated in the primary *and* secondary and absent from the tertiary — unrecoverable, so the class name is this port's; `formatSeconds()` is AS3's own).
  - **Method note.** All three root causes came from instrumenting the running client; every prior guess from reading the code was wrong (including "the black square is the `place_holder_pet`" — it was a real pet object with furniture logic). One probe that hardcoded room-object ids from an earlier session reported "no object" for a fix that was in fact working: check the trace measures the object you think it does.

- 🔴 **Phase 8 step 2: the 8 remaining `room/pets` events+parsers ported — and the premise that "the server already sends everything" is false for 6 of them.** Cross-checking every AS3 parser against `Revision20260701`'s serializer (the method that validated every parser in step 1) found that only **`PetCommands` (332)** and **`PetPlacingError` (3195)** agree with the server. Three serializers have an **empty body** (`PetStatusUpdate` 2753, `PetFigureUpdate` 3796, `PetRespectFailed` 31) and three write a different message than the AS3 defines (`PetLevelUpdate` 3104 omits the leading roomIndex; `PetBreedingResult` 2940 writes 3 ints instead of two 7-field records; `PetExperience` 946 writes 5 ints where the client reads 3). `Revision20260112` is identical throughout — unfinished server work, not a regression. All 8 composers *are* dispatched (2-4 call sites each). Full table now in `docs/CLIENT-SERVER-ARCHITECTURE.md` §18, which previously claimed pets were "Fully Implemented" — corrected to scope that claim to the game logic.
  - **`PetExperience` (946) is the only one that fails silently** and is the real hazard: the server writes more data than the client reads, so nothing throws — `petRoomIndex` receives the total experience and `gainedExperience` receives the next-level threshold. Carries a prominent `TODO(AS3)`. The other five throw `RangeError('End of buffer')`, which `SocketConnection` catches and logs per message (each packet has its own wrapper, so no stream desync) — a loud failure, which is the better kind.
  - Ported AS3-faithfully and registered regardless: the client's job is to speak the real protocol and the fix belongs in the emulator's serializers. Headers taken from the emulator's own `Revision20260701/Headers.cs` (several carry its "AS3-verified" annotations); 20260701 is the revision that pairs with `WIN63-202607011411`. **The primary AS3 tree has no pet message package at all** — it is package-obfuscated under `src/unknowns/`, so `win63_version` is the authority for this whole set (another instance of the known-wrong `src/unknowns/` claim in `CLAUDE.md`).
  - **A 5th occurrence of the `while(0 < count)` decompiler corruption** found in `parser/inventory/pets/class_2486.as` (the pet figure wire class `PetFigureUpdate` embeds) — the custom-part loop increments an index it never compares. Already correctly ported as `parsePetFigureData()`, which `PetFigureUpdateEventParser` reuses rather than duplicating.
  - **`PetRespectFailedEvent` was already ported and registered** under `incoming/notifications/` (its directory does not match AS3's `room/pets/`, but its parser is already field-exact). Writing a second copy would have **silently overwritten** the first, since `_events` is a `Map` and `.set()` does not warn — caught by a collision scan before the build. Left the original in place; a global scan now confirms no duplicate header exists anywhere in `HabboMessages.ts`.

- 🟢 **Phase 8: the `PetInfo` data chain now runs from the wire to `InfoStandPetData`** (1 new widget event + 3 files filled in), continuing `e72e51f` (`PetInfoMessageEvent` parser + header 3192 + `RoomUsersHandler.onPetInfo()` → `RoomSessionPetInfoUpdateEvent`). The break was that **nothing subscribed to `RSPIUE_PET_INFO`**: `InfoStandWidgetHandler.set container()` wired only its connection's group-details event and registered *none* of AS3's nine `roomSessionManager` listeners, so the session event was emitted into the void. The chain is now: click → `handleGetObjectInfoMessage()` case 100 → `handleGetPetInfoMessage(userData.webID)` (AS3 passes the **webID** here, not the room index it passes to the user/bot handlers) → `requestPetInfo()` → server → parser → `RoomUsersHandler` → `sessionEvents` → `InfoStandWidgetHandler.onPetInfo()` → `RoomWidgetPetInfoUpdateEvent` → `InfoStandWidget.onPetInfo()` → `InfoStandPetData.setData()`.
  - **`RoomWidgetPetInfoUpdateEvent`** ported (10 constructor-assigned read-only getters + 24 getter/setter pairs, preserving AS3's split); `InfoStandPetData.setData()` and `InfoStandWidget.onPetInfo()` were empty stubs and now copy AS3's exact field set — note AS3 deliberately does *not* copy `hasFreeSaddle`/`isRiding`/`canBreed`/`canHarvest`/`canRevive` into `InfoStandPetData`, and routes `petRespectLeft` to `InfoStandUserData` instead.
  - **The secondary tree's `onPetInfo()` is decompiler-corrupted and the primary is clean** — the same trap as `PetAnimationSizeData` above. `win63_version` computes the monsterplant growth posture (`"std"` / `"grw"+level`) into a local, then drops it: the cache key decompiles to `figure + ""` and the image call to `getPetImage(figure, null)`. The primary keeps both: key is `figure + "/posture=" + posture`, call is `getPetImage(figure, posture)`. Porting the secondary verbatim would have collapsed every growth stage of a monsterplant onto one cache entry.
  - **`InfoStandPetView` was still a 59-line stub against 892 lines of AS3** at the time of this entry (`window` always null), so `selectView(PET)` resolved to no child and the panel showed nothing. Superseded — see the pet-usability entry above, which ports the view and closes the chain.
  - ~~`PetSelectedMessageComposer` … its header is unverified and `Revision20260701` has no handler for it.~~ **This was wrong.** The header is AS3-verified (2757) and `PetSelectedMessageHandler` does exist. The claim came from a grep run with a relative `vortex-emulator/` path from inside `vortex-modern-client/`, where that directory does not exist — it returned nothing, and the empty result was read as proof of absence. A false negative is not evidence; check the command resolves before trusting a null result. Composer now ported and registered.

- 🔴 **Pet rendering made to work for the first time — 10 engine bugs, found by driving the real client, most of them not pet-specific.** Phase 7's widgets were the first caller to exercise `RoomEngine.getPetImage()` end-to-end, and it had never functioned since being ported. Every one of these failed *silently* (a `continue`, a default, a coercion) rather than throwing, which is why they had gone unnoticed. Verified live against the running client at each step.
  - **`RoomObjectVisualizationFactory`**: `case PET_ANIMATED` was missing from `createRoomObjectVisualization()`'s switch entirely, so every pet resolved to a null visualization and `getGenericRoomObjectImage()` returned `id = -1`. `pet_animated` was also folded into the *avatar* branch of `getRoomObjectVisualizationData()` and handed an `AvatarVisualizationData`; AS3 gives it its own `AnimatedPetVisualizationData` (Nitro's own factory agrees). Added an `assets` setter (AS3 reads `assets` off its Component base) so `AnimatedPetVisualizationData.commonAssets` can be injected, wired from `RoomEngine`'s constructor.
  - **`AnimatedPetVisualizationData` had three *invented* overrides** — `getDirectionValue()`/`getZOffset()`/`getTag()` — with no AS3 counterpart, shadowing `FurnitureVisualizationData`'s real implementations. `getDirectionValue()` returned `Math.floor(direction / 45) * 45`, i.e. the raw angle (90) instead of the visualization's own direction index (0-7), so every sprite was looked up as `cat_64_a_90_0` — a name no pet library contains. **Deleting them is what first made a pet appear.** They were labelled `// TODO: Implement...`, which is what made them read as gaps to fill rather than code to remove.
  - **Asset layer, 4 bugs, all affecting any palettised `.nitro` content and not just pets**: `RoomContentLoader` returns before `collection.define()` for every bundle with a spritesheet, and `define()` was the only place palettes were parsed — so no `.nitro` library ever had any (`defineFromSpritesheet()` now takes them). `rgb` is an array of `[r,g,b]` triplets, not a flat byte run, and `new Uint8Array([[0,0,0],…])` coerces each triplet to 0 — every palette loaded solid black. `usesPalette` is a real JSON boolean and was read through `getNumber()`, which handled only numbers and strings, so it always answered "no palette". `colorizePalette()` blitted `texture.source.resource` — the *whole atlas* — at 0,0 into a frame-sized canvas, colourising whatever sat in the atlas's top-left corner.
  - **`AnimatedPetVisualization` was missing four AS3 overrides**: `getAsset()` (pets are palette-swapped via `getAssetWithPalette()`; without it they rendered greyscale even though `_paletteName`/`_customLayerIds`/`_customPartIds`/`_customPaletteIds` were already being filled in and read by nobody), `getSpriteColor()` (same shape: `pet_color` was parsed off the model every frame and never applied), `getSpriteAssetName()`/`getSpriteZOffset()` + the `getDirection()`/`isHeadSprite()`/`isNonHeadSprite()`/`isSaddleSprite()` helpers (a pet's head resolves its own direction), and **`getAnimationId()`** — the last one found: furniture has one animation so the base answers with its single shared `animationId`, but a pet runs posture and gesture states concurrently and each must answer with its own. Without it both states resolved to the same animation and produced identical frames; since `getSpriteXOffset()`/`getSpriteYOffset()` sum the frames of *every* state (faithfully to AS3), each layer's offset was applied exactly twice — the head and tail rendered visibly detached.
  - **`PetAnimationSizeData` (AS3 name; Nitro calls it `PetSizeData`) had never been ported**, and `AnimatedPetVisualizationData`'s posture/gesture methods were stubs returning `-1`/`''`, `createSizeData()` explicitly commented "when pet rendering is implemented". Ported from the primary tree (the secondary's copy is decompiler-corrupted: `add(null,0)` instead of `add(_loc6_,_loc7_)`); uses `OrderedMap`, not a native `Map`, because AS3 reads these tables back *by index* via `getKey()`.
  - **`FurnitureVisualizationData.processVisualizationElements()` iterated a hard-coded `['layers','directions','colors','animations']`** where AS3 walks the visualization node's actual children. No subclass could contribute an element, so `AnimatedPetVisualizationData`'s `'postures'`/`'gestures'` cases were unreachable dead code and no pet ever had an animation. Now iterates the real keys — safe because an unknown name falls through the switch and returns true, in this port exactly as in AS3.
  - **`RoomEngine.getGenericRoomObjectImage()` did not honour AS3's `result.id = 0` "synchronous hit" contract** — documented on `ImageResult` itself, just never implemented. It handed the canvas to the async `createImageBitmap()` and delivered via `imageReady()` instead, on the stated grounds that Texture→ImageBitmap "is inherently asynchronous in the browser". It is not: `OffscreenCanvas.transferToImageBitmap()` is synchronous and this codebase already relied on that elsewhere. The consequence was not cosmetic: AS3-faithful callers call `onWidgetsInitialized()` from `imageReady()`, which re-dispatches `SelectProduct` → `updateImage()` → `getPetImage()`, and with an always-async result that re-enters `imageReady()` and **loops forever**, allocating a temporary-room object per iteration. Fixed at the source rather than guarded per widget (`NewPetsCatalogWidget` has the same shape and would have looped too).
  - **`App.registerImageAssets()` only fed the window manager's URL registry**, so `CatalogWidget.getAssetBitmapData()` — which reads `vortex.assets` — got null for every catalog bitmap: `ColourGridCatalogWidget`'s swatches and `RecyclerCatalogWidget`'s slot background rendered blank. The PNGs were present all along (`src/assets/images/ctlg_clr_*.png`); only the registration was missing. Scoped to the `ctlg_` prefix (each entry eagerly decodes an ImageBitmap).
  - **Still open**: `RoomManager.isContentAvailable()` answers `hasInternalContent()` where AS3 answers `getGraphicAssetCollection() != null`, so `getGenericRoomObjectImage()` always parks its listener in `_pendingImageListeners` — a queue drained only on content *load*. Left as a documented `TODO(AS3)` **with the two failing approaches recorded**: returning AS3's expression, with or without `&& isLoaded(type)`, regresses to no image at all, because `createRoomObject()` and this guard disagree about the same instant (the former builds against the place-holder library, the latter then reports "available" once the .nitro is browser-cached). The real fix is upstream, in the `updateObjectContents()` re-initialisation path.
- 🟢 **Phase 7 complete: pet catalog widgets — the 7-phase catalog roadmap is now closed** (3 widgets + a 4-file wire-protocol slice + 2 widget events + 4 `RoomEngine` delegates), taking `CatalogPage.createWidget()` from 36 to 39 of ~45 AS3 cases. Chosen as the roadmap's last item; `docs/CLIENT-SERVER-ARCHITECTURE.md` §18 confirms pets are 100% implemented server-side (`RoomPetSystem`, `PetPaletteEntity`, palettes loaded at startup), so this is exercisable end-to-end.
  - **Wire protocol** (`communication/messages/{outgoing,incoming,parser}/catalog/`): `GetSellablePetPalettesComposer` (header 3342, single string productCode) and `SellablePetPalettesMessageEvent`+`SellablePetPalettesMessageEventParser` (header 3350). Headers cross-verified against `../vortex-emulator/Turbo.Revisions/Revision20260701/Headers.cs`, whose AS3-verified comment for 3342 literally names both the primary class (`_SafeCls_2003`) and `HabboCatalog::getSellablePetPalettes()` as its sender. Confirmed the emulator's `Headers.cs` names messages from the **server's** perspective (its `…MessageEvent` = the client's composer and vice-versa) by checking three already-registered pairs.
  - **`SellablePetPalette`** (the per-entry DTO) has an unrecoverable class name in every tree (`[SecureSWF(rename="true")]`) — ported under a TS-derived name, documented as such, same handling as `GuildMembership`. Its getters are *not* obfuscated, so those names are real.
  - **`src/unknowns/` is not what CLAUDE.md says it is** — worth acting on. CLAUDE.md calls it "an unrelated, fully-obfuscated module bundled in the same dump, not part of the Habbo client". That is demonstrably wrong: the primary tree has **no `messages/*/catalog/` directories at all** (only `camera`/`inventory`/`landingview`/`quest`/`room`/`userdefinedroomevents`), because its catalog message layer was *package*-obfuscated and dumped into `unknowns/_SafePkg_1714` (parser + palette DTO), `_SafePkg_1716` (event) and `_SafePkg_1749` (composer). Those files are imported directly by the genuine `PetsCatalogWidget.as`/`NewPetsCatalogWidget.as`, and the same directory holds `SnowWarGameTokenOffer.as`/`SnowWarGameTokensMessageParser.as` under their **real** names. Following CLAUDE.md literally costs real information: the primary's parser copy is clean while the secondary's is decompiler-corrupted (see next bullet). This port's 4 new message files trace to the primary's `unknowns/` paths per an explicit user call; note the surrounding convention still traces messages to `win63_version` (192 files vs 2), which is consistent with the primary genuinely lacking them at any non-`unknowns/` path.
  - **Third instance of the `while(0 < count)` decompiler corruption**: `SellablePetPalettesMessageEventParser`'s secondary-tree copy has the index declared and incremented but never compared — an infinite loop. The primary's copy (`unknowns/_SafePkg_1714/_SafeCls_1905.as`) is correct and even keeps readable field names (`_productCode`/`_sellablePalettes`); it is what was ported. Same signature as `HabboClubOffersMessageEventParser` (Phase 2). `PetsCatalogWidget.as`'s secondary copy is corrupted in two more spots (`onDropMenuEvent()` loses its local, `updateAvailablePalettes()` passes `null` instead of the fetched array) where the primary is clean — a good reminder that tree priority is load-bearing, not cosmetic.
  - **`RoomEngine`/`IRoomEngine` gained 4 genuine AS3 members that had never been ported**: `getPetColor()`/`getPetColorsByTag()`/`getPetLayerIdForTag()`/`getPetDefaultPalette()`. All four already existed on `RoomContentLoader` but were exposed nowhere, while the widgets call them via `roomEngine.*`. Ported as AS3's null-guarded delegates with its exact fallbacks (`null`/`null`/`-1`/`null`) — note `getPetColorsByTag()` returns `null`, not an empty array, on a missing loader, and that distinction is preserved rather than smoothed over.
  - **`HabboCatalog`**: real `approveName()` and `getSellablePetPalettes()` (an `OrderedMap` cache that returns `null` on a miss and fires the request; the response returns via `onSellablePetPalettes()` → `CatalogWidgetSellablePetPalettesEvent`), plus `onApproveNameResult()`. **`ApproveNameMessageEvent` was registered in `HabboMessages` but had no listener anywhere — dead code until now.** `showPurchaseConfirmation()` gained AS3's params 7-8: param7 is **declared and never read anywhere in AS3's own method body** (a dead parameter in the original client, kept for signature fidelity), and param8 (the rendered pet image) is accepted but not displayed — AS3 forwards it to `PurchaseConfirmationDialog.showOffer()`, which is this port's documented minimal stub with no preview-image surface, so it is a `TODO(AS3)` rather than a scope expansion into the dialog.
  - **Widgets**: `PetsCatalogWidget` (classic pets, type index < 8: breed drop-menu + colour swatches), `NewPetsCatalogWidget` (type index >= 8: per-palette primary/secondary colour swatches, no drop-menu, purchase colour hardcoded white since the palette carries the colouring), `PetPreviewCatalogWidget` (read-only preview, decodes `customParams` four different ways by furniture category 13-16). The two buy widgets split on exactly the `< 8` / `>= 8` boundary and each bails out of `init()` for the other's range. `NewPetsCatalogWidget`'s swatches are consumed by the already-ported `ColourGridCatalogWidget` via `MULTI_COLOUR_ARRAY`.
  - **TS deviation, documented in all three**: `getPetImage()` returns null where AS3 returns a ready `BitmapData`, because `RoomEngine` always resolves an `ImageResult` through the asynchronous `id>0` path (a pre-existing architectural constraint documented on `ImageResult` itself: Texture→ImageBitmap cannot be synchronous in a browser). AS3 has that same async path and does the same thing in its own `imageReady()`, so the preview behaves identically. AS3's `BitmapData` compositing (`fillRect` to a fully transparent `0x00FFFFFF`, Matrix upscale, centred `copyPixels`) is reproduced on an `OffscreenCanvas` per `ColourGridCatalogWidget`'s established recipe. `PetPreviewCatalogWidget`'s dead `_gridItemLayout` field is omitted (AS3 assigns it and never reads it; this port has no raw-XML asset path anyway). Several AS3 quirks preserved rather than "fixed": both buy widgets' `onPurchase()` only *logs* on a pending name approval and falls through, the colour-index bound checks use `>` instead of `>=`, and `PetPreviewCatalogWidget`'s category-16 branch logs a short param list but does not break.
- 🟢 **Phase 6 complete: guild catalog cluster** (`habbo/catalog/guilds/GuildMembershipsController.ts` + 4 new catalog widgets + a small guild wire-protocol slice), taking `CatalogPage.createWidget()` from 32 to 36 of ~45 AS3 cases. Chosen next per the 7-phase roadmap because `docs/CLIENT-SERVER-ARCHITECTURE.md` §14 confirms Groups/Guilds is fully implemented server-side (unlike Recycler/Club-VIP when those phases started), so this is exercisable end-to-end immediately.
  - **Wire protocol** (`communication/messages/{outgoing,incoming,parser}/users/`): `GetGuildMembershipsMessageComposer`/`GetGuildCreationInfoMessageComposer` (both empty-payload) and `GuildMembershipsMessageEvent`+`GuildMembershipsMessageEventParser` (array-of-`GuildMembership`). Headers (3918/2989 outgoing, 3994 incoming) cross-verified two ways: primary-tree class resolution via `win63_version` name recovery, and independently against `../vortex-emulator/Turbo.Revisions/Revision20260701/Headers.cs`'s own AS3-verified comments (one of which literally names `BuyGuildWidget::onButtonClicked()` as the real sender for header 2989). `GuildMembership` (the per-entry DTO, AS3: `_SafeCls_3623`/`class_3489`) has an unrecoverable class name in every source tree checked (`win63_version`, `win63_2023_version`, primary), consistent with a `[SecureSWF(rename="true")]` tag — ported under a TS-derived name, documented as such.
  - **`GuildMembershipsController`** (no `I*.as` interface in any tree, concrete class only): mediates one registered `GuildSelectorCatalogWidget` at a time, requests memberships on register, re-fetches on `onGuildVisualSettingsChanged(guildId)` (AS3's own `guildId` param is accepted but unused - re-fetch is gated only on "a selector widget is open"). `HabboCatalog.onGuildMemberships()`/`onGuildVisualSettingsChanged()` forward the raw incoming event through, matching the existing `ClubExtendController.onOffer()` precedent (not `ClubBuyController.onOffers()`'s pre-unwrapped-data precedent) - the two otherwise-similar AS3 handler shapes disagree on this and both were preserved faithfully where they already existed.
  - **`HabboCatalog.ts` gained a real `navigator` getter** (`IHabboNavigator`, sourced from a new `IID_HabboNewNavigator` dependency's `.legacyNavigator`) - a genuine pre-existing AS3 member (`get navigator() : _SafeCls_93 { return _newNavigator.legacyNavigator; }`) that had never been ported, needed by `GuildSelectorCatalogWidget.onFindGroups()` → `navigator.performGuildBaseSearch()`. Also wired `HabboCatalog`'s dependency on `IID_HabboGroupsManager`'s `GSCIME_GUILD_VISUAL_SETTINGS_CHANGED` event (AS3: a 4-arg `ComponentDependency` with only an event-listener array, no plain setter) using the existing `ComponentDependency`/`IDependencyEventListener` infrastructure, which already supported this exact shape unused until now. Currently dormant: `HabboGroupsManager` never dispatches that event yet (the guild-settings management UI that would fire it isn't ported), so `onGuildVisualSettingsChanged()` is real but unreachable today - same class of gap as `ClubBuyController.showConfirmation()`.
  - **`StringArrayStuffData.ts` gained a real `setArray(data: string[])`** (AS3: trivial passthrough setter, `_SafeStr_4556 = param1`) - needed by `GuildSelectorCatalogWidget.getPreviewerStuffData()` to build a literal-array stuff-data for the room previewer. This was a pre-existing, already-documented gap (`ItemGridCatalogWidget.ts`'s guild-recolor preview branch has a code comment noting `StringArrayStuffData` "only supports construction from a room-object model/message wrapper" - that branch itself is still not ported, but the underlying capability gap is now closed for any future work that needs it).
  - **Widgets**: `GuildSelectorCatalogWidget` (drop-list of the user's groups, 2-color swatch bitmap built via `OffscreenCanvas` fillRect - AS3's `GUILD_COLORS_BMP_BORDER_COLOR`/`_WIDTH` constants are preserved as declared-but-unreferenced, matching the original), `GuildForumSelectorCatalogWidget` (subclass: filters to forum-eligible groups, warns if the selection already has a forum), `GuildBadgeViewCatalogWidget` (badge preview, listens for `GUILD_SELECTED`; its AS3 ctor's `GuildMembershipsController` param is genuinely unused at runtime, ported for signature fidelity only), `BuyGuildWidget` (single button; AS3's ctor has an `if(_button)` guard that's always false at construction time, preserved as harmless dead code rather than cleaned up).
- 🔴 **Root-cause fix: `HabboWindowManager.buildFromXML()` passed the desktop as `parent` instead of `null`, unlike real AS3 - fixed at the source in `WindowParser`/`WindowContext`, not patched per call site.** User-reported symptom: a small unstyled grid-item-shaped badge (price text + currency icon) appeared fixed in the screen's top-left corner whenever a catalog page with an item grid loaded, and never went away, not even after closing the catalog; later found to reproduce for the chat style selector too. Found via `WindowDebuggerOverlay`'s picker + a new ancestor-chain readout (see below), which showed the orphan's parent was a bare `desktop_N`, not the real owning window.
  - **Investigation history (worth keeping - the first two fix attempts are real dead ends, not just noise)**: attempt 1 patched the 3 catalog call sites that build a `.clone()`-source template and never re-add it (`removeChild()` after construction) - fixed the reported symptom but left the same bug latent everywhere else `buildFromXML()` is called this way. Attempt 2 fixed `WindowParser`/`HabboWindowManager` to pass `parent = null` like AS3 (`HabboWindowManagerComponent.as::buildFromXML()`: `parseAndConstruct(param1, null, param3)`) - this is correct AS3 architecture (AS3's `WindowParser` constructor takes and stores its owning context independent of `parent`, which is why it can safely pass `null`; this port's `WindowParser` had no such field, so `buildFromXML()` was passing `desktop` as a workaround, with the side effect of auto-attaching every top-level built window to the desktop) - but shipping it alone broke room click-to-move, furniture clicks, and own-avatar clicks entirely, because `WindowContext.create()`'s `USE_PARENT_GRAPHIC_CONTEXT` (`0x10`) param-flag handling means a `null`-parent window with that flag routes to `_substituteParent` (a legitimate AS3 "holding pen" controller, `SubstituteParentController.ts`) instead of falling through to the desktop - and one specific caller (`RoomDesktopLayoutManager.setLayout()`, next bullet) was missing the explicit re-attach AS3 requires for exactly that case, which the *previous* desktop-fallback bug had been silently masking.
  - **The actual fix, in order**: (1) `WindowParser` constructor now takes and stores the owning `IWindowContext` (`core/window/utils/WindowParser.ts`), matching AS3's `WindowParser(context)` constructor - `parseSingleWindowEntity()` falls back to this stored context (`this._context`) instead of `parent.context` when there's no parent to read it off of. (2) `HabboWindowManager.buildFromXML()` now passes `null` as parent (was `desktop`), matching AS3 exactly. (3) `RoomDesktopLayoutManager.setLayout()` (`habbo/ui/RoomDesktopLayoutManager.ts`) - the ROOT of the *entire* room desktop UI (room view, infostand, room tools, chat input/chat widgets all nest inside it via `addRoomView()`/`addWidgetWindow()`) - was missing the explicit `desktop.addChild(this._layoutContainer)` call that real AS3 has right after building it (`DesktopLayoutManager.as::setLayout()` line 55: `(_layoutContainer.desktop as class_3151).addChild(_layoutContainer);` - confirmed via the clean `win63_2023_version` reference, since `win63_version`'s copy of this class is corrupted here). `room_desktop_layout_xml`'s root does carry the `0x10` flag (confirmed by inspecting the compiled JSON), so this one call site was a real, pre-existing gap that the old bug happened to paper over, not something introduced by fixing `WindowParser`.
  - **Audit method for the rest of the codebase**: rather than auditing all ~65 `buildFromXML()`/`buildWidgetLayout()` call sites by hand, wrote a one-off script checking every referenced compiled `window-layouts/*.json`'s root `params` for the `0x10` bit - only a root carrying that flag is even affected by the `parent=null` change (everything else still unconditionally falls through to `parent ?? this._desktop` inside `WindowContext.create()`, completely unchanged by this fix). Of the ~35 flagged layouts found this way, all but `room_desktop_layout_xml` already had a correct, explicit re-attach downstream (`rootWindow =` on an `IWidgetWindow` placeholder - used by all 26 files in `habbo/window/widgets/`, which internally does an unconditional `addChild()`; or `addGridItem()`/`addListItem()`/a direct `addChild()` by the constructing code's caller) - confirmed the shared `WindowController.addChild()`/list/grid-item-add implementations all correctly detach a child from whatever parent it already has (including `SubstituteParentController`) before reattaching, so these patterns are safe regardless of which parent a window started under.
  - Reverted the 3 catalog-specific `removeChild()` band-aids from attempt 1 once the real fix landed - they're redundant now (those templates simply never get attached anywhere to begin with) and AS3 has no equivalent of them.
  - **New debugger capability, not AS3-related** (`vortex-client/src/debugger/WindowDebuggerOverlay.ts`): the tree inspector previously only showed a picked window's *descendants*; there was no way to see what it was actually parented under. Added an ancestor-chain readout (`IWindow.parent` walked to the root) to both the node detail panel and the "Copy tree as text" output - this is what made both the original leak and the room-interactivity regression diagnosable at all.
- 🟡 **Catalog gap audit + 7-phase implementation roadmap** (plan file: see session notes) — three parallel deep-research passes inventoried all 48 AS3 widgets under `catalog/viewer/widgets/` against the 10 already ported, plus every dependent controller/logic/data class, cross-checked against `docs/CLIENT-SERVER-ARCHITECTURE.md` for real server-side coverage (Marketplace and Groups/Guilds confirmed real and working server-side; classic Trading confirmed a complete stub; Club/VIP purchase and Recycler server coverage unconfirmed, flagged as a risk to verify early). Sequenced into 7 phases: (1) no-new-protocol widgets, (2) Habbo Club/VIP purchase, (3) Marketplace, (4) Recycler + `CatalogObjectMover`, (5) offscreen furniture/room image rendering (`RoomEngine.getFurnitureImage`/`getWallItemImage`/`getRoomImage`/`getGenericRoomObjectImage`, currently 0% ported and blocking 5 widgets at once), (6) guild catalog cluster, (7) pets. Trax/song-disk widgets and classic trading explicitly marked out of scope (each would need porting an entire unrelated 0%-ported subsystem for 1-2 small widgets).
- 🟡 **Phase 1: 13 of ~16 no-new-protocol widgets ported** (`SpecialInfoWidget`, `WarningCatalogWidget`, `TextInputCatalogWidget`, `FirstProductSelectorCatalogWidget`, `SingleViewCatalogWidget`, `BuilderCatalogWidget`, `SoldLtdItemsCatalogWidget`, `MadMoneyCatalogWidget`, `AddOnBadgeViewCatalogWidget`, `FeaturedItemsCatalogWidget`, `ColourGridCatalogWidget`, `BundleGridViewCatalogWidget`, `ActivityPointDisplayCatalogWidget`), all registered in `CatalogPage.createWidget()`. 4 new internal widget-bus events added (`TextInputEvent`, `CatalogWidgetShowWarningTextEvent`, `CatalogWidgetRoomChangedEvent`, `CatalogWidgetMultiColoursEvent`). `MadMoneyCatalogWidget` ported faithfully as inert - confirmed directly in the primary AS3 source that `init()`'s button lookup is followed by an empty `if` block (no listener ever attached), so its `eventProc()`/`windowManager.alert("TODO", "Fix in MadMoneyCatalogWidget.as", ...)` is dead code in the original client too, not a porting gap.
  - **New `CatalogWidgetName.ts`** (TS-only, no AS3 equivalent): named constants for every `CatalogPage.createWidget()` layout-name case (ported and not-yet-ported alike), replacing the raw string literals both in that switch and in each widget's own `attachWidgetView()` call - user-requested cleanup, matches this codebase's existing "class with `static readonly` members" convention for AS3-style enums (`ActivityPointTypeEnum`, `HabboToolbarIconEnum`, ...) rather than a native TS `enum`.
  - **`BuilderCatalogWidget` surfaced a real cross-system gap deeper than the initial survey found**: its button-enabled state depends on `HabboCatalog.getBuilderFurniPlaceableStatusForOffer()`, which didn't exist on this port at all (only referenced in a pre-existing `isDraggable()` TODO comment). Real AS3 logic needs `builderFurniCount`/`builderFurniLimit`/`roomSession` (owner/guild-room/controller-level/user-data-manager), none of which are wired into the catalog yet. Added as an honest `TODO(AS3)` stub always returning `0` ("placeable") - safe because the actual placement action (`requestSelectedItemToMover()`) is itself still a `CatalogObjectMover`-blocked stub (Phase 4), so nothing is falsely blocked or falsely placed. Also fixed `requestSelectedItemToMover()`'s signature, which was missing the AS3 `placeMany: boolean = false` third parameter entirely.
  - **`FeaturedItemsCatalogWidget` needed a real (if small) `HabboCatalog.ts` data-plumbing fix, not just the widget**: `frontPageItems` was never stored anywhere, even though `CatalogPageMessageEventParser` already parses it in full (`FrontPageItem[]`, itself already ported). Added the missing `_frontPageItems` field + `frontPageItems` getter (now on `IHabboCatalog` too) and the one-line assignment in `onCatalogPage()` AS3 already does right before `showCatalogPage()`.
  - **`ColourGridCatalogWidget`'s AS3 `BitmapData.colorTransform()`-based swatch recolouring** ported via the same canvas `'multiply'`/`'destination-in'` compositing recipe already established in `BitmapSkinRenderer.colorizeEntity()`, using `OffscreenCanvas.transferToImageBitmap()` (synchronous) instead of the async `createImageBitmap()` to avoid turning a synchronous AS3 flow async. Confirmed the `color_chooser_cell`/`gridItem`/`badgeGridItem` one-off XML templates AS3 loads via `assets.getAssetByName()` + `buildFromXML()` are already present as ordinary compiled `window-layouts/*.json` and buildable via the normal `buildWidgetLayout()` path - no raw-XML-asset special-casing needed.
  - **Corrected mid-session**: `UserBadgeSelectorCatalogWidget` genuinely needs a new `HabboCatalog.inventory` getter (still doesn't exist at all) plus a new incoming badge-update message event and `buildFromXML`-per-item construction - still outstanding. `ActivityPointDisplayCatalogWidget`'s dependency turned out to already be fully ported as `ActivityPointTypeEnum.ts` (the earlier claim that it was missing was wrong - found the real file on a second, more careful look). Note: that existing port's `isVisible()` does **not** literally match the primary AS3 source's corrupted `if([1,2,4].indexOf(param1) != 1) return false` (which only ever passes for `param1 === 2`) - it implements the evidently-intended "hide only NO_OP_1/2/4" logic instead. This predates this session; flagged here rather than silently touched, since rewriting working, already-relied-upon code to match a near-certainly-decompiler-corrupted line needs an explicit call, not a drive-by "fix".
- 🟢 **Phase 1 complete: last 3 stragglers ported** (`RedeemItemCodeCatalogWidget`, `SpacesNewCatalogWidget`, `BundlePurchaseExtraInfoWidget` + the whole `bundlepurchaseinfodisplay/` sub-module), taking `CatalogPage.createWidget()` from 20 to 23 of ~45 AS3 cases:
  - **`RedeemItemCodeCatalogWidget`**: ported the previously-empty-stub `HabboCatalog.redeemVoucher()` for real, adding `RedeemVoucherMessageComposer` (header 471) plus the `VoucherRedeemOkMessageEvent`/`VoucherRedeemErrorMessageEvent` response events + parsers (headers 2083/2211, all three sourced from `win63_version/habbo/communication/class_1881.as` since the primary tree's registry file is fully obfuscated) and the matching `HabboCatalog.onVoucherRedeemOk()`/`onVoucherRedeemError()`/`alertDialogEventProcessor()` handlers. `alertDialogEventProcessor()`'s AS3 counterpart also calls `resetPlacedOfferData()`, which depends on the unported `CatalogObjectMover`/`PlacedObjectPurchaseData` (Phase 4) — added as a real but currently-no-op method (matches AS3 exactly today, since nothing sets placed-offer state yet) rather than inlining or omitting it.
  - **`SpacesNewCatalogWidget`**: subclasses `ItemGridCatalogWidget`, adding a wall/floor/landscape category selector that drives the "spaces" room preview (new `CatalogWidgetUpdateRoomPreviewEvent`). **TS-only deviation, documented in-code**: AS3 restarts a persistent `flash.utils.Timer` after a category switch whose fire-closure is permanently bound to the *first* (empty) `populateItemGrid()` call from `init()` — meaning in the original client, only the first 3 offers of any category with more than 3 items ever get their icons loaded (confirmed by reading `ItemGridCatalogWidget.as`'s `init()`/`loadItemGridGraphics()` closures directly, not decompiler corruption). This port's timer is a plain `setInterval` created fresh wherever needed (an architecture decision already made when `ItemGridCatalogWidget.ts` was ported, predating this session), so `switchCategory()` starts a fresh interval over the *current* category's offers instead of reproducing the stale-closure quirk.
  - **`BundlePurchaseExtraInfoWidget` + `bundlepurchaseinfodisplay/`** (9 AS3 files, all new except `ExtraInfoItemData.ts` which already existed): `ExtraInfoViewManager` (implements the engine's existing `IUpdateReceiver`/`registerUpdateReceiver()` rather than a bespoke AS3-style update hook), `ExtraInfoListItem`/`UpdateableExtraInfoListItem` base classes, and the 5 concrete row types (`ExtraInfoPromoItem`, `ExtraInfoBundlesInfoItem`, `ExtraInfoDiscountValueItem`, `ExtraInfoBonusBadgeItem`/`ExtraInfoBonusAchievementItem` — the last two are inert stubs matching AS3, since nothing in this widget ever constructs those two item types). `ExtraInfoItemData.ts` was missing its `TYPE_BUNDLES_INFO_SCREEN` constant despite it being recoverable in both the primary and secondary trees — added. Two of its six type constants (values 2 and 4) are unrecoverable by name in all three source trees; named `TYPE_DISCOUNT_VALUE`/`TYPE_BONUS_ACHIEVEMENT` (TS-only names, not invented — derived unambiguously from which concrete list-item class `ExtraInfoViewManager.as::addItem()`'s switch constructs for each value). Added `HabboCatalogUtils.spinnerValueChangedEventTrack()`/`bundlesInfoShownEventTrack()`/`discountShownEventTrack()` (delegating to the already-ported `HabboTracking.trackEventLogOncePerSession()` instead of duplicating AS3's hand-rolled per-method boolean flags — behaviorally identical since one `HabboCatalogUtils` lives exactly as long as one session) and `bundleDiscountHighestFlatPriceStep` (same not-yet-parsed-config-message `TODO(AS3)` as the existing `bundleDiscountFlatPriceSteps` stub). Wired a new `IID_HabboTracking` dependency into `HabboCatalog.ts` (existing DI pattern, same as `HabboHelp`/`HabboNavigator`) so `HabboCatalogUtils` can reach it. **Documented null-guard deviation**: `onSpinnerEvent()`'s AS3 condition dereferences `catalog.bundleDiscountRuleset.bundleSize`, but `bundleDiscountRuleset` is a pre-existing always-null stub on this port — null-guarded instead of literally ported, using the same cast convention `ProductViewCatalogWidget.ts::setSpinnerToBundleRuleset()` already established for this same stub.
- 🟢 **Phase 2 complete: Habbo Club/VIP purchase** (`habbo/catalog/club/`, 12 new files + 5 new catalog widgets), taking `CatalogPage.createWidget()` from 23 to 28 of ~45 AS3 cases. Confirmed via `docs/CLIENT-SERVER-ARCHITECTURE.md` §9 that generic catalog purchasing (`CatalogPurchaseGrain`) is "genuinely solid" server-side and already resolves club level/discount via `IPlayerGrain.GetClubSubscriptionAsync` — no new server-side risk identified for this phase.
  - **Controllers**: `ClubBuyController` (buy HC/VIP days, requests offers via new `GetClubOffersMessageComposer`/`HabboClubOffersMessageEvent`, header 2965/2426), `ClubExtendController` (renew-before-expiry upsell, new `HabboClubExtendOfferMessageEvent`/header 3509 + `PurchaseVipMembershipExtensionComposer`/`PurchaseBasicMembershipExtensionComposer`, headers 415/3352), `ClubGiftController` (gift Club to a friend — reuses the already-ported `GetClubGiftMessageComposer`/`ClubGiftInfoEvent` pair from the earlier ClubCenter work instead of new protocol, only needed one new composer, `SelectClubGiftComposer`, header 227). All three wired into `HabboCatalog.init()` (`createClubBuyController()`/`createClubExtendController()`/`createClubGiftController()`, previously a documented `TODO(AS3)` gap) alongside `getClubBuyController()`/`getClubExtendController()`/`getClubGiftController()`, `purchaseProduct()`, `purchaseVipMembershipExtension()`/`purchaseBasicMembershipExtension()`, and `interpolate()`/`updateUrlProtocol()` (HabboCatalog-as-config-accessor methods `Component` didn't already expose).
  - **New wire-protocol wrinkle found via cross-referencing three source trees**: `HabboClubOffersMessageEventParser`'s secondary-tree copy (`class_2374.as`) has a genuine infinite-loop bug (`while(0 < count)`, never incremented/compared) — the primary tree's copy is correct (`while(i < count) { ...; i++; }`) and was used as the actual source of truth, per the documented tree-priority order.
  - **`ClubBuyOfferData`** (implements the existing `IPurchasableOffer` interface, confirmed member-for-member) is constructed directly by the parser via a new `fromWrapper()` static factory, skipping AS3's unnamed intermediate wire-DTO class (unrecoverable in all three trees). `ClubExtendOfferData` extends it with 4 more fields; ported its `discountActivityPointAmount` getter's real bug faithfully (multiplies by `months` twice, confirmed identical in the primary and secondary trees, unlike the sibling `discountCreditAmount` getter which only multiplies once).
  - **Confirmation dialogs**: `ClubBuyConfirmationDialog` (real but currently unreachable — confirmed via grep that neither `ClubBuyItem` nor `VipBuyItem`, the only two "buy" buttons in the live widget flow, ever call `ClubBuyController.showConfirmation()`; both route through the generic `HabboCatalog.showPurchaseConfirmation()`/`PurchaseConfirmationDialog` instead, matching this session's `MadMoneyCatalogWidget`-style "port it anyway, it's genuinely dead in the original client too" precedent), `ClubExtendConfirmationDialog` (real renewal upsell UI: price breakdown, animated credit-icon flourish, remote teaser image via the already-ported `loadAssetFromFile`/`AssetLoaderEvent` pipeline), `ClubGiftConfirmationDialog`. All three widgets' raw-XML-asset + `buildFromXML()` window-construction pattern (`assets.getAssetByName(name) as XmlAsset` + `windowManager.buildFromXML(xml)`) was replaced with the already-established `catalog.utils.createWindow(name)` compiled-layout-registry call, per the same substitution this port already uses elsewhere for one-off XML templates (see `ColourGridCatalogWidget`'s port notes) — not a new deviation, an existing one applied consistently.
  - **Widgets**: `ClubBuyCatalogWidget`, `VipBuyCatalogWidget` (also handles VIP gifting via an `isGift` constructor flag — reused directly for both `vipBuyWidget` and `vipGiftWidget`, matching AS3), `LoyaltyVipBuyCatalogWidget` (near-identical to `VipBuyCatalogWidget`, differs only in offer-request source), `ClubGiftWidget` (converts the raw `ClubOfferData[]` from `ClubGiftController` into real `Offer`/`Product` instances before display, matching AS3's own `updateList()`). Two small pre-existing `ITextWindow` gaps surfaced and were left as documented `TODO(AS3)` stubs rather than expanded: no `align`/`leading` fields on the per-range text-format type (`fixFormatting()`), and no `StyleSheet`-equivalent for CSS-in-caption links (`setLinkStyle()`, same gap `LocalizationCatalogWidget.ts` already documented). `ClubGiftWidget`'s hover-preview (`showPreview()`) needed `RoomEngine.getFurnitureImage()`/`getWallItemImage()` — Phase 5 scope at the time this was written, now ported (see Phase 5 entry below).
- 🟢 **Phase 3 complete: Marketplace** (`habbo/catalog/marketplace/`, 10 new files + 2 new catalog widgets + a full new wire-protocol module), taking `CatalogPage.createWidget()` from 28 to 30 of ~45 AS3 cases.
  - **Core utility gap found and filled first**: `MarketPlaceLogic`/both widgets all depend on AS3's `com.sulake.core.utils.Map` (an insertion-ordered, index-addressable key/value `Proxy` — real name confirmed via `PRODUCTION-201601012205-226667486/src/com/sulake/core/utils/Map.as`, whose own `callProperty()` literally returns `"Map"` for `toString()`), which had never been ported despite being used engine-wide in AS3. Added `core/utils/OrderedMap.ts` (renamed to avoid shadowing the built-in `Map`) implementing the full API (`add`/`remove`/`getValue`/`getWithIndex`/`getKey`/`getKeys`/`hasKey`/`indexOf`/`concatenate`/`clone`/`reset`/`dispose`, plus `values()`/`Symbol.iterator` standing in for AS3's `for each`/Proxy iteration).
  - **Wire protocol** (`communication/messages/{outgoing,incoming,parser}/marketplace/`): 9 composers, 8 events, 8 parsers. Two composer/event pairs (`CancelAllMarketplaceOffersMessageComposer`/`MarketplaceCancelAllOffersResultEvent`, headers 831/921; `ClearOwnMarketplaceHistoryMessageComposer`/`MarketplaceClearOwnHistoryResultEvent`, headers 1242/1760) are genuinely absent from `win63_version`/`PRODUCTION-201601012205-226667486` entirely — newer features only in the primary tree, headers confirmed directly from its registry (`_SafeCls_2046.as`), TS-derived names since no readable-name tree has them. `CancelMarketplaceOfferMessageComposer` (header 2096) is what `redeemExpiredMarketPlaceOffer()` actually sends — client-facing wording is "redeem" but the wire-level name (recovered from `win63_version`) is "cancel".
  - **Decompiler corruption found and worked around**: both `MarketPlaceOffersEventParser` and `MarketPlaceOwnOffersEventParser` are corrupted in `win63_version` in the same distinctive way already seen once this session for `HabboClubOffersMessageEventParser` — non-terminating loop condition, discarded reads, and `_offers.push(null)` instead of the constructed object. The primary tree's copies (`_SafeCls_2885`/`_SafeCls_3542`) are complete and were used as the real source; field-by-field diffing them also revealed the two parsers read genuinely different shapes (public offers read `timeLeftMinutes`/`averagePrice`/`offerCount` per-entry and a trailing `totalItemsFound`, with an `isUsable`/`isUsed` variant via furniType 4; own offers read a leading `creditsWaiting` once, no offer count, and a conditional `statusTime` `readLong()` only when status is SOLD/EXPIRED) — preserved as two distinct parsers rather than unified.
  - **Two-class raw-DTO split preserved from AS3**: the wire parsers construct an obfuscated, unrecoverable-by-name raw entry class (`_SafeCls_2516`/`class_3020`/`_Str_4192` — a decompiler placeholder in all three trees; ported as `MarketPlaceOfferEntry`, TS-derived name) which `MarketPlaceLogic.onOffers()`/`onOwnOffers()` then re-project field-by-field into the real domain class `MarketPlaceOfferData` (implements `IMarketPlaceOfferData`) — a deliberate AS3 architectural split, not collapsed into one class per the "never simplify AS3 architecture" rule.
  - **Two small supporting data classes recovered by name from `PRODUCTION-201601012205-226667486`** (both obfuscated in the two newer trees): `MarketplaceItemStats` (price/volume history for a furni type; its `offerCount` accessor is named from the real private field `_currentOfferCount`, not the ambiguous getter name) and `MarketPlaceOfferState` (an `ONGOING`/`SOLD`/`EXPIRED` constant set with a genuine duplicate-value quirk — two differently-named constants both `= 1`, confirmed identical across all three trees — that is unreferenced anywhere in the current client; ported for completeness, not wired to anything, matching its real orphaned status). `MarketPlaceOfferStatus` (`OPEN`/`SOLD`/`EXPIRED` + `isClearable()`, gating `clearOwnHistory()`) has no readable-name counterpart in any tree at all - TS-derived from usage.
  - **Pre-existing header collision found and fixed**: `HabboMessages.ts` had `LatencyPingRequestMessageComposer` registered at header 1242 (carried over from `win63_version`'s registry), but the primary tree's registry (`_SafeCls_2046.as`) reassigns 1242 to `ClearOwnMarketplaceHistoryMessageComposer` and registers the real latency-ping composer (`_SafeCls_2653`, same single-int-param shape, confirmed by reading the class directly) at 544 instead — moved to 544 to resolve the collision, since the primary tree is this port's protocol source of truth.
  - **`MarketplaceChart`** (price/volume line chart bitmap): AS3 draws synchronously into a `BitmapData` via `Shape`/`TextField`/`Matrix`; ported to `OffscreenCanvas` + `createImageBitmap()`, which makes `draw()` return `Promise<ImageBitmap>` instead of a synchronous value — the one unavoidable signature change, documented in-code, since browsers have no synchronous canvas→`ImageBitmap` path (same constraint already documented on `ImageResult.ts`).
  - **`MarketplaceConfirmationDialog`**: reuses the already-ported `IRoomEngine.getFurnitureIcon()`/`getWallItemIcon()` + `IGetImageListener` async-icon pattern (established by `ProductImageWidget.ts`) rather than needing a new Phase 5 stub, and `HabboCatalogUtils.replaceCenteredImage()` in place of AS3's manual `BitmapData.copyPixels()` centering (same pre-existing `ImageBitmap`-is-immutable substitution used throughout the catalog module).
  - **Widgets**: `MarketPlaceCatalogWidget` (search/browse/buy, with a `setInterval`-based list population batching 5 items per 25ms tick, and a details panel with a price-history/trade-volume chart selector) and `MarketPlaceOwnItemsCatalogWidget` (ongoing/sold/expired "my offers" tabs, with its own AS3-faithful per-status window-recycling pool capped at 2000 entries per status, and local substring search over localized name+description). Both wired into `HabboCatalog.getMarketPlace()` (lazily created in `init()` alongside the Phase 2 club controllers) and the 8 new `onMarketPlace*`/`onMarketplace*` event handlers registered in `initComponent()`.
- 🟢 **Phase 4 complete: Recycler + `CatalogObjectMover`** (`habbo/catalog/recycler/`, 9 new files + `CatalogObjectMover.ts` + 2 new catalog widgets + a full new wire-protocol module), taking `CatalogPage.createWidget()` from 30 to 32 of ~45 AS3 cases. Confirmed via a direct read of `vortex-emulator`'s codebase that the server has **zero** Recycler implementation (no `Turbo.PacketHandlers` handler, no message classes, no grain logic) — ported client-first anyway per explicit direction, so the feature is wire-complete and will start working the moment server support exists, but cannot be exercised end-to-end today.
  - **Wire protocol** (`communication/messages/{outgoing,incoming,parser}/catalog/`): 3 composers (`GetRecyclerStatusMessageComposer`/`GetRecyclerPrizesMessageComposer`, both empty-body; `RecycleItemsMessageComposer`, an item-id-array send), 3 events (`RecyclerStatusMessageEvent`/`RecyclerFinishedMessageEvent`/`RecyclerPrizesMessageEvent`) + parsers, plus 3 supporting prize-table data classes (`PrizeLevelMessageData`/`PrizeMessageData`/`PrizeMessageSubProduct`, one `PrizeLevelMessageData` per star tier). Headers (1246/2516/2956 outgoing; 1919/281/3783 incoming) came directly from `vortex-emulator/Turbo.Revisions/Revision20260701/Headers.cs`, already realigned against this same primary AS3 tree earlier in this session — cross-checked one pair (`MarketPlaceOffersComposer`/`MarketPlaceOffersEvent`, header 2442) against `HabboMessages.ts`'s existing Marketplace registration to confirm both sides of this port target the identical protocol baseline before trusting the new numbers.
  - **Decompiler corruption found again, same pattern as Phase 2/3**: `PrizeLevelMessageData`'s and `PrizeMessageData`'s deal-bundle sub-product loops are both `while(0 < count)` with an unrelated counter incremented inside (never terminates as literally written) — ported as bounded `for` loops per the now-established precedent for this exact corruption shape.
  - **`RecyclerLogic`** (state machine: local status off/ready/waiting-for-server × server status off/ready/waiting, slot pool sized from config `recycler.number_of_slots` default 5, ducket cost from `recycler.ducket_cost`, timeout from `recycler.timeout_seconds`, cached prize table with a pending-callback queue for `getPrizeTable()`) ported member-for-member against `IRecycler`/`IRecyclerVisualization`, including the quirky `setNextRecycleAllowedTimestamp()`/`secondsToWait()` interplay (`_systemStatus` gets force-set to `3` as a side effect of the timestamp setter, called both internally and externally from the widget's animation-complete handler) — preserved exactly as AS3 has it rather than "cleaned up".
  - **Prize-table class family**: `FurniSlotItem` (slot contents), `PrizeGridItem`→`PrizeContainer`→`DealPrizeContainer` (single-product vs. bundle-deal prize display, sharing icon-resolution/grid-item plumbing with the existing `ProductGridItem`/`IGridItem` base already used by regular catalog grids), `PrizeLevelContainer` (one per star tier, builds the right container subclass per prize), `RecycleRewardDisplayWrapper` (adapts a prize to the generic `IProductDisplayInfo` product-preview widget). The `"chat_style"` prize-icon branch (`PrizeGridItem.initProductIcon()`) needs `HabboCatalog.freeFlowChat.chatStyleLibrary`, which isn't exposed on `HabboCatalog` yet — same pre-existing gap `HabboCatalogUtils.showExtraOnProduct()` already documents; left as a logged, honest no-op rather than invented.
  - **`CatalogObjectMover`** (drag-ghost-icon while placing a purchased/moved/returned-from-recycler furni item): confirmed by reading it fully that it sends no network messages of its own and only needs `IRoomEngine.getFurnitureIcon()`/`getWallItemIcon()` (already ported) for the icon bitmap. **One AS3 mechanism deliberately not reproduced 1:1**: AS3 mounts its overlay `Sprite` onto `mainContainer.desktop.getDisplayObject()` (the window system's own root display object); this port instead calls the *already-existing* `IRoomEngine.addStageChild()`/`removeStageChild()` (built for `RoomEngine.ts`'s own in-room object-mover icon sprite) — same "mount directly on the PixiJS stage, above everything" outcome, reusing established engine plumbing instead of threading a new `getDisplayObject()`-equivalent through the window-system layer. `mainContainer` is still a real public setter (AS3 has one) but unused internally as a result.
  - **Two small, well-scoped `IRoomEngine` additions, not full features**: (1) `ISelectedRoomObjectData.ts` ported as a faithful data-shape interface (confirmed by reading the real, non-obfuscated AS3 file) plus `getSelectedObjectData(roomId)` — but the concrete implementation (obfuscated `class_34.as`, covering full room-object selection for both placement *and* already-placed objects being moved/inspected) is a much larger, unrelated subsystem that was out of scope here, so this always returns `null` (an honest `TODO(AS3)` stub; every caller — `CatalogObjectMover`, `RecyclerCatalogWidget`'s slot click handler — already null-checks it, so slot clicks currently always behave as "release slot" rather than "place item" until this is filled in). (2) `setObjectMoverIconSpriteVisible()`/`getObjectMoverIconSpriteVisible()` ported **for real**, not stubbed — `RoomEngine.ts` already tracks a `_moverIconSprite` for its own in-room placement-icon flow, so these just toggle/read its existing `.visible`.
  - **`HabboCatalog` wiring**: real `_recycler: RecyclerLogic` field + `createRecycler()` (called from `init()` alongside the Phase 2/3 controllers) + `getRecycler()` (was a `null`-returning stub), `getRecyclerStatus()`/`getRecyclerPrizes()`/`sendRecycleItems()` now send real composers, 3 new `onRecycler*` handlers registered in `initComponent()`. `setupInventoryForRecycler()`/`requestInventoryFurniToRecycler()`/`returnInventoryFurniFromRecycler()` remain honest no-op/zero/`false` stubs — AS3 delegates these to `HabboInventory`'s own `RecyclerModel` (a purely local inventory-side furni-locking helper, confirmed via a full read — it sends no network messages either), and `HabboInventory` isn't wired into `HabboCatalog` at all yet. Also refreshed 4 pre-existing stale `TODO(AS3)` comments elsewhere in `HabboCatalog.ts`/`CatalogPage.ts` that said "CatalogObjectMover isn't ported" — it now exists, but `requestSelectedItemToMover()`/`cancelFurniInMover()`/`resetPlacedOfferData()` (the purchase-flow call sites that would each need to own a `CatalogObjectMover` instance) are separate, still-open wiring gaps, not re-closed by this phase.
  - **Widgets**: `RecyclerCatalogWidget` (slot grid, ducket-cost display, recycle-button state machine, abort flow) and `RecyclerPrizesCatalogWidget` (browsable star-tier prize grids, reusing `gridItem`/`recyclerPrizesWidgetLevelItem` compiled layouts already present in the JSON registry) — plus their two small supporting helpers, `RecyclerEngineAnimator` (the spinning-arrow/shake animation state machine, `setInterval`-based in place of `flash.utils.Timer`) and `FrankRecyclerEmotion` (floating pat-Frank blush/heart bubbles with a static reuse pool; one AS3 branch — nulling `_bitmap` then reading it again in the same tick — is a decompiler control-flow artifact, ported as an early `return` instead of reproducing the guaranteed crash).

Registered as-is (matching AS3), header 2913's emulator-side mismatch left as a note for a future `vortex-emulator`-side fix, not touched here. **Follow-up, same session**: user confirmed `vortex-emulator` does have `WiredPermissionsComposer` registered at 3483 too (both headers exist server-side) — added `WiredPermissionsEvent`/`WiredPermissionsEventParser` (3483, `canModify`/`canRead`) under `messages/{incoming,parser}/userdefinedroomevents/` (flat, matching the existing `WiredRewardResultMessageEvent` placement rather than nesting AS3's `wiredmenu` subpackage) — name recovered via `sources/win63_version`'s readable copy, exact field match against the primary tree's `_SafeCls_3768`(event, `src/unknowns/_SafePkg_2538/`)/`_SafeCls_2783`(parser, real path) pair.

Traced the real consumer for both room messages to `sources/win63_version/habbo/room/class_1788.as` (confirmed to be `RoomEngine`'s own `RoomMessageHandler`-equivalent, matching this port's `RoomMessageHandler.ts` 1:1 by its `addMessageEvent()` subscription list) — its `onRoomProperty()`/`onRoomVisualizationSettings()` call straight through to `RoomEngine.updateObjectRoom()`/`updateObjectRoomVisibilities()`/`updateObjectRoomPlaneThicknesses()` (`class_34.as`, confirmed real `RoomEngine`), which just dispatch the already-fully-ported `RoomObjectRoomUpdateMessage`/`RoomObjectRoomPlaneVisibilityUpdateMessage`/`RoomObjectRoomPlanePropertyUpdateMessage` internal messages that `RoomLogic.ts` already listens for and applies to the room object's model (`RoomVisualization.ts` already reads `room_floor_type`/`room_wall_type`/`room_landscape_type`/thickness-multiplier model vars every update tick) — this whole internal pipeline existed and was fully working, just never fed by a live network message. Verified samples: 264/1131/834 are genuinely absent from `HabboMessages.ts` and genuinely subscribed in AS3.

Visible symptoms: dice show no value, furni states never update, one-way gates inert, wired area-hide inert, guide sessions dead, avatar effects never triggered (matches the standing "trigger deferred" note).
    - **✅ CLOSED (2026-07-24): 16/16 fixed**, plus 6 more found by re-measuring. Commits: `689f54a` 264 `onDiceValue` · `7d46764` 1778/254/2361 · `0de4ec0` the wall-item trio 834/1787/540 · `7824586` 1131 `onAreaHide` · `63fc51c` 325 `onWiredMovements` · `5316023` 2624 `onAvatarEffect` + 3649/2126/2377 `onGuideSession*` · `22c3e1a` 3643/2458 confirm dialogs · `0df631e` 536 `onSpecialRoomEvent` · `f2dc29d` the 6 orphaned subscriptions below.
      - **The shape of the bug class, confirmed 16 times: the pipeline exists, nothing feeds it.** In almost every case the engine and logic side was already ported and correct; only the parser+event+registration+subscription were missing.
      - **2624 `onAvatarEffect` was not a missing port at all — the header was wrong.** Parser, event, subscription and handler all existed and matched AS3 exactly; the port registered them on 3629, which the registry maps to a different message (`_SafeCls_3136` → parser `_SafeCls_4142`, a single `type` int, consumed by `AvatarEditorMessageHandler.onAvatarEffectSelected`). The room-side header is 2624. That one number is why in-room avatar effects never appeared despite the whole render pipeline being ported — it also retires the standing "trigger deferred" note.
      - **The 325 wall-item branch was NOT blocked on `getLegacyGeometry()`** as previously recorded: this port keeps its `LegacyWallGeometry` on `RoomMessageHandler`, initialised from the heightmap, and already used it for every other wall-item placement.

The re-measurement surfaced **6 further mode-2 orphans** that were fully ported and registered but had no subscriber at all — fixed in `f2dc29d`: 946 `onPetExperience` · 3796 `onPetFigureUpdate` · 1600 `onPlayingGame` · 412 `onYouAreNotSpectator` · 1052 `onGamePlayerNumberValue` · 2942 `onConfigurationItemStates`. `HanditemConfigurationMessageParser` was also reading only 1 of its 4 booleans, silently dropping three room toggles at the wire.

  - **Follow-up, same session — "0" treated as "no override"**: once 1956 was actually wired, the user's room (floor/wall paint never customized) started rendering a real, wrong-looking motif instead of the classic default. Root cause confirmed by reading `vortex-emulator/Turbo.Rooms/Grains/RoomGrain.cs` (lines ~317-326): the server sends `RoomPropertyMessageComposer{Key, Value=entity.PaintFloor/PaintWall/PaintLandscape.ToString()}` straight from the DB columns, whose C# default/unset value is `0` - the emulator never omits the property or substitutes the real default motif ids for an unset room, and AS3's own `updateObjectRoom()` has no `"0"` special-case to fall back on (confirmed by reading it directly), so a real client talking to this same server would show the identical wrong motif. This is a `vortex-emulator` data/protocol quirk, not an AS3 behavior - documented as such (not tagged `AS3:`) via a new `RoomMessageHandler.normalizeRoomPropertyValue()` that maps `"0"` to `null` before calling `updateObjectRoom()`, so the pre-existing `initializeRoom()` default motifs (`'111'`/`'201'`/`'1'`) stay in effect until a real (non-`"0"`) value arrives.
- 🟢 **Phase 5 complete: offscreen furniture/room image rendering** (`RoomEngine.getFurnitureImage()`/`getWallItemImage()`/`getRoomImage()`/`getPetImage()`/`getRoomObjectImage()`/`getGenericRoomObjectImage()` + `IRoomObjectVisualization.getImage()`), unblocking the 3 widgets that were waiting on it: `ProductImageWidget` (wall/floor item full-size preview), `ClubGiftWidget` (gift-offer hover preview), `ProductViewCatalogWidget` (no-room-canvas fallback path — the room-canvas path itself was already working).
  - **Core finding that reframed the whole phase**: the roadmap's "0% ported" note undersold how much was already there. `RoomPreviewer.ts` (973 lines, live on-screen furniture/avatar preview canvas) was already advanced and unrelated to this gap — the real missing piece was a completely different mechanism: `RoomEngine.getGenericRoomObjectImage()`, which spins up a *hidden* room object (no canvas) for a single forced render pass, then tears it down. Most of its supporting primitives already existed and needed no changes: `IRoomManager.createRoom()`/`getRoom()` (raw string-keyed rooms, bypassing the numeric-roomId wrapper - AS3 does the same for its own `"temporary_room"`), `IRoomInstance.createRoomObject()`/`disposeObject()`, `RoomContentLoader.getObjectCategory()`/`isContentAvailable()`, `RoomGeometry`, and critically `RoomEngine.contentLoaded()` (already existed for the icon/thumbnail path - extended, not replaced, to also resolve pending "image" listeners the same way AS3's `contentLoaded()` does).
  - **The one genuinely new piece**: `IRoomObjectVisualization.getImage()`/`get image()` (declared in the base interface in AS3, not just the sprite-specific one) didn't exist in this port at all. AS3 composites via `BitmapData.draw()` (a software rasterizer needing no live renderer); PixiJS needs an actual `Renderer` to rasterize, so this builds a throwaway `Container` of plain `Sprite`s per AS3's `IRoomObjectSprite` field (position/tint/alpha/blendMode/flip mirroring AS3's `Matrix`+`ColorTransform` math exactly, sorted by `relativeDepth` descending) and reads it back via `Vortex.instance.application.renderer.extract.canvas()` - the same synchronous texture-to-canvas mechanism `RoomEngine.pixiTextureToCanvas()` already used elsewhere, applied to a `Container` instead of a single `Texture`.
  - **`extractDarknessToAlpha()` ported in full** (the additive-glow-on-transparent-background edge case, user-confirmed in scope rather than deferred): AS3's per-pixel `BitmapData.getVector()`/`setVector()` HSL loop, ported as a synchronous 2D-canvas `getImageData()`/`putImageData()` pass using the already-ported `ColorConverter.rgbToHSL()`/`hslToRGB()` (confirmed identical H/S/L bit-packing convention by reading both).
