  - **TS deviation, matches this port's established `ImageResult` convention**: AS3 populates `ImageResult.data` synchronously when content is already loaded (id=0) and only defers via a listener when it isn't. `ImageBitmap` conversion is always async in the browser (documented on `ImageResult.ts` since Phase 1), so this always resolves through the `id>0` pending path and delivers via `imageReady()`/`imageFailed()` - same convention `getGenericRoomObjectThumbnail()` (the icon path) already established. One real behavioral difference from a literal port: in the "content not loaded yet" branch, AS3 still captures and stores a (necessarily blank) image on the result before registering the listener; this port skips that blank capture entirely since nothing may ever read it synchronously anyway.
  - **Two lower-priority AS3 members ported with an honest, narrower gap** rather than skipped: `getRoomObjectImage()` (queries an already-placed room object's own furniture_color/extras/figure) has a `TODO(AS3)` for the `furniture_data_format != 0` branch, which needs a stuff-data-wrapper factory (AS3 `_SafeCls_2295.getStuffDataWrapperForType()`) that isn't ported. `initializeRoomForGettingImage()` (backs `getRoomImage()`'s `type="room"` case) wires the real floor/wall/landscape model strings and door mask, but the tile-height/geometry init is a `TODO(AS3)` - the `RoomVisualization` event handler's `initialize()` data-shape contract for a standalone "room" object isn't confirmed on this port (`RoomPreviewer`'s own room setup goes through a different, higher-level `IRoomEngine.initializeRoom()` path that doesn't apply here). Neither gap blocks any of the 3 widgets this phase was scoped to unblock - `getRoomImage()` itself is used by later, not-yet-started phases (room-ad/guild previews).
  - **`ProductCategoryMapping.categoryMapping()`** (already ported) turned out to gate `ProductImageWidget`'s wall-item case in AS3 too - only wall items where `categoryMapping("I", classId) === 1` get a full-image preview; others fall through to `clearPreviewer()`, matching AS3 exactly rather than an invented simplification.
  - **`ClubGiftWidget`'s hover preview needed one adaptation AS3 doesn't have**: AS3's `showPreview()` passes a `null` listener and reads `ImageResult.data` synchronously (safe in AS3 since content is normally already loaded by hover time). Since this port never trusts synchronous `ImageResult.data` (see above), `ClubGiftWidget` now implements `IGetImageListener` and delivers the preview through `imageReady()`, tracked by a pending-request id - same shape as `ProductImageWidget`'s already-established `_pendingImageId` pattern, applied here for the first time in this widget.
- 🟢 **Floor-furniture placement/move ghost-object pipeline ported for real, plus 4 unrelated pre-existing bugs found and fixed while chasing user bug reports** (rotate a piece of furniture: server round-trip was correct but nothing visually changed; drag-move showed a preview sitting at the wrong height; placing anything from inventory silently did nothing). What started as a rotation-animation report cascaded into a full audit of the client/server furniture lifecycle; all fixes below are confirmed against the real AS3, not invented workarounds.
  - **Real ghost-object placement** (`RoomEngine.ts::initializeRoomObjectInsert()`/`handleObjectPlace()`/`placeObject()`, matching `_SafeCls_1821.as`'s real method names recovered from the primary tree): placing an item from inventory now spawns an actual semi-transparent room object that snaps tile-by-tile via `handleFurnitureMove()`/`validateFurnitureLocation()` against a real `FurniStackingHeightMap` (now built from the live `HeightMapMessage`/`HeightMapUpdateMessage`, previously unused dead code), falling back to the old flat icon only when no valid tile is hovered — same fallback behavior AS3 itself has. `OBJECT_MOVE` (dragging an already-placed item) was unified onto the same `SelectedRoomObjectData`/`handleObjectMove()`/`confirmObjectMove()` pipeline, replacing a separate simplified `_pendingMove` mechanism that ignored stacking height entirely. Wall-item placement is still out of scope: AS3 runs it through the identical pipeline (`handleWallItemMove()`/`validateWallItemLocation()`), but finalizing it needs `PlaceObjectMessageComposer`'s `wallLocation` string encode (world position → `"w=x,y l=x,y r|l"`), and no AS3 source here has the inverse of `LegacyWallGeometry.getLocation()` to port that encode from — left rejected rather than guessed.
  - **Rotating an already-placed item now validates like AS3** (`getValidRoomObjectDirection()`, already-ported but unused, plus a new `validateFurnitureDirection()`): previously cycled blindly through 4 cardinal directions with no stacking check; now respects `furniture_allowed_directions` and rejects a rotation that wouldn't fit.
  - **`TileObjectMap` wired in** (`RoomEngine.getTileObjectMap()`/`refreshTileObjectMap()`, `RoomInstanceData`'s real AS3 setter behavior reproduced: assigning a new `FurniStackingHeightMap` also rebuilds the paired `TileObjectMap` at the same dimensions): was a fully-written, fully-unused utility class since some earlier phase.
  - **Root cause #1 — `RoomManager.ts::createRoomObject()`/its content-loaded re-init path both called `logic.initialize(null)`** instead of the already-fetched `visualizationConfig`, so `FurnitureLogic`'s `furniture_size_x/y/z`, `furniture_center_x/y/z`, and `furniture_allowed_directions` were never populated for *any* furniture — the stacking-map validation above was silently defaulting every item to a 1×1 footprint with no rotation restriction data at all.
  - **Root cause #2 — fixing #1 exposed a second bug in `FurnitureLogic.ts::initialize()`**: it read `data.model.dimensions`/`data.model.directions.direction` (an object list), but the real compiled bundle nests the furniture's logic config one level deeper (`data.logic.model...`, sibling to `assets`/`spritesheet`/`visualizations`) and stores `directions` as a flat array of raw index numbers, not `{id}` objects. Fixed both the nesting path and the entry shape (defensively accepting either shape for the latter, matching an existing quirk-handling pattern already used elsewhere in `RoomContentLoader.ts` for singular-vs-array XML→JSON collapsing).
  - **Root cause #3, the one that actually explained the placeholder-forever symptom — `RoomEngine.ts::addRoomObjectFurniture()`/`addRoomObjectWallItem()` passed a pre-resolved `logicType` (e.g. `"furniture_multistate"`) into `room.createRoomObject(id, type, category)`'s `type` parameter**, instead of the real furniture classname. AS3's `createObjectFurniture()`/`createObject()` (`_SafeCls_90.as`) always passes the real classname and lets the room-object-creation path resolve `visualizationType`/`logicType` itself; passing an already-resolved, generic logic-type string made `loadObjectContent()` try to fetch assets for a type that doesn't exist (empty URL list), so the object could never leave placeholder content — not just missing rotation data, but permanently placeholder-only for its whole lifetime.
  - **Root cause #4 — even after #1–#3, a `RoomObjectModel` "immutable" write-once flag (confirmed identical in real AS3's own `setNumber()`/`setNumberArray()`: `if (protect) firstWriteWins` — this is faithful AS3 behavior, not a porting bug) meant the *first* `logic.initialize()` call (still fired at creation time with placeholder data) permanently locked `furniture_allowed_directions`/size fields before the real content's re-init call could ever take effect.** Fixed by gating `RoomManager.createRoomObject()`'s `logic.initialize()` call on `initialized` (real content already available) — when using placeholder content, logic is left uninitialized at creation time and gets its one real `initialize()` call from the content-loaded re-init path instead, matching the effect of AS3's own conditional skip (real AS3's placeholder bundle apparently carries no logic XML at all, so it never reaches this call in the first place; this port's placeholder bundle does have one, generic and wrong, so the fix reproduces AS3's *outcome* via the information already available here rather than inventing a new condition).
  - **No visual change on rotate, once the direction data above was correct, traced to a 5th and final gap**: `RoomEngine.ts::updateObjectFurniture()` called `object.setLocation()`/`setDirection()` directly instead of dispatching a `RoomObjectUpdateMessage`/`RoomObjectDataUpdateMessage` through `object.getEventHandler()?.processUpdateMessage()` like AS3's real `updateObjectFurniture()` (`_SafeCls_90.as`) does — bypassing `FurnitureLogic`'s existing-but-dead rotation-bounce mechanism (`_bounceStep`/`BOUNCE_STEPS`/`getLocationOffset()`, already fully implemented, never reachable). Rewired to dispatch through the event handler (also added the missing `RoomEngineObjectEvent.REOE_UPDATED` constant, recovered from the primary tree's `_SafeStr_10306`). Once reachable, the bounce animation ran ~2–3x too fast: AS3's `_bounceStep` also has no internal throttle, relying on the whole engine ticking at a fixed, much lower Flash framerate; this port's logic layer runs at raw `requestAnimationFrame` rate with no equivalent throttle (unlike `FurnitureVisualization.ts`, which already self-throttles to ~24fps/41ms for the same reason) — added the same 41ms throttle to `FurnitureLogic`'s bounce-step advance.
- 🟢 **Vortex-custom protocol messages wired: rentable space config + pet vocal/command/move** — 6 new message classes (not part of any official AS3 dump; source is the sibling `vortex-client` AS3/Flash project that pairs with the real `vortex-emulator`, confirmed via its git history: commits `f3bba54`/`d6bc0d0`/`e8dc43d`). `GetRentableSpaceConfigMessageComposer`/`ConfigureRentableSpaceMessageComposer` (outgoing, headers 4600/4601) + `RentableSpaceConfigMessageEvent`/`RentableSpaceConfigMessageEventParser` (incoming, header 4600 — same number as the outgoing side is not a collision, they're different wire tables) under `messages/{outgoing,incoming,parser}/room/furniture/`. `IssuePetCommandMessageComposer`/`MovePetMessageComposer` (outgoing, headers 3072/2761) + `PetVocalMessageEvent`/`PetVocalMessageEventParser` (incoming, header 3073) under `messages/{outgoing,incoming,parser}/room/pet/` (new `incoming/room/pet/` and `parser/room/pet/` folders added, `outgoing/room/pet/` already existed). All header values cross-confirmed two ways: exact match against `vortex-client/src/.../HabboMessages.as`'s own registry, and the literal IDs named in its commit messages. Registered in `HabboMessages.ts` with `// Vortex-custom (not in official AS3 dumps)` comments in place of the usual `sources/WIN63-...` trace, since the mandate's AS3-traceability format doesn't apply to a feature that was never in the official client.
- 🟡 **`HabboCatalog` never registered `ScrSendUserInfoEvent` (message id 1114)** — surfaced as `SocketConnection`'s "No registered handler for incoming message id 1114" debug log on every login/club-status push. The event class/parser (`ScrSendUserInfoEvent`/`ScrSendUserInfoMessageParser`) and its `HabboMessages.ts` id mapping already existed and parsed correctly; only the `addMessageEvent()` subscription + handler (`onSubscriptionInfo`) were missing from `HabboCatalog.initComponent()`, so club/VIP subscription state (`Purse.clubDays`/`clubPeriods`/`isVIP`/`pastClubDays`/`pastVipDays`/`isExpiring`/`minutesUntilExpiration`/`minutesSinceLastModified`) was silently never applied and the purse UI's club badge never updated. Ported `onSubscriptionInfo()` for real, including the `ExternalInterface.call("FlashExternalInterface.subscriptionUpdated", ...)` web-bridge call, now routed through `HabboWebTools` (new `subscriptionUpdated()` static method + `IHabboWebToolsEvents` entry, matching that class's existing `FlashExternalInterface.*` bridge-method pattern). **TODO(AS3)**: the `responseType === RESPONSE_TYPE_PURCHASE` branch (`reset()` + reopen the page remembered via `rememberPageDuringVipPurchase()`) is not ported — `reset()` tears down/reloads the whole catalog (navigators/viewer/product data) after a club purchase, and its prerequisite `rememberPageDuringVipPurchase()` is still a no-op stub.
- ✅ **In-room group panel + the navigator's guild badge** (`habbo/groups/GroupRoomInfoCtrl.ts`, `habbo/navigator/GuildInfoCtrl.ts`, `GetGuildEditInfoMessageComposer` 874): two separate faults behind "no guild widget in the room, and the navigator's guild icon does nothing". `GuildInfoCtrl` set the guild name but never touched the badge — AS3 looks `guild_badge` up on the *outer* container (not on the `guild_info` window it just built; different windows) and pushes `groupBadgeCode`/`habboGroupId` into its `BadgeImageWidget`; that block was absent, and the click handler was commented out with a stale "composer unavailable" note. `GroupRoomInfoCtrl` was not ported at all — now whole, with `HabboGroupsManager` forwarding `GetGuestRoomResult` (3042, gated on `enterRoom` so navigator listing lookups can't retarget it), group details, deactivation and `CloseConnection`. **Caveat, not in the code**: both entry points are gated on `groupRoomInfo.enabled` (toolbar attach additionally on `groupRoomInfo.attach.enabled`), and neither key is defined anywhere in this setup — not in the shipped `common_configuration`, not by the emulator. `getBoolean()` answers false for a missing key, so the panel stays hidden until external variables supply them; hardcoding a default would diverge from AS3.
- ✅ **Group creation ported end-to-end** (`habbo/groups/GuildManagementWindowCtrl.ts` + `ColorGridCtrl.ts`, `GuildSettingsCtrl.ts`, `GroupCreatedWindowCtrl.ts`, `HcRequiredWindowCtrl.ts`, `badge/` ×5, and 19 new message classes): the request side already worked — `BuyGuildWidget` sent `GetGuildCreationInfo` (2989) — but **nothing on the client could receive the reply**. There was no incoming event, no parser and no wizard, so the button fired into silence. Now wired: events 973 `GuildCreationInfo` / 1288 `GuildEditInfo` / 1132 `GuildEditorData` / 2138 `GuildCreated` / 496 `GuildEditFailed`, composers 3398 `GetGuildEditorData` / 207 `CreateGuild` / 2009 `UpdateGuildIdentity` / 3882 `UpdateGuildBadge` / 3421 `UpdateGuildColors` / 3716 `UpdateGuildSettings` (headers from WIN63's own registry `_SafeCls_2046.as`, corroborated against `vortex-emulator`'s `Headers.cs`; both serializers verified field-for-field against the port's parsers). The badge editor composites in `OffscreenCanvas` — AS3's `ColorTransform` multiply becomes a `multiply` blend plus `destination-in` to restore the part's alpha. `HabboGroupsManager` also gained the room-id/HC/flat-created listeners the wizard reads (`RoomEntryInfo`, `ScrSendUserInfo`, `FlatCreated`). Deferred with `TODO(AS3)`: `GuildMembersWindowCtrl` (the members link null-checks, as AS3 does), `GroupRoomInfoCtrl.expectedGroupId`, and `onRoomLeave` (nothing to close until `DetailsWindowCtrl`/`GroupRoomInfoCtrl` land).
- ✅ **InfoStand user/bot identity display + extended user profile (click-to-view) ported end-to-end** (`habbo/ui/handler/InfoStandWidgetHandler.ts`, `habbo/ui/widget/infostand/InfoStandUserView.ts`/`InfoStandBotView.ts`, new `habbo/groups/ExtendedProfileWindowCtrl.ts`): was category-100 stub (no user/bot info panel at all, no click-to-profile). Real user/bot identity views ported (avatar, motto, online status, profile-name link) — the click-to-profile handler (`RWOPEM_OPEN_USER_PROFILE`) sends `GetExtendedProfileMessageComposer`, which was defined but never registered in `HabboMessages.ts` (silently dropped every send). Basic-scope `ExtendedProfileWindowCtrl` ported: header, badges (no glow/hover popup), add/block/unblock-as-friend with confirm dialogs, change-looks/change-badges links. Groups list (`GroupDetailsCtrl`, 306 AS3 lines), relationship status, and badge glow/popup explicitly deferred (`TODO(AS3)`).
  - Found and fixed a second, unrelated blocker while diagnosing "the profile window never opens": `HabboGroupsManager`'s `IID_HabboFriendList` dependency was required, but nothing in the DI graph provides that interface (the three other consumers all mark it optional) — the manager was permanently locked, so *none* of its message events, old or new, ever registered. No console error, no warning; the fix (`required: false`) also silently un-breaks pre-existing group functionality that was likely broken by this same gap.
  - Also fixed two window-system bugs surfaced by this work, both broader than this one feature: `WindowParser` resolved `${key}` localization tokens eagerly at parse time, before `TextController`'s setter could detect the `${key}` shape and register the live-update listener `registerParameter()`'s `%param%` substitution needs — froze any caption using both patterns together with literal `%placeholders%` forever. And `FormattedTextController` has no equivalent to Flash's native `TextField.htmlText` tag rendering — added a minimal `<b>`/`<i>`/`<u>`/`<br>` parser (`core/window/utils/HtmlFormatting.ts`) plus per-run bold/italic support in `WindowComposite`'s text renderer (previously only color/underline).
  - Also fixed a black-square avatar bug (`AvatarImageWidget.ts`): used `renderer.extract.canvas()`, a GPU-round-trip method for textures that went through an actual PixiJS render pass, on a canvas-backed `Texture.from({resource})` texture that never did — switched to a direct `texture.source.resource` read, matching `ProductGridItem`'s existing pattern. And an invented `widgetWindow.visible = true` shortcut that was masking the real AS3 "crikey" easter-egg `sticker_croco`/`avatar_image` visibility swap, which lives in `setMotto()`.
- ✅ **Clickable chat message links ported end-to-end** (`habbo/ui/widget/roomchat/RoomChatItem.ts`): was a documented `TODO(AS3)` — messages with links rendered as plain text. Found a deeper bug while porting it: `RoomChatHandler.ts` flattened the parser's `{url, displayText, isTrusted}` link objects down to bare display-text strings before they reached the chat bubble, so even a correct render would have had no URL to open. Fixed at the source, re-typed `IChatLink[]` through `RoomSessionChatEvent`/`RoomWidgetChatUpdateEvent`. Also added the missing rendering primitives: `ITextWindow`/`TextController` gained `getCharIndexAtPoint()`/`getTextFormat()`/`setTextFormat()` with per-range format "runs" (AS3's `setTextFormat()` is a no-op without a valid range - preserved as-is), and `WindowComposite.compositeText()` gained a run-aware single-line path so one text window can mix a base style with a colored/underlined link substring. Cross-referencing `win63_version` against `win63_2023_version` also confirmed `if(var_1993 && false)` in the latter's click handler is decompiler corruption (`&& false` constant-folded); the real condition is `links.length > 0`.
- ✅ **`ModalDialog.refresh()` ported for real** (`habbo/window/utils/ModalDialog.ts`): was `desktop.visible = false` (room disappears behind a dialog instead of dimming). AS3 captures/darkens the desktop via `BitmapData.draw(stage)`; this port's equivalent (`WindowComposite`, Canvas2D, lives in `vortex-engine` — not PixiJS/client) gained `renderWindowToCanvas()`/`darken()` primitives, and `ModalDialog.refresh()` now snapshots the real desktop before hiding it (this compositor skips invisible desktops, unlike Flash's `draw()`), darkens it, and layers in each stacked dialog's own frozen appearance — full multi-dialog-stacking parity, not just single-dialog dimming.
- ✅ **Landing view (hotel view) fully ported** (`habbo/friendbar/landingview/`, ~76 AS3 files → 76 TS files): was a shell (empty background window, toolbar state switching only, zero widgets rendered) — now the complete AS3 subsystem.
  - **Controller (`HabboLandingView.ts`)**: added the missing `catalog`/`questEngine`/`habboHelp`/`avatarEditor`/`gameManager`/`tracking` DI dependencies and getters, `goToRoom()`/`requestBadge()`/`communityGoalVote()`/`getProductData()`, `hotel_view_welcome_window` cleanup, new-identity dynamic-slot override, `onExpiredLinkClick`/`onNavigatorSettings` listeners, and an `initialization_error` fallback window in `tryInitialize()`. **Onboarding flow (`OnBoardingHcFlow`) intentionally excluded** — confirmed by the user to be an unrelated login/account-creation system, not part of hotel view; the landing view activates unconditionally rather than gating on `suggestedLoginActions`.
  - **Layout engine**: `WidgetContainer` (placeholder/dynamic-slot binding), `DynamicLayoutManager` (6-slot grid, ported the slot-balancing math verbatim including an apparent off-by-one in the AS3 that only balances slots 0-4, not 5 — not "fixed"), `CommonWidgetSettings`, `MovingBackgroundObjects` + 4 motion classes (`Linear`/`Spiral`/`RandomWalk`/`StaticAnimated`) + `PathResetEvent`, and `WidgetContainerLayout` completed end-to-end (`registerFixedWidgets`/`registerDynamicWidgets`, dynamic-vs-custom resize paths, background-image timing codes, navigator auto-open, scheduling composer).
  - **All 22 widgets** ported to `widget/`, plus the composable `GenericWidget` + its `ElementHandlerFactory` and ~24 content-element handler classes (button/text/timer/image/badge/meter variants), de-obfuscated by content-signature cross-reference against `win63_version` (directory position between the two trees was found to be unreliable/misaligned — don't rely on it for future de-obfuscation in this module).
  - **Wire protocol**: promo articles (was already ported, just needed a consuming widget), community-goal voting (`CommunityGoalVoteMessageComposer`/`CommunityVoteReceivedEvent`, absent from the primary `WIN63-202607011411-782849652` tree — registered using `win63_version`'s real header ids 1104/551, matching this project's existing precedent of reusing win63_version ids elsewhere, e.g. `GetCommunityGoalHallOfFameMessageComposer=1034`), `ForwardToARandomPromotedRoomMessageComposer`/`RequestABadgeComposer` (ids 2120/2276, same rationale), `CurrentTimingCodeMessageEvent`+parser (competition module), plus a handful of other previously-missing composer/event pairs discovered mid-port.
  - **Interface-completeness fixes found along the way** (concrete controllers already had the members; the interfaces didn't expose them — same pattern as prior audit sessions): `IBitmapWrapperWindow`/`IStaticBitmapWrapperWindow` gained `stretchedX`/`stretchedY`; `ITextWindow` gained `etchingPosition`+`border`; `IHabboQuestEngine` gained `reenableRoomCompetitionWindow()`.
  - **Local-dev config**: `landing.view.dynamic.slot.{1-5}.widget` default assignments added to `common_configuration_txt.txt` (production would get these from server-pushed `external_variables`, which this project doesn't have a real backend for — same category as the pre-existing `landing.view.background_left/right.uri` defaults in that file).
  - **Process note**: this was built via 5 parallel forked agents (4 widget batches + GenericWidget/elements); 2 of the 5 initially reported false completion (generic status messages instead of actual deliverables, caught by checking the filesystem rather than trusting the report) and had to be resumed with a corrective prompt — worth remembering if delegating large parallel porting batches again.
- 🟡 **`SocketConnection.processReceivedData()` swallowed a fatal wire-format error, causing an infinite console-spam loop of "Invalid message length" with a different random value every frame**: `WireFormatter.splitMessages()` throws on an out-of-range length (faithful to AS3 `class_2705.as::splitMessages()`, which has the same unconditional throw with no `encryption.reset()`/buffer compaction on that path). AS3's caller (`class_1752.as::processReceivedData()`) catches this, notifies `messageParseError()`, and **re-throws** - i.e. treats it as fatal. Our port's catch block only logged the error and returned, leaving `receivedBuffer` uncompacted and the encryption keystream already advanced past the corrupted length prefix (the failed `decipher()` call consumed keystream with no matching `reset()`). Since something calls `processReceivedData()` every frame, this replayed the same stale bytes against an ever-advancing keystream forever, producing a new garbage `length` value each time. Fixed by clearing the buffer and closing the connection (via the existing `connectionError` callback + `close()`) instead of silently continuing.
- 🟡 **`RoomContentLoader.getClassName()` silently dropped indexed-color floor/wall furniture (e.g. `carpet_polar*73`) — icon rendered fine, the actual 3D furniture never loaded, with zero console output anywhere**: `populateFurniData()` correctly mirrors AS3, storing the raw `"baseClassName*colourIndex"` string in `_activeObjectTypes`/`_wallItemTypes` while keying `_floorItems`/`_wallItems` by the base name alone (matching `class_1835.as::populateFurniData()`'s `var_765`/`var_752` split exactly). AS3's public `getActiveObjectType()`/`getWallItemType()` both call `getObjectType()` to strip the `*colourIndex` suffix before returning - but `RoomContentLoader.getClassName()` (the helper `RoomEngine.getFurnitureClassName()` actually calls when creating a room object) read `_activeObjectTypes`/`_wallItemTypes` directly, bypassing that stripping. The raw suffixed className then flowed into `getObjectCategory()`, which checks `_floorItems.has(type)` - a lookup that can never match a suffixed key, so it fell through to `RoomObjectCategoryEnum.MINIMUM`. `getObjectContentURLs()` requires `OBJECT_CATEGORY_FURNITURE`/`OBJECT_CATEGORY_WALL` to build any download URL, so it silently returned `[]`, and `loadObjectContent()`'s `if(urls.length > 0)` gate silently returned `false` - no network request, no error event, no log, and the object stayed queued in `_pendingFurnitureViz` forever (so `createVisualizationForFurniture()` was never even called for it). Fixed by making `getClassName()` delegate to the already-correct `getActiveObjectType()`/`getWallItemType()` instead of reading the raw maps. Also added warn logs to two previously-silent branches in `createVisualizationForFurniture()` itself (`getGraphicAssetCollection()`/`getVisualizationXML()` returning null) while diagnosing this, in case a different className fails to resolve for a different reason in the future.
- 🟡 **Further bugs found live in the same debugging session (input bubbling, skin rendering, PixiJS renderer setup)**:
  - **`VortexApp`'s mouse-wheel handler never bubbled**: `WindowController.update()` returns `false` for `WHEEL` by default — only `ItemListController`/`ScrollBarController` override it to actually scroll. Our handler dispatched the `WHEEL` event straight at `findWindowAtPoint()`'s result (the deepest/leaf window under the cursor — inside a scrollable box, usually a list item/icon/label, not the scrollable container itself) and stopped, so scrolling only worked on the rare occasion the cursor happened to be directly over the scrollable window itself. Fixed by walking up the `parent` chain (mirroring `MouseEventProcessor.passMouseEvent()`'s bubbling for other mouse events) re-dispatching at each ancestor until one actually reports `handled`.
  - **PixiJS `multiView` not enabled**: several widgets read pixels back off-screen via `renderer.extract.canvas()` (`AvatarImageWidget` avatar/badge previews, `RoomEngine.pixiTextureToCanvas()` room icons, `RoomRenderingCanvas.takeScreenShot()`) — each of those is, from WebGL's point of view, "another canvas" sharing the app's GL context. Without `multiView`, PixiJS's `GlContextSystem.ensureCanvasSize()` warns on every one of those extractions ("multiView is disabled, but targetCanvas is not the main canvas") because it only expects the single view canvas. Set `multiView: true` on the `Application.init()` config in `Vortex.ts` — PixiJS's documented flag for exactly this one-context/several-canvases case.
  - **`BitmapSkinParser`/`HabboWindowManager` renderer cache key too coarse, and descriptor `states` ignored**: AS3's `parseSkinDescription()` prefers the caller-supplied `states` (the element descriptor's own `<states>`) and only falls back to the skin XML's own `<states>` when that list is empty — our `BitmapSkinParser.parse()` never accepted a states override at all, which silently broke skins like `habbo_skin_icon_set` whose own top-level `states` is empty (every icon style's state→layout/template mapping instead lives entirely on its element descriptor). Compounding this, `HabboWindowManager`'s renderer cache was keyed by `layout` filter alone, but several descriptors share the same `layout` (e.g. `icon16` reused across arrow styles 0-9) while pointing at different `states`/`template` — so the first-parsed descriptor's renderer silently got reused for every other descriptor sharing that layout name. Fixed by adding an optional `descriptorStates` parameter to `parse()` (AS3-priority: descriptor states first, XML states as fallback) and folding the descriptor's own states into the cache key (`` `${layoutFilter}::${JSON.stringify(descriptor.states ?? [])}` ``).
  - **`BitmapSkinRenderer.draw()` stretched pieces that should never scale**: AS3 only resizes a drawn piece when its scale mode actually calls for it (`STRETCH`/`TILED` on either axis); `FIXED`/`MOVE`/`CENTER` always blit the piece at its own native size via `BitmapData.copyPixels()`, never stretched to fill the layout region's authored box, even when that box is a different size than the source crop (e.g. a small icon glyph centered in a bigger padded box). Our port instead sized the draw off `destW === srcW`/`destH === srcH`, which stretched every `FIXED`-mode entity whose region size didn't happen to match its source crop. Fixed by branching on the actual scale mode (`tiled` / `stretched` / neither) instead of comparing dimensions, matching AS3's `_loc16_`/`_loc9_` logic in `BitmapSkinRenderer.as::draw()`.
- 🟡 **Catalog icon/preview rendering bugs found and fixed live (dev-environment debugging session)**: three separate, previously-invisible bugs, each masking the next.
  - **`BitmapFileLoader.dispose()` premature `Assets.unload()`**: `AssetLibrary.handleAssetLoadEvent()` disposes the loader as routine cleanup right after handing its loaded `Texture` off to a new `BitmapDataAsset` (which caches it under an asset name for reuse). `dispose()` was calling `Assets.unload(url)`, telling PixiJS's `Assets` cache to release the GPU resource almost immediately — so a furniture/product icon rendered once (before/during the async unload) but broke on every later reuse of that cached asset (icon disappears on a second click; a catalog page loading many icons at once hit the race on nearly all of them). Fixed by dropping the local `_texture` reference only; the `BitmapDataAsset` now owns the texture's lifecycle from there (its own `dispose()` already calls `texture.destroy(true)`).
  - **`RoomEngine`/`AvatarImageWidget` texture→canvas conversion fragility**: both read `texture.source.resource` directly and `drawImage()`'d it — but PixiJS doesn't guarantee a CPU-side resource stays attached to a `TextureSource` once uploaded to the GPU (`resource` can legitimately be `undefined` for a fully valid on-screen texture). Switched both to `renderer.extract.canvas(texture)`, PixiJS's own supported readback API.
  - **`ProductGridItem.setIconImage()` use-after-close**: AS3 (`ProductGridItem.as::setIconImage()`) copies the source `BitmapData`'s pixels into a buffer the target *already owns* (`copyPixels()`), so disposing the source parameter afterward never touches what's on screen. Our port instead did `target.bitmap = image` (a reference assignment, not a copy) and then unconditionally called `image.close()` on that *same* object when `disposeSource` was true — destroying the bitmap the target had just been handed. This was the actual root cause behind "icon shows once then disappears" surviving even after the `BitmapFileLoader` fix above. Fixed: only close the source when it was *not* handed to a live target.
  - **`ProductViewCatalogWidget`'s `product_image_widget` never hidden**: AS3 finds this child (`var_1030`), unconditionally hides it in `init()` and again before every preview render, only re-showing it for the one product type its own preview switch doesn't otherwise handle (`ProductDisplayWrapper.isSupported()` — in practice only `chat_style`, since `"r"` has its own earlier case in the same switch and is therefore unreachable there despite `isSupported()` accepting it too). Our port never looked this node up at all, so its `placeholder_image` (visible by default per `ProductImageWidget`'s constructor) stayed on screen permanently, overlapping the room-canvas furniture/wall-item preview. Fixed by porting the lookup/hide-on-init/hide-before-render steps, and also implemented the previously-stubbed `ProductDisplayWrapper` (`habbo/catalog/viewer/ProductDisplayWrapper.ts` — a small `IProductDisplayInfo` adapter over `IProduct`, per the primary `WIN63-202607011411-782849652` source which adds `"r"` support and `botFigureString`/`extraData` over the secondary source's chat-style-only version) to wire up that default case for real.
- 🟡 **`LocalizationCatalogWidget.retrieveCatalogImage()` fully ported** (network fallback for a catalog page's localized images not already in the local asset library - was just logging and skipping): now calls the real `IAssetLibrary.loadAssetFromFile()` (already used elsewhere, e.g. `AvatarAssetDownloadLibrary`), builds the URL from `image.library.catalogue.url` or `image.library.url + "Top_Story_Images/"` depending on whether the target element carries a `TOP_STORY` tag (matching AS3 exactly), and applies the image via the existing `setElementImage()` once loaded. One AS3-fidelity note: AS3 listens via `addEventListener("AssetLoaderEventComplete", ...)`, but our `AssetLoaderStruct.dispatchEvent()` always emits under the generic `'event'` key regardless of the event's own `.type` - so a literal translation would silently never fire. Used the already-established, actually-working pattern instead (`loader.events.on('event', event => { if(event.type === AssetLoaderEventType.COMPLETE) ... })`, same as `AvatarAssetDownloadLibrary`/`EffectAssetDownloadLibrary`).
- 🟡 **`UniqueLimitedItemWidget` ported and registered** (the "X left of Y" LTD supply overlay on the product view page): was entirely missing - not implemented, not registered in `CatalogPage.createWidget()`'s switch (`'limitedItemWidget'` case). Its layout container (`limitedItemWidget` -> `unique_item_overlay_container`, `widget_type="limited_item_overlay_supply"`) has no default-hidden attribute in the compiled layout - AS3's widget class is the only thing that ever calls `window.visible = false`, so with the widget never constructed it showed constantly regardless of the selected product, which is what surfaced this gap. Full port: shows only for `pricing_model_single` + `isUniqueLimitedItem` offers, sets `supplyLeft`/`seriesSize` on the existing `LimitedItemSupplyLeftOverlayWidget`, and refreshes every 20s via a new real `HabboCatalog.sendGetProductOffer()` (new `GetProductOfferComposer`, message ID `1692` - cross-checked against both `win63_version` (id `3691`, a different build's numbering) and the primary `WIN63-202607011411-782849652` manifest (`1692`), which wins per the project's source-priority rule).
  - **TODO(AS3), clearly scoped**: the *response* handler (`ProductOfferEvent`/`onProductOffer`, ~40 AS3 lines reconstructing a full `Offer`/`Product` from the reply) isn't ported - it's a materially separate feature (new parser + full offer/product reconstruction shape + `CatalogPage.updateLimitedItemsLeft()` + `ProductOfferUpdatedEvent` dispatch). The periodic request now goes out correctly over the wire, but nothing currently handles the reply, so the supply count won't live-update if someone else buys the last one while a page is open - it's still correct at selection time from the initial catalog-page load data.
- 🟡 **`ProductViewCatalogWidget` preview interaction controls ported** (rotate_avatar_left/right, toggle_preview_magic, toggle_preview_zoom - previously present in every layout but entirely unwired): full `_previewMode` state machine (none/avatar/floor-furniture/wall-item), avatar direction rotation, avatar pose cycling ("preview magic" - stand/walk/dance/sit/lay/wave, skipping invalid poses per direction like AS3's diagonal-sit and non-cardinal-lay checks), and the 2-stage zoom animation (`applyRoomCanvasZoom()`/`onPreviewZoomAnimationFrame()`), all matching `ProductViewCatalogWidget.as` method-for-method. Also added the "s"-product category-23 sub-case (avatar wearing a rideable/wearable floor item, via `sessionDataManager.getFloorItemData()` + `avatarRenderManager.getFigureStringWithFigureIds()`), previously entirely missing (always fell into the plain-furniture path).
  - **New real `RoomPreviewer` API**: `updateAvatarDirectionAndLocation()` - previously missing entirely, now calls the real `IRoomEngine.updateRoomObjectUser()` (found under that name rather than AS3's generic `updateObjectUser()` - this engine has specific `updateRoomObjectUser*` methods per concern instead of one generic entry point). `addAvatarIntoRoom()`'s `effect` param now defaults to `0` (was required, blocking the 1-arg AS3 call shape used by the category-23 case).
  - **New `RoomPreviewer` stubs, honestly non-functional**: `canRotatePreviewFurniture()`/`rotatePreviewFurniture()`/`canRotatePreviewWallItem()`/`rotatePreviewWallItem()` always report "not rotatable" - AS3's versions need the previewed object's allowed-direction set, which the ported room engine doesn't expose yet. Floor-furniture/wall-item preview modes wire up correctly (rotate buttons appear) but stay disabled; avatar-mode rotation doesn't depend on this gap and works fully.
  - **TS deviation, documented at each site**: AS3 drives `setFloorFurnitureRotationAvailabilityMonitorEnabled()` and the zoom animation via a DisplayObject's own `enterFrame` event; ported via the window manager's shared update-receiver loop instead (same mechanism `HintManager` already uses), since there's no per-DisplayObject frame event here.
- 🟡 **`habbo/window/theme` audited (`Theme.ts`, `ThemeManager.ts`)**: found and fixed a missing theme plus 13 missing default properties.
  - **Missing "Misc" theme**: AS3 registers a real (`isReal=true`) `Theme.MISC` covering styles 10000-10007 (Ubuntu's cloned property set); our port had neither the `Theme.MISC` constant nor the theme registration, so `getThemeAndIntent()`/`getPropertyDefaults()` for that style range silently fell back to `"None"` instead. Added both.
  - **13 missing `PropertyMap` defaults**: `fill_mode`, `flip_x`, `flip_y`, `color1`, `color2`, `mode` (gradient), `radius`, `shape`, `sides`, `stroke_color`, `stroke_hsv_shade`, `stroke_thickness`, `tint` were never registered as theme defaults. Checked real runtime impact before fixing: `GradientController`/`ShapeController`/`StrokeController` carry their own internal defaults independent of the theme map (so no visual regression), and `BitmapFillController`'s hardcoded JS fallbacks (`'stretch'`/`false`) happened to already match AS3's real defaults by coincidence — so this was a silent metadata gap (dev-tool property-editor validation info), not a rendering bug, but still a genuine AS3-fidelity gap now closed.
  - Confirmed AS3 order/values otherwise match exactly; the one AS3 quirk *not* replicated (the base property map object being mutated by reference after being handed to the `None`/`Icon`/`Legacy border` theme constructors, changing their stored `etching_color`/`illumina_border:border_style` defaults post-hoc) is intentionally left as-is — those 3 themes are never `isReal`, so `getPropertyDefaults()` never reads their stored map at all; replicating the reference-aliasing would be invisible either way.
- 🟡 **`habbo/window` root-file audit finished (HintManager/HintTarget, ResourceManager, ElementRegistry, HabboWindowManager, and the 4 root interfaces)**: found and fixed 2 real bugs, confirmed the rest faithful.
  - **`HabboWindowManager.buildFromXML()` bug**: AS3 wires every frame built this way to `helpButtonAction = openHelpPage` (`if(result is FrameController) result.helpButtonAction = ...`); our port never did this, so the "?" help button on any frame built via `buildFromXML()` was permanently inert. Fixed. (`openHelpPage()` itself is still a `TODO(AS3)` stub pending `HabboPagesViewer`, so nothing visibly changes yet, but the wiring is now correct for when it lands.)
  - **`ResourceManager.retrieveAsset()` bug**: on a failed URL fetch, AS3 falls back to delivering the `"missing_image_icon"` placeholder to queued receivers; our port only cleared the loading flag and left receivers queued forever with nothing ever shown. Fixed — delivers the cached `missing_image_icon` bitmap on fetch failure, same as AS3.
  - **`HintManager.ts`/`HintTarget.ts`**: fully faithful; only benign differences (zero-rect vs `null` passed to `context.create()`, self-corrected by `fitSizeToContents`; a defensive `Math.max(1, distance)` guard against a division-by-zero AS3 itself doesn't guard).
  - **Dead parallel "declarative window" system removed** (user-confirmed, per the project's own "AS3 is the source of truth, never invent" rule): `HabboWindowManager`'s `openWindow()`/`closeWindow()`/`getWindow()`/`getWindows()`/`registerLayout()`/`getLayout()`/`getMaxZOrder()`, the `_windows`/`_layouts`/`_nextId`/`_windowEvents` fields, `WindowManagerEvents`, and the whole `IWindowInstance.ts` + `WindowLayoutParser.ts` files had zero AS3 equivalent and zero real callers anywhere in either package (confirmed by grep before deleting) — an early prototype superseded by the real `WindowContext`/`WindowParser`/`create()`/`buildFromXML()`/`buildWidgetLayout()` path and never cleaned up. `IWindowLayout.ts`'s data-shape interfaces were kept: `vortex-client/src/App.ts` genuinely uses them for its own JSON->XML layout serialization, unrelated to the deleted method cluster.
    - **Bug uncovered by the removal**: deleting `getLayout()` broke `SimpleAlertDialog.ts`, which was calling `windowManager.getLayout('simple_alert')` and checking `typeof result === 'string'` — but `getLayout()` always returned an `IWindowLayout` *object* (or `null`), never a string, and `'simple_alert'` was never a name anything registered under `registerLayout()` in the first place. So `SimpleAlertDialog` was **already silently broken before this session** — every call fell into its own `log.warn('Missing simple_alert XML layout'); return;` early-out and showed nothing. Fixed by adding a public `IHabboWindowManager.requireWidgetLayout(name, purpose)` (promoted from a private helper already used by `alert()`/`confirm()`/etc, matching AS3's inline `assets.getAssetByName("simple_alert_xml")` pattern) and pointing `SimpleAlertDialog` at it with the correct asset name.
  - **Minor fixes**: `IHabboWindowManager.createWidget()` was mislabeled `// TS-only`, it's a real AS3 member (`HabboWindowManagerComponent.as::createWidget()`) — trace comment corrected. `ElementRegistry.ts` doc comment updated to clarify it's a TS-only JSON index (not a `SkinContainer` port) that feeds the real, AS3-faithful `SkinContainer`.
- 🟡 **`HabboWidgetFactory` completed: `ProductIconWidget`/`ProductImageWidget` ported, plus a `createWidget()` silent-failure fix** (`habbo/window` root-file audit): the widget registry (`WidgetClasses.as` in AS3) has 26 entries; ours only had 24 — missing `product_icon`/`product_image`, the catalog/inventory product-preview widgets. Both are now ported (`ProductIconWidget.ts` small icon preview, `ProductImageWidget.ts` larger angled preview) and registered. Also found the factory's trace comment pointed at a nonexistent file (`class_3474.as` — a class-number collision with an unrelated AS3 module) and `createWidget()` silently returned `null` for an unknown widget type where AS3 throws `"Unknown widget type ..."`; now logs a warning instead of throwing (matches this session's other "loud instead of silent" fixes, without the blast radius of a hard throw).
  - **New supporting API**: `IHabboWindowManager`/`HabboWindowManager` gained `catalog`/`freeFlowChat`/`configuration` accessors (all real AS3 members, wired via the existing DI `ComponentDependency` pattern for `catalog`/`freeFlowChat`, direct field exposure for `configuration` which was already collected but never exposed). `IIconWindow` was missing `fitToSize()` (the *only* real AS3 member — its previous single field, `imageUrl`, didn't exist in AS3 and had zero callers, so it was replaced rather than kept alongside). `IBitmapWrapperWindow`/`IStaticBitmapWrapperWindow` were both missing `pivotPoint` (present on the concrete `BitmapDataController` they each inherit from/delegate to; AS3's real interface, obfuscated as `_SafeCls_1989`, exposes it plus zoom/wrap/flip/etch/greyscale properties still not surfaced on either interface). `BadgeImageWidget`/`PetImageWidget.refresh()` made `public` (were `private`; `ProductIconWidget`/`ProductImageWidget` call them externally after a blend change, matching AS3) and `BadgeImageWidget` gained a real `pivotPoint` passthrough.
  - **Deferred within `ProductImageWidget`** (`TODO(AS3)`, each naming the real gap): wall/floor-item image preview needs `IRoomEngine.getWallItemImage()`/`getFurnitureImage()` (angled 3D renders — AS3's `getFurnitureIcon()`/`getWallItemIcon()`, which we do have, turn out to just be `getFurnitureImage(...,scale=1,...)` convenience wrappers around the same missing general method); pixel-effect-on-avatar preview needs an unported `EffectPreviewer` class; the chat-style/easter-egg preview path needs `IHabboFreeFlowChat.createPreviewBitmap()` (stubbed to always return `null` in a new small `ProductCategoryMapping.createChatItemPreview()` helper — the easter-egg repeat-count tracking itself is fully real, it just can't render the reveal image yet, exactly like AS3 when its own preview creation fails). `ProductIconWidget`'s habbicon case is similarly stubbed (needs `HabbiconAssetManager`, unported).
- 🟡 **`HabboCatalogUtils` (partial) + `SimplePriceCatalogWidget` ported; `CatalogPage` now creates real widgets** (`habbo/catalog` phase 4, continued): per user direction, ported the price-display pipeline of `HabboCatalogUtils` (`catalog.utils`) for real — `createWindow()`, `showPriceInContainer()`/`renderPriceInContainer()`/`renderPriceItem()`/`createPriceContainer()`, `getPriceArray()`/`getPriceMap()`, `showPriceOnProduct()` (the method both `SimplePriceCatalogWidget` and, later, `ProductViewCatalogWidget` need), `calculateBundlePrice()`, `setCatalogItemImage()`/`displayProductIcon()`/`imageReady()`/`imageFailed()`. `SimplePriceCatalogWidget` itself is fully real. `CatalogPage.createWidget()`/`createWidgetsRecursion()`/`initializeWidgets()`/`removeWidgets()` (including the init-failure cascading-removal logic) are now genuinely wired for `itemGridWidget`/`simplePriceWidget` — a page with those two layout windows renders and behaves for real; `CatalogPage.selectOffer()` now really calls into `ItemGridCatalogWidget.select()`.
  - **win63 vs. clean-reference version split**: `HabboCatalogUtils.as` (631 lines, win63) is one of the most heavily decompiler-corrupted files found this session (`getPriceArray`/`getPriceMap` compute the right value into a local, then push a hardcoded `{amount: 0, ...}` instead of using it — same "compute correctly, return/use the wrong thing" pattern as this session's other corruption finds). Used `vortex-client`'s clean, uncorrupted `HabboCatalogUtils.as` as the primary structural reference for the ported subset, cross-checked against win63 for real newer-build additions (silver/emerald currency lines in `getPriceArray`, which the older clean reference doesn't have).
  - **New real `HabboCatalog`/`IHabboCatalog` wiring**: `utils` getter (+ `_utils` field, constructed eagerly like AS3's own `_utils`), `getSeasonalCurrencyActivityPointType()`, `displayProductIcon()` now delegates to `utils.displayProductIcon()` for real (was an empty stub).
  - **Interface-completeness fix**: `IBitmapWrapperWindow` was missing `bitmapAssetName` (AS3's `class_2251.bitmapAssetName` — the concrete `BitmapWrapperController` already implements it, same class of gap as `ITextWindow.etchingColor`/`.focus()`/`.unfocus()` found earlier this phase).
  - **Deferred within `HabboCatalogUtils`** (`TODO(AS3)`, not needed by `SimplePriceCatalogWidget`): `showVipBenefits()`/`openLink()` (needs `VipBenefitsWindow`, not ported), `showBadgeOnProduct()`/`showAssetImageAsBadgeOnProduct()`/`hideBadgeFromProduct()` (needs `IBadgeImageWidget`, not ported — these are what `ProductViewCatalogWidget` needs next), bundle-discount-ruleset math (`getDiscountItemsCount()` + helpers, needs `BundleDiscountRuleset` message data, itself unported), `resolveBundleDiscountFlatPriceSteps()`, and the 3 `HabboTracking` one-shot analytics methods. `showPriceOnProduct()`'s seasonal-currency price colour branch (`getSeasonalCurrencyPriceColor()`, needs `ColorConverter.hexToUint()` + seeded config) falls back to the default price colour instead.
  - Next: `ProductViewCatalogWidget`/`SingleViewCatalogWidget` (needs the deferred badge/chat-style display methods above, plus `RoomPreviewer` for the in-room product preview — still the most heavily corrupted widget file found) or `PurchaseCatalogWidget` (independent of `HabboCatalogUtils`, but needs `PurchaseConfirmationDialog` to actually complete a purchase) are the two remaining candidates to reach a full "browse → select → buy" flow.
- 🟡 **First catalog widget ported: `ItemGridCatalogWidget`** (`habbo/catalog` phase 4, started): the offer-grid engine — grid item creation (single/multi-price layouts), batched/timer-based icon loading (25ms tick, 3 offers/tick, matching AS3's `Timer`), selection (`select()`/`SelectProductEvent`/`SetExtraPurchaseParameterEvent`), and the `default_3x3_color_grouping` swatch-grouping logic (`onColourIndex`/`getCurrentItemColors`) are all real. Added `ICatalogWidget`/`CatalogWidget` (the shared widget base — `page`/`events`/`window`, `attachWidgetView()`), `IDragAndDropDoneReceiver`, and 4 small widget-event classes (`SelectProductEvent`, `SetExtraPurchaseParameterEvent`, `CatalogWidgetColoursEvent`, `CatalogWidgetColourIndexEvent`).
  - **Deferred within this widget** (`TODO(AS3)`, each naming the real gap): guild-recolor furniture preview (`onGuildSelected`) — needs `StringArrayStuffData.setArray()`, which this port's `StringArrayStuffData` doesn't have (it only builds from a room-object model/message wrapper, not an ad-hoc string array); drag-to-room placement (`startDragAndDrop`) — needs `HabboCatalog.requestSelectedItemToMover()` + `CatalogObjectMover`, both still unported (same deferred in-room purchase flow noted in `Offer.ts`).
  - **Widget-layer scope finding, important for planning phase 4's remaining widgets**: `SimplePriceCatalogWidget` and `ProductViewCatalogWidget`/`SingleViewCatalogWidget` (the rest of the originally-planned minimal set) both funnel through `HabboCatalogUtils` (`catalog.utils`, 631 AS3 lines, not ported — price/badge/extra-icon display helpers used across most widgets) — and `ProductViewCatalogWidget` (683 AS3 lines) is additionally one of the most heavily decompiler-corrupted files found this session (dozens of `null.` substitutions) and depends on `RoomPreviewer` (product-in-room 3D preview, not ported). `PurchaseCatalogWidget` (327 lines) turned out to be independent of `HabboCatalogUtils`/`RoomPreviewer`, but its core action, `HabboCatalog.showPurchaseConfirmation()`, needs `PurchaseConfirmationDialog` (a full offer-confirmation/gift dialog, not ported) to do anything. **Net effect**: a real end-to-end "browse → select → buy" flow needs at least one of {`HabboCatalogUtils`, `PurchaseConfirmationDialog`} ported in addition to more widgets — this is now a user-facing scope decision, not just "port more widgets."
- ✅ **`CatalogPage`/`CatalogViewer` orchestration skeleton ported** (`habbo/catalog` phase 3b, completes phase 3): `CatalogViewer` (owns the currently-shown page + its container, page-switch/refresh logic, `roomEngine`/`setLeftPaneVisibility` pass-through) and `CatalogPage` (loads a page's layout window via `windowManager.buildFromXML()`, offer/localization/mode accessors, `selectOffer`/`replaceOffers`/`updateLimitedItemsLeft`) are both real and wired to each other. **What's intentionally not real yet**: `CatalogPage`'s `createWidget()` — a ~45-case switch instantiating one widget class per layout window name (`itemGridWidget`, `productViewWidget`, `purchaseWidget`, ...) — is a `TODO(AS3)` no-op, since none of `viewer/widgets/` (52 AS3 files) is ported. A loaded page therefore renders its background/layout window but has zero interactive content until widgets start landing. `CatalogViewer.showSearchResults()` is also a stub (needs the deferred `FurnitureOffer`).
  - **New real `HabboCatalog` wiring**: `roomEngine` getter + `IID_RoomEngine` DI dependency (real AS3 member, was missing), `setLeftPaneVisibility()` (real logic, but a no-op until Phase 5's `createMainWindow()` sets `_mainWindow` — same pattern as `setImageFromAsset`'s cache-hit-only path from phase 3a).
  - **Interface-completeness fix**: `ITextFieldWindow` was missing `focus()`/`unfocus()` (AS3's `class_1898`/`IFocusWindow` — the concrete `TextFieldController` already implements them) — added `extends IFocusWindow`.
  - Next phase (phase 4, not started): a minimal `viewer/widgets/` set (`ItemGridCatalogWidget`, `SimplePriceCatalogWidget`, `ProductViewCatalogWidget`/`SingleViewCatalogWidget`, `PurchaseCatalogWidget`) sufficient to browse a category and buy one offer end-to-end, then wiring `HabboCatalog.ts`'s still-stub `loadCatalogPage`/`purchaseOffer`/`purchaseProduct`/`openCatalog*`/`getCatalogNavigator` (phase 5) to the navigation+viewer+widget pieces built in phases 1-4.
- ✅ **Catalog product/offer data model ported** (`habbo/catalog` phase 3a — the "browsable/purchasable item" layer that `CatalogPage`/`CatalogViewer` will render; those two orchestrators are the next, not-yet-started slice): `IGridItem`/`IItemGrid`/`IPageLocalization`/`IProduct`/`IProductContainer`/`ICatalogPage`/`ICatalogViewer` interfaces, `PageLocalization`, `ProductGridItem` (base grid-item view + drag/select mouse handling), `Product` (single product icon rendering across all AS3 product types), `ProductContainer`/`SingleProductContainer`/`MultiProductContainer`/`BundleProductContainer` (pricing-model-specific grid item variants), `Offer`/`IPurchasableOffer` (the top-level purchasable unit). `FurniProductContainer`/`FurnitureOffer`/`GameTokensOffer`/`CatalogObjectMover`/`ProductDisplayWrapper` were read but deliberately deferred — they depend on `catalog.roomEngine`/`sendGetProductOffer`, which belong to the in-room "buy this placed item" flow, not the base catalog-browsing path `Offer`/`ProductContainer` cover.
  - **New real (non-stub) `HabboCatalog` wiring**: `avatarRenderManager`/`sessionDataManager` getters + DI dependencies (both real AS3 members, previously missing), `getProductData()`/`getFurnitureData()` now call through to `sessionDataManager` for real (previously always-null stubs).
  - **Two more decompiler-corruption fixes** (cross-checked against `vortex-client` first, per convention): `HabboCatalog.as::getFurnitureData()` computes the right value into a local but always `return null`s instead of returning it. `ProductContainer.as::set view()`'s ninja-effect-addon check decompiles as `while(true) { ...; if(null.productType...) ...}` (infinite loop reading a literal `null`) — reconstructed as a bounded 2-item loop over `products[i]`, matching the clean reference exactly. Also found (not corruption, a genuine missing method body): `Product.as::set view()`'s multi-count branch computes `multiContainer`/`multiCounter` locals but the win63 build's copy never uses them — reconstructed from `vortex-client`, which shows `multiContainer.visible = true` / `multiCounter.text = "x" + productCount`.
  - **Real icon rendering wired for furniture/wall-item/effect/subscription/avatar-render/badge product types** (matching AS3, per explicit user direction to favor real wiring over stubs where the backing systems exist). One unavoidable architecture divergence, called out inline: AS3's avatar/badge icon paths are synchronous (`BitmapData`); the PixiJS port's equivalents (`AvatarImage.getCroppedImage()` → `Texture`, `sessionDataManager.getBadgeImage()` → `HTMLImageElement`) need the browser's async `createImageBitmap()` with no synchronous alternative, so those two paths apply the icon via a promise continuation instead of a direct return — the call shape is otherwise unchanged.
  - **`TODO(AS3)` stubs added, each naming the specific missing dependency** (per project convention — compatible signatures exist, behavior does not yet): `HabboCatalog.isDraggable()` (needs room-session owner/guild/controller-level state and Builders-Club furniture-placement status — always returns `false`, i.e. drag-and-drop off, rather than guessing); `HabboCatalog.setImageFromAsset()`'s async cache-miss fallback (`retrievePreviewAsset()`); `Product.initIcon()`'s `chat_style` case (`catalog.freeFlowChat.chatStyleLibrary` doesn't exist at all) and its wall-item room-preview special case; the `BIRE_BADGE_IMAGE_READY` live-update event for badges requested before they're cached.
  - Naming: kept AS3's literal `UPPER_SNAKE_CASE` constant/field names throughout (`Offer.PRICING_MODEL_*`, `PageLocalization.LAYOUTS_TEXT_FIELDS`, etc.) per explicit user direction — this trips the repo's `camelCase` ESLint naming rule (pre-existing tension already present in `ClubOfferProductData.ts`/`FrontPageItem.ts`), left as-is intentionally.
- ✅ **Catalog navigation module ported** (`habbo/catalog` phase 2 — category tree + tab/list navigator UI; builds on phase 1's message layer): `ICatalogNavigator`/`ICatalogNode` interfaces, `CatalogNode` (invisible/non-interactive tree node), `CatalogNodeRenderable` (the real, window-rendering node — builds its list-item window from `navigator.getItemTemplate(depth).clone()`, wires `WME_CLICK`/`WME_OVER`/`WME_OUT`), `CatalogNavigator` (owns the category tree, the tabbed/non-tabbed top-level view, search/filter, and page-open routing), `TopViewSelector` (tab bar for the tabbed variant), `RequestedPage`, and `event/CatalogEvent` + `navigation/events/{CatalogPageOpenedEvent,CatalogFurniPurchaseEvent}`. Added `HabboCatalog.useNonTabbedCatalog()` (real, config-driven — `CatalogNavigator`'s constructor needs it) and `ITextWindow.etchingColor` (existing implementation gap: `TextController` already had it, the interface didn't expose it — `CatalogNodeRenderable`'s active/inactive tab-item styling needs it through the interface).
  - **Two more decompiler-corruption bugs found and fixed** (per convention, cross-checked against `vortex-client` first): `CatalogNavigator.as::getPathToNodeWithLayout()` reads/returns a literal `null` instead of the recursive call's result (`null.length`/`null.unshift` — guaranteed `TypeError` if that branch is ever hit); `openPageById()` reads `undefined[0]` instead of the just-computed offer-node list. Both reconstructed with the obvious intended local variable. Unlike phase 1's corruption (identical logic to the clean reference, just mangled), this method itself doesn't exist at all in `vortex-client`'s older build — `win63_version` added a genuine "deep hierarchy" path-to-node feature on top of it, confirmed via the `isDeepHierarchy`/`catalog.deep.hierarchy` config flag also present in `getItemTemplate()`/`CatalogNodeRenderable.createWindow()`'s depth-based icon indent math (which itself differs materially from `vortex-client` — win63 repositions the icon itself via `icon.x`, not just its visibility — a real newer-build behavior change, ported as-is, not corruption).
  - Next phase (not started): `viewer/` (`CatalogPage`/`CatalogViewer`/`Offer`/`Product` models + a minimal widget set to actually render a page and buy something), then `HabboCatalog.ts`'s real `loadCatalogPage`/`purchaseOffer`/`purchaseProduct`/`openCatalog*`/`getCatalogNavigator` (still stubs).
- ✅ **Catalog core message layer ported** (`habbo/catalog` phase 1 of a multi-phase port — navigation/viewer/purchase UI is still not started): `GetCatalogIndexComposer`/`CatalogIndexMessageEvent`+`Parser` (category tree, header 1606/2248), `GetCatalogPageComposer`/`CatalogPageMessageEvent`+`Parser` (page contents, header 2400/1405), `PurchaseFromCatalogComposer`/`PurchaseOKMessageEvent`+`Parser` (header 2697/2558), plus the failure paths `PurchaseErrorMessageEvent`(930)/`PurchaseNotAllowedMessageEvent`(1872)/`NotEnoughBalanceMessageEvent`(3883). Numeric headers came from the real emulator (`vortex-emulator/Turbo.Revisions/Revision20260112/Headers.cs`), not guessed. New data classes: `NodeData` (recursive category tree node), `CatalogLocalizationData`, `FrontPageItem`, `PurchaseOKMessageOfferData`. `CatalogPageMessageEventParser`'s `offers` reuses the already-ported `ClubOfferData`/`ClubOfferProductData` (confirmed via `win63_version`'s own imports — `CatalogPageMessageEventParser.as` and `PurchaseOKMessageEventParser.as` both import the *same* anonymous classes, `class_2138`/`class_2104`, that `ClubGiftInfoEventParser` already uses — this is one shared offer/product shape across catalog and club-gift, not a coincidence).
  - **Decompiler corruption found and fixed** (per project convention, cross-checked against the clean `vortex-client` reference before "fixing"): `class_2971.as` (`NodeData`), `class_2409.as` (`CatalogLocalizationData`), and `CatalogPageMessageEventParser.as` all have count-prefixed read loops written as `while(0 < _loc2_) { ...; _loc3_++; }` — the loop condition checks the original count and never terminates as literally decompiled (an infinite loop if the count is nonzero). The real loop is `while(_loc3_ < _loc2_)` (counter vs. count), confirmed identical in `vortex-client`'s clean equivalents (`NodeData.as`, `CatalogLocalizationData.as`, `CatalogPageMessageParser.as`). Ported as a normal bounded `for` loop.
  - Next phases (not started): `navigation/` (`CatalogNavigator`/`CatalogNode`), `viewer/` (`CatalogPage`/`CatalogViewer`/`Offer`/`Product` models — depends on the `ItemGridController`/`ItemListController` fixes below), a minimal widget set to render a page and buy an offer, then `HabboCatalog.ts`'s real (currently stub) `loadCatalogPage`/`purchaseOffer`/`purchaseProduct`/`openCatalog*` methods.
- ✅ **Window property-application timing fixed at the architecture level** (root cause of a Club Center HC Payday/Gift section height-doubling bug, 208→416 on reopen): AS3 initializes instance fields with literal defaults *before* the constructor body runs (including before `super()`); TypeScript/JS (with `target: ES2022`) initializes a derived class's own field initializers *after* `super()` returns — never before, and no tsconfig flag changes this. `WindowContext.create()` now applies `properties`/`caption` uniformly *after* the window is fully constructed for every window class (previously attempted passing `properties` into the constructor to match AS3's literal parameter order, which crashed `DropMenuController`/`ItemGridController` — their own `set properties()` touches fields not yet initialized during `super()`). `ItemListController` now creates its internal `_container` *lazily* (`ensureContainer()`, called from `addListItem`/`addListItemAt`/`populate`, not the constructor), keeping it `null` during property application exactly as AS3's own `_container` field is during `super()` — this is what actually restores order-independence between the `scale_to_fit_items`/`resize_on_item_update` layout vars. Also fixed in `WindowParser.ts`: `itemgrid_vertical` children were being routed through `addListItemAt` instead of `addGridItemAt` (per `ItemGridIterator.as::setProperty()`), causing grid children to render as side-by-side list columns instead of stacked grid cells. See `[[project_as3_field_init_before_super]]` memory for full detail — do not "fix" this class of bug by reordering JSON layout vars or changing the TS compile target, both were tried and rejected.
- ✅ **`WIN63-202607011411-782849652` window-system diff ported (new AS3 source added to `sources/`)**: this new source tree is a heavily obfuscated later client build (flat `_SafeCls_N`/`_SafePkg_N` files for one unrelated module, but a normal `com/sulake/...` package tree for `core/window`/`habbo/window`). Diffed file-by-file against `win63_version` (filtering out anonymous `class_N`/`_SafeCls_N` renumbering noise, which is not a real change) and confirmed via the `Classes.as`/`WidgetClasses.as` type registries — previously-unused window-type slots 22/24/25/26 are now real, and `WidgetClasses` gained one entry — that exactly 10 `core/window` classes and 5 `habbo/window` items are genuinely new:
  - **New skin primitives** (`core/window/components` + matching `graphics/renderer`): `ShapeController`/`ShapeSkinRenderer` (rectangle/round_rectangle/ellipse/rhombus + inset stroke — AS3 rasterizes to `BitmapData` pixel-by-pixel; ported to native Canvas2D path fill/stroke instead), `GradientController`/`GradientSkinRenderer` (linear/radial 8-direction two-color gradient — AS3 always calls `beginGradientFill("radial", ...)` regardless of `mode`, which looks like a bug but is preserved as-is per project convention; reproduced via a clip + rotated/scaled `createRadialGradient`, matching Flash's `createGradientBox()` idiom), `StrokeController`/`StrokeSkinRenderer` (per-side top/right/bottom/left stroke bitmask + optional rounded corners), `BitmapFillController`/`BitmapFillSkinRenderer` (stretch/tile/center/cover/contain fill modes with tint/greyscale/spacing, tile mode via a cached repeating `CanvasPattern` cell). Registered as `WindowType.SHAPE_WRAPPER`(22, previously reserved/unused)/`GRADIENT`(24, new)/`STROKE`(25, new)/`BITMAP_FILL`(26, new) in `Classes.ts`, and as `'shape'`/`'gradient'`/`'stroke'`/`'bitmap_fill'` renderer types in `HabboWindowManager.createRendererForType()`.
  - **Fixed a pre-existing wrong tag-name guess**: the AS3 skin-catalog tag table (`core/window/utils/_SafeCls_2816.as`/old `class_3537.as`, both confirmed identical here) has never had a `"shape_wrapper"` entry — the real tag for type 22 has always been `"shape"`. The TS `WindowType.ts`/`WindowXmlAssetParser.ts`/`compile-window-skins.mjs`/`compile-window-layouts.mjs` tables all had an invented `'shape_wrapper'` key (confirmed unused anywhere in actual asset data); replaced with `'shape'` in all four, consistent with the numeric `WindowType.SHAPE_WRAPPER` constant name (which *is* real AS3, unlike the tag string).
  - **Other new utilities**: `graphics/renderer/HsvLayerColor` (HSV-shade color derivation, used by `ShapeController.strokeHsvShade`), `utils/SmoothScroller` (inertial/eased scroll animator, cubic-bezier with an inverse-delta duration ramp — ported the AS3 `Timer` to `setInterval`/`performance.now()`, matching `ItemListController`'s existing scroll-wheel timer convention), `utils/WindowUtils` (`disableSection()` — recursive tag-based section disabling; two AS3 anonymous-type branches — an interactive-control early-out and two container subtypes that force-blend themselves — could not be identified with confidence from the obfuscated source and are marked `TODO(AS3)` rather than guessed), `habbo/window/utils/AnimatedColor`/`AnimatedScalar` (color/scalar tweening helpers, the latter using constant-acceleration physics bounded by max velocity).
  - **`ChestItemGridOverlayWidget`** (new `habbo_window/widgets` entry, `WidgetClasses`'s only new registration: `"chest_overlay_grid"`): silver/gold/brown plaque + contents-count overlay for chest-type grid items, same shape as the existing `RarityItemGridOverlayWidget`/`LimitedItemGridOverlayWidget`. Its `color` setter is fully implemented (`IStaticBitmapWrapperWindow.assetUri` swap). Its `contentsCount` setter is `TODO(AS3)`-stubbed (state tracked, not rendered): AS3 composites `unique_item_number_glyph_*` bitmaps via a shared, still-unported helper (`habbo/window/utils/_SafeCls_4213.as`) that both `RarityItemGridOverlayWidget`/`LimitedItemGridOverlayWidget` *also* skip in the existing TS port — `IHabboWindowManager` doesn't currently expose raw asset lookup to widgets, so this matches the pre-existing sibling simplification rather than introducing a new one.
  - **Also added while porting `BitmapFillController`**: `BitmapDataController.flipX`/`flipY` — these already existed in AS3 (both old and new builds) but were missing from the TS port entirely; `BitmapFillSkinRenderer`'s stretch/tile/cover/contain math genuinely needs them.
  - No real skin/layout JSON data exercises these new types yet — `WIN63-202607011411-782849652` only ships AS3 source, not a full extracted `binaryDataXml` asset catalog, so nothing has been fabricated on the data side; the classes and registries are wired and ready for when real 2026-era skin/layout data is available.
- ✅ **ClubCenter avatar-preview pipeline fixed end-to-end, plus an `HTMLTextController` rich-text gap**:
  - `RoomPreviewerWidget.showPreview()` (the static-bitmap preview mode used by `ClubCenterView`'s membership-comparison avatar) only stored the given URL on an unused `previewImageUrl` getter — nothing ever displayed it. Ported the real AS3 `showPreview(param1:BitmapData)`: swaps out the live PixiJS room canvas (unregistering its per-frame `syncCanvasPositionBound` sync callback) and hands a pre-scaled (2x, matching AS3's `Bitmap.scaleX/scaleY = 2`) canvas to the window's `display_object_wrapper` slot instead.
  - `WindowComposite.ts` only knew how to punch a transparent `clearRect` hole for `display_object_wrapper` windows (so an external PixiJS layer could show through) — it had no path for a plain pre-rendered bitmap (`HTMLCanvasElement`/`ImageBitmap`/etc.) occupying that same slot, so `showPreview()`'s canvas would've been invisible even once wired up. Added `isDrawableImage()` to tell the two cases apart and `drawImage()` the bitmap straight into the composite when there's no separate PIXI layer to reveal.
  - `AvatarImage.ts::getCroppedImage()` rendered the full (mostly empty) offscreen canvas without cropping — AS3 tracks the union of each drawn body part's rect (`Rectangle.union()`) and crops to that bounding box at the end; ported the same via a new `unionRect()` helper.
  - `ClubCenterView.ts` read the (never-cropped) texture back as a `toDataURL()` string; switched to handing `RoomPreviewerWidget.showPreview()` a copied canvas directly (copied rather than the live texture, since `avatarImage.dispose()` runs immediately after and would invalidate it).
  - `HTMLTextController.ts`'s `set text()` was a bare passthrough (no localization-key detection, no HTML-to-plain-text reduction) — AS3's `htmlText` goes through Flash's real rich-text renderer, which this port's plain-string `TextController` can't do, so `<br>`/tags need reducing to `\n`/stripped before reaching `_text` or they'd render as literal characters. Added `convertHtmlToPlainText()` and a matching `set localization()` override.
  - `docs/CLIENT-SERVER-ARCHITECTURE.md` was rewritten from scratch: the previous draft assumed a Java/Netty "Arcturus"-style server, which doesn't match this project's actual server (Turbo Cloud — .NET/Orleans/SuperSocket/EF Core) or client (`vortex-client`, protocol `WIN63-202601121721-391685409`). Old protocol IDs/bug list discarded rather than patched; rewritten sourced directly from `vortex-emulator`/`vortex-client`.
- ✅ **Furni inventory preview panel: z-order and missing name/description fixed**:
  - `RoomPreviewerWidget`'s mini room canvas and the main room view's canvas both get parented directly onto the same shared PixiJS stage (see `RoomEngine.createRoomCanvas()`) — their relative stacking depends purely on PixiJS child-add order, not window z-order. Entering a room while the inventory preview was already open recreated the main room canvas *after* the preview's, pushing it on top and visually burying the preview wherever their screen rects overlapped. Fixed by re-asserting front-of-stage every frame in `syncCanvasPosition()` (which already runs continuously via the shared ticker).
  - `GroupItem.name`/`description` were computed once in `push()` and cached — including when empty. `FurnitureDataParser` injects the `roomItem.name.<id>`/`wallItem.name.<id>` localization keys these read from, but nothing guarantees that finishes before the first inventory items arrive and call `push()` (a race that doesn't exist in AS3, where furnidata + external texts load strictly before inventory contents). Once cached empty, name/description stayed blank forever. Now the getters retry the lookup lazily on read instead of trusting the one-time push() computation.
- ✅ **Furni inventory preview panel: fixed unconditionally-visible next/view buttons and rarity/LTD badges** — `FurniView.ts::updateActionView()` never set visibility on `viewItemButton`/`nextItemButton` (AS3: only shown for "external image" wall items — photo/moodlight frames with cyclable custom images, hidden for every other item) or on `unique_limited_item_overlay_widget`/`rarity_item_overlay_widget` (only shown for genuinely limited-edition/rare items, driven by `stuffData.uniqueSerialNumber`/`rarityLevel`). All four defaulted to the layout's base visibility (effectively always-on), so every regular furni item showed "Next"/"View" buttons plus an uninitialized rarity/LTD badge overlay (rendering as a placeholder "forbidden" icon). Ported the real AS3 conditions; the grid-thumbnail equivalent (`GroupItem.ts::updateItemImageVisual()`) already had this right, this was specific to the big preview panel.
- ✅ **Chat display fixes found via cross-reference with a clean AS3 source (`C:\Users\Ctuto\Desktop\Habbo\vortex-client`, a compilable non-decompiled project — much more reliable than `win63_version`/`win63_2023_version` for corrupted sections)**:
  - `RoomChatItem.ts` conflated `userType` and `userCategory` into a single field — `onItemMouseClick/Down/Over/Out` were passing the wrong value through, and the pet vertical-offset special-case (`userType===2 && petType===15`) always read the wrong field. Split into `_userType`/`_senderCategory` matching AS3.
  - `RoomChatWidget.resetArea()` was a no-op TODO (reasoned that AS3 gates it on a null history viewer, which we always are) — but AS3 always constructs a (possibly-disabled) history viewer, so this method is never actually skipped there; it's what shrinks the chat area back down after it grows. Implemented for real (dropping only the historyViewer-derived width/height terms, which are 0 without that widget).
  - Confirmed via the clean source that the from-scratch reconstruction of `selectItemsToMove()`/`moveItemsUp()` (both corrupted in every previously-available decompiled copy) was accurate.
  - Fixed a camera-sync bootstrap bug in `ChatWidgetHandler.updateWidgetPosition()`: AS3 deliberately initializes the reference scale to `geometry.scale - 10` (not the real value) so the scale-changed branch is guaranteed to fire once and bootstrap downstream listeners; the port used the real value, which would've suppressed that first sync.
- ✅ **Furniture move/rotate/pickup wired to the server** — `RoomEngine.modifyRoomObject()` only handled `OBJECT_PICKUP`/`OBJECT_EJECT`, and even those only removed the object from the local view without telling the server (so it wouldn't actually persist — the item would reappear on reload). `OBJECT_ROTATE_POSITIVE`/`NEGATIVE` fell through to an unimplemented no-op entirely. Added `MoveObjectMessageComposer`/`PickupObjectMessageComposer` (new, matching AS3's `RoomObjectEventHandler.as::modifyRoomObject()`) and wired rotate (cycles 90°, sends the new direction at the same tile) and pickup/eject (sends the pickup composer before the local dispose) through `container.connection`.
  - `TODO(AS3)`: rotation skips AS3's `furniture_allowed_directions` model-array validation (some furniture only rotates through a subset of the 8 compass directions) — always cycles the 4 cardinal directions here.
- ✅ **`OBJECT_MOVE` (furniture drag-to-relocate) implemented** — `RoomEngine.modifyRoomObject()`'s `OBJECT_MOVE` case previously logged "not implemented yet" and did nothing; the InfoStand furni view's "Move" button (`InfoStandFurniView.ts::onButtonClicked()` → `RWFAM_MOVE` → `InfoStandWidgetHandler.ts`) was already wired up to it, so clicking it was a silent no-op. AS3's real version is corrupted/`null.`-riddled in both `win63_version/habbo/room/class_1947.as` and `class_34.as`; ported from the clean `C:\Users\Ctuto\Desktop\Habbo\vortex-client\src\com\sulake\habbo\room\RoomObjectEventHandler.as`/`RoomEngine.as` instead (`modifyRoomObject()`, `handleObjectMove()`/`handleFurnitureMove()`, `handleRoomObjectMouseClick()`'s `OBJECT_MOVE` case, `setObjectAlphaMultiplier()`). New `RoomEngine.ts` state: `_pendingMove` (mirrors AS3's `SelectedRoomObjectData`) tracks the object plus its pre-move location/direction; starting a move sets `furniture_alpha_multiplier` to 0.5 on the model (already consumed by `FurnitureVisualization.ts`, no renderer changes needed) and cross-cancels any in-flight catalog placement (`_pendingPlacement`) and vice versa. Hovering a floor tile (`handleTileMouseEvent()`'s `ROE_MOUSE_MOVE`) snaps the semi-transparent object to that tile at that tile's own floor height (`event.tileZ` — the same value the tile cursor is positioned at); clicking a tile sends `MoveObjectMessageComposer(objectId, tileX, tileY, direction)` — reusing the same composer already used for rotate — and hands the object's final position back to the existing server-echo path (`RoomObjectMoveUpdateMessage`/`MovingObjectLogic`, already live for rotate) rather than trusting the local preview position.
  - Initial version kept the object at its *original* z while dragging instead of the hovered tile's height, so it visibly floated/sank on any room with steps (caught via live testing right after the first pass) — fixed by switching to `event.tileZ`.
  - `TODO(AS3)` scope cuts (matching the pre-existing wall-item gap in `initializeRoomObjectInsert()`): wall items (category 20) aren't supported — AS3's `handleWallItemMove()`/wall-location-string math isn't ported. Client-side `FurniStackingHeightMap`/`validateFurnitureLocation()` collision validation is skipped, and the hover preview ignores any furniture already stacked on the hovered tile (both would need the same stacking-height system — the server is authoritative and rejects illegal spots regardless). There's still no cancel/right-click binding for an in-progress move — that's shared with the unbuilt furniture-context-menu widget, same as before.
- ✅ **Two toolbar-adjacent bug fixes found via live testing**:
  - `BottomBackgroundBorder` (the toolbar's background panel) was fully implemented but never constructed anywhere (`grep` found zero call sites besides its own file/barrel export) — `HabboToolbar.as::initComponent()` constructs it alongside `BottomBarLeft`, but the TS port only ever created `BottomBarLeft`. Added construction (and disposal) in `HabboToolbar.ts`'s lazy `bottomBarLeft` accessor. This is why the toolbar's background never rendered, most visible once in a room (icons floating directly on the room canvas with no panel behind them).
